+

+    const out = io.stderrText() + io.stdoutText();
+    assert.match(out, /nothing to resume/i, 'the refusal states there is nothing to resume');
+    assert.match(out, /baton loop run/, 'the refusal points at `baton loop run` to start a fresh run');
+    assert.equal(io.__runner.calls.length, 0, 'a nothing-to-resume refusal spawns no children');
+    // The teeth: a wrong-directory resume must not initialize and start over.
+    assert.equal(io.fs.existsSync(`${DIR}/state.json`), false, 'refusing to resume never creates a state.json');
+    assert.ok(!io.fs.existsSync(`${DIR}/supervisor.lock`), 'the nothing-to-resume refusal leaves no supervisor.lock behind (I4)');
+  });
+

   225	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   226	// RED — `baton loop run` supervisor state machine (subtask loop-run). Extends
   227	// core/src/commands/loop.mjs with the `run` subcommand that COMPOSES the loop
   228	// modules (spec/state/children/failover) over the real command machinery.
   229	// Source: docs/plans/2026-07-18-goal-loop-worktree-pipeline.md §"baton loop
   230	// (Layer 2)", §"Smoke gate", §"Caps", §"Supervisor lifetime and recovery".
   231	//
   232	// INJECTION SEAM (pinned): the command runs children via
   233	//   io.superviseChild ?? superviseChild
   234	// so tests inject a scripted fake runner that returns the next queued result
   235	// and records the {command, args, env} it was invoked with. Everything else
   236	// drives the real modules over memfs.
   237	//
   238	// The `run` subcommand does not exist yet: `loop run` currently exits 2
   239	// ("unknown subcommand 'run'"). Reds assert TARGET behavior (exit codes, spawn
   240	// counts, persisted state, ESCALATION/SMOKE-REVIEW files) — distinguished from
   241	// today's generic exit 2. --detach is OUT of unit scope (deferred to e2e).
   242	//
   243	// PINNED EXIT CODES: done 0 · awaiting-smoke-approval 0 · escalated 3 ·
   244	// parked 4 · live-lock refusal 1 · usage error 2.
   245	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

   284	function ownedBundle(originPlatform = 'claude-code', status = 'open') {
   285	  return {
   286	    schema: 'baton/bundle@1',
   287	    bundleId: 'b_looprun00000000',
   288	    generation: 1,
   289	    createdAt: T0,
   290	    updatedAt: T0,
   291	    origin: { platform: originPlatform, model: 'm', sessionHint: 'loop-sup', unstable: false },
   292	    task: { goal: 'Ship the loop', constraints: [], acceptance: [] },
   293	    plan: { steps: [] },
   294	    decisions: [],
   295	    files: { touched: [] },
   296	    roles: { assignments: {} },
   297	    git: null,
   298	    handoff:
   299	      status === 'sealed'
   300	        ? { status: 'sealed', reason: 'seal', reasonClass: 'usage-limit', toPlatformHint: null, finalizedAt: T0, receive_log: [] }
   301	        : { status: 'open', reason: null, reasonClass: null, toPlatformHint: null, finalizedAt: null, receive_log: [] },
   302	    journalSeq: 0,
   303	    compaction: { droppedDecisions: 0, note: null },
   304	    dedupeRing: [],
   305	  };
   306	}
   307	
   308	/**
   309	 * Scripted fake child runner honoring the injection seam. Each call shifts the
   310	 * next result off the queue, writes its logContent to opts.logPath (so the
   311	 * command reads the transcript from the child's LOG file), and records the
   312	 * spawn spec.
   313	 */
   314	function fakeRunner(io, script) {
   315	  const queue = [...script];
   316	  const calls = /** @type {any[]} */ ([]);
   317	  const fn = (/** @type {any} */ spec, /** @type {any} */ opts = {}) => {
   318	    calls.push({ spec, opts, args: spec?.args ?? [], command: spec?.command, env: spec?.env });
   319	    const r = queue.shift() ?? { timedOut: false, exitCode: 0, verdict: 'APPROVED', findings: '' };
   320	    // No fallback: the command MUST pass a real logPath (asserted below).
   321	    const logPath = opts.logPath;
   322	    if (typeof r.logContent === 'string') {
   323	      const dir = logPath.slice(0, logPath.lastIndexOf('/'));
   324	      io.fs.mkdirSync(dir, { recursive: true });
   325	      io.fs.writeFileSync(logPath, r.logContent);
   326	    }
   327	    return Promise.resolve({ timedOut: r.timedOut ?? false, exitCode: r.exitCode ?? 0, verdict: r.verdict ?? 'APPROVED', findings: r.findings ?? '', logPath });
   328	  };
   329	  fn.calls = calls;
   330	  return fn;
   331	}
   332	
   333	function makeLoopRepo({ spec = loopSpec(), files = {}, runner, execFile, env = {} } = {}) {
   334	  const io = makeIo({
   335	    now: T0,
   336	    host: 'loop-host',
   337	    pid: 4242,
   338	    startTime: 111000,
   339	    fencingSeed: 1,
   340	    env,
   341	    files: {
   342	      [SIG_ABS]: SIG_CONTENT,
   343	      '/repo/baton.config.json': JSON.stringify(CONFIG, null, 2),
   344	      ...(spec ? { '/repo/loop.json': JSON.stringify(spec, null, 2) + '\n' } : {}),
   345	      ...files,
   346	    },
   347	  });
   348	  if (execFile) io.execFile = execFile;
   349	  const fake = runner ?? fakeRunner(io, []);
   350	  io.superviseChild = fake;
   351	  io.__runner = fake;
   352	  return io;
   353	}
   354	
   355	const argsOf = (io, i) => (io.__runner.calls[i]?.args ?? []).map(String);
   356	const serialize = (io, i) => argsOf(io, i).join(' ');
   357	const valAfter = (arr, flag) => { const i = arr.indexOf(flag); return i >= 0 ? arr[i + 1] : undefined; };
   358	const loopFile = (io, name) => io.files()[`${loopPaths('/repo').dir}/${name}`];
   359

   383	describe('loop run — supervisor run lock', () => {
   384	  it('a LIVE supervisor.lock refuses a second run (exit 1) naming the live pid', async () => {
   385	    const io = makeLoopRepo({ execFile: undefined });
   386	    // A different, ALIVE supervisor already holds the lock.
   387	    io.processAlive = (p) => p === 55555;
   388	    const p = loopPaths('/repo');
   389	    io.fs.mkdirSync(p.dir, { recursive: true });
   390	    io.fs.writeFileSync(`${p.dir}/supervisor.lock`, JSON.stringify({ host: 'loop-host', pid: 55555, startTime: 222, runId: 'loop-other' }));
   391	
   392	    const code = await cmdLoop(['run'], io);
   393	    assert.equal(code, 1, 'a live supervisor blocks a second run');
   394	    assert.match(io.stderrText() + io.stdoutText(), /55555/, 'the refusal names the live supervisor pid');
   395	    assert.equal(io.__runner.calls.length, 0, 'no child spawned while another supervisor is live');
   396	  });
   397	
   398	  it('a provably-DEAD supervisor.lock is recovered and the run proceeds', async () => {
   399	    const io = makeLoopRepo({ runner: undefined });
   400	    io.superviseChild = fakeRunner(io, [{ verdict: 'APPROVED' }, { verdict: 'APPROVED' }]);
   401	    io.__runner = io.superviseChild;
   402	    // Default processAlive: only this io's own pid is alive; 999999 is dead.
   403	    const p = loopPaths('/repo');
   404	    io.fs.mkdirSync(p.dir, { recursive: true });
   405	    io.fs.writeFileSync(`${p.dir}/supervisor.lock`, JSON.stringify({ host: 'loop-host', pid: 999999, startTime: 7, runId: 'loop-dead' }));
   406	
   407	    const code = await cmdLoop(['run'], io);
   408	    assert.equal(code, 0, 'a dead lock is reclaimed and the run completes');
   409	    assert.ok(io.__runner.calls.length >= 1, 'children were spawned after recovering the dead lock');
   410	  });

   414	describe('loop run — phase driving', () => {

   418	    io.__runner = io.superviseChild;
   419

   421	    assert.equal(code, 0, 'a fully-APPROVED run finishes clean');
   422	    assert.equal(io.__runner.calls.length, 2, 'exactly one child per phase');
   423

   426	    assert.ok(argsOf(io, 0).includes('-p'), 'the planner child runs headless');
   427	    // Phase 2: plan-reviewer -> codex/gpt-5.6-sol, reviewer read-only sandbox.
   428	    assert.equal(io.__runner.calls[1].command, 'codex');

   430	    assert.equal(valAfter(argsOf(io, 1), '-s'), 'read-only', 'the reviewer runs in a read-only sandbox');
   431	
   432	    // Every spawn is given a real per-child log path under .handoff/loop/children/
   433	    // plus its platform and timeout/grace budget.
   434	    for (const [i] of io.__runner.calls.entries()) {
   435	      const opts = io.__runner.calls[i].opts;
   436	      assert.match(String(opts.logPath), /\/\.handoff\/loop\/children\//, `child ${i} log path is under .handoff/loop/children/`);
   437	      assert.ok(typeof opts.platform === 'string' && opts.platform.length > 0, `child ${i} opts carry the platform`);
   438	      assert.ok(typeof opts.timeoutMs === 'number' && opts.timeoutMs > 0, `child ${i} opts carry a timeout`);
   439	      assert.ok(typeof opts.graceMs === 'number' && opts.graceMs > 0, `child ${i} opts carry a grace period`);
   440	    }
   441	
   442	    const { state } = await loadLoopState('/repo', io);
   443	    assert.equal(state.status, LOOP_STATUS.DONE, 'the run reached done');
   444	    assert.equal(state.phaseIndex, 2, 'both phases advanced');
   445	    assert.ok(loopFile(io, 'state.json'), 'loop state persisted under .handoff/loop');
   446	    assert.ok(io.fs.existsSync(loopPaths('/repo').journal), 'the loop journal persisted');
   447	  });

   468	    assert.equal(code, 3, 'escalation exits 3 (defined nonzero)');
   469	    assert.equal(io.__runner.calls.length, 5, 'exactly five gate attempts — a 6th is never spawned');
   470	    // Each retry carries the PRECEDING attempt's findings — later findings are
   471	    // not dropped in favor of the first.
   472	    assert.match(serialize(io, 1), /FINDING-ALPHA-1/, "the 2nd attempt's prompt carries the 1st attempt's findings");
   473	    assert.match(serialize(io, 2), /FINDING-BETA-2/, "the 3rd attempt's prompt carries the 2nd attempt's findings (continuity, not just the first)");
   474	
   475	    const { state } = await loadLoopState('/repo', io);
   476	    assert.equal(state.status, LOOP_STATUS.ESCALATED, 'the run escalated at the cap');
   477	    const esc = loopFile(io, 'ESCALATION.md');
   478	    assert.ok(esc, 'an ESCALATION.md was written under .handoff/loop');
   479	    assert.match(esc, /gate-1/, 'the escalation names the exhausted gate');
   480	  });

   483	// ===========================================================================
   484	describe('loop run — smoke gate', () => {
   485	  const smokeSpec = loopSpec({
   486	    smoke: { cmd: 'npm run smoke', expect: 'ok' },
   487	    phases: [
   488	      { id: 'smoke-tests', role: 'test-author' },
   489	      { id: 'smoke-verify', role: 'test-verifier' },
   490	      { id: 'smoke-build', role: 'implementer' },
   491	      { id: 'subtask-implement', role: 'implementer' },
   492	    ],
   493	  });
   494	
   495	  function smokeExecFile(rec) {
   496	    return (/** @type {string} */ cmd, /** @type {string[]} */ args = []) => {
   497	      rec.push([cmd, ...args].join(' '));
   498	      // git stays unavailable; the smoke command "passes".
   499	      if (cmd === 'git') return Promise.reject(Object.assign(new Error('spawn ENOENT'), { code: 'ENOENT' }));
   500	      return Promise.resolve({ stdout: 'ok\n', stderr: '' });
   501	    };
   502	  }
   503	
   504	  it('after smoke-build: runs smoke.cmd, writes SMOKE-REVIEW.md + a smoke-approval token, awaits, exit 0, no further child', async () => {
   505	    const rec = [];
   506	    const io = makeLoopRepo({ spec: smokeSpec, execFile: smokeExecFile(rec), runner: undefined });
   507	    io.superviseChild = fakeRunner(io, [{ verdict: 'APPROVED' }, { verdict: 'APPROVED' }, { verdict: 'APPROVED' }]);
   508	    io.__runner = io.superviseChild;
   509	
   510	    const code = await cmdLoop(['run'], io);
   511	    assert.equal(code, 0, 'pausing for smoke approval is a clean exit 0');
   512	    assert.equal(io.__runner.calls.length, 3, 'the smoke slice ran (3 phases); the post-smoke phase did NOT spawn');
   513	    assert.ok(rec.some((c) => /smoke/.test(c)), 'smoke.cmd was executed via io.execFile');
   514	
   515	    assert.ok(loopFile(io, 'SMOKE-REVIEW.md'), 'SMOKE-REVIEW.md was written');
   516	    const approval = loopFile(io, 'smoke-approval.json');
   517	    assert.ok(approval, 'a smoke-approval record was written');
   518	    const token = JSON.parse(approval).token;
   519	    assert.ok(typeof token === 'string' && token.startsWith('smk1.'), 'the approval carries a smokeApprovalToken over the six inputs');
   520	
   521	    const { state } = await loadLoopState('/repo', io);
   522	    assert.equal(state.status, LOOP_STATUS.AWAITING_SMOKE_APPROVAL, 'the run is awaiting smoke approval');
   523	  });
   524	
   525	  it('--approve-smoke <correct token> resumes into the next phase (a child spawns again)', async () => {
   526	    const rec = [];
   527	    const io = makeLoopRepo({ spec: smokeSpec, execFile: smokeExecFile(rec), runner: undefined });
   528	    io.superviseChild = fakeRunner(io, [{ verdict: 'APPROVED' }, { verdict: 'APPROVED' }, { verdict: 'APPROVED' }]);
   529	    io.__runner = io.superviseChild;
   530	    assert.equal(await cmdLoop(['run'], io), 0);
   531	    const token = JSON.parse(loopFile(io, 'smoke-approval.json')).token;

   534	    const runner2 = fakeRunner(io, [{ verdict: 'APPROVED' }]);

   538	    assert.equal(code, 0);
   539	    assert.equal(runner2.calls.length, 1, 'a verified approval continues into the post-smoke phase');
   540	  });
   541	
   542	  it('--approve-smoke with the REAL issued token after a bound input drifted -> refused: exit 4, PARKED, zero spawns', async () => {
   543	    // A git-aware execFile whose HEAD is a mutable ref — HEAD is a bound token
   544	    // input, so moving it between issuance and approval is genuine drift.
   545	    let head = 'HEAD-A';
   546	    const gitAware = (/** @type {string} */ cmd, /** @type {string[]} */ args = []) => {
   547	      if (cmd === 'git') {
   548	        const key = args.join(' ');
   549	        const out = key === 'rev-parse HEAD' ? head : key === 'rev-parse --abbrev-ref HEAD' ? 'main' : '';
   550	        return Promise.resolve({ stdout: out + '\n', stderr: '' });
   551	      }
   552	      return Promise.resolve({ stdout: 'ok\n', stderr: '' }); // the smoke command
   553	    };
   554	    const io = makeLoopRepo({ spec: smokeSpec, execFile: gitAware, runner: undefined });
   555	    io.superviseChild = fakeRunner(io, [{ verdict: 'APPROVED' }, { verdict: 'APPROVED' }, { verdict: 'APPROVED' }]);
   556	    io.__runner = io.superviseChild;
   557	    assert.equal(await cmdLoop(['run'], io), 0);
   558	    const token = JSON.parse(loopFile(io, 'smoke-approval.json')).token; // the ACTUAL issued smk1 token
   559	
   560	    // Drift a bound input: HEAD moves after the human looked.
   561	    head = 'HEAD-B';
   562	    const runner2 = fakeRunner(io, [{ verdict: 'APPROVED' }]);
   563	    io.superviseChild = runner2;
   564	    io.__runner = runner2;
   565	    const code = await cmdLoop(['run', '--approve-smoke', token], io); // the OLD token, now stale
   566	    assert.equal(code, 4, 'the plan parks on smoke-token drift');
   567	    assert.equal(runner2.calls.length, 0, 'a drifted approval spawns no child');
   568	    const { state } = await loadLoopState('/repo', io);
   569	    assert.equal(state.status, LOOP_STATUS.PARKED, 'drift parks the run (not left silently awaiting)');
   570	  });
   571	});

   662	// A seeded handoff bundle so a pipeline writer limit-death has a real bundle to

   664	function ownedBundle(originPlatform = 'claude-code', status = 'open') {

   673	    plan: { steps: [] },

   679	      status === 'sealed'
   680	        ? { status: 'sealed', reason: 'seal', reasonClass: 'usage-limit', toPlatformHint: null, finalizedAt: T0, receive_log: [] }
   681	        : { status: 'open', reason: null, reasonClass: null, toPlatformHint: null, finalizedAt: null, receive_log: [] },
   682	    journalSeq: 0,
   683	    compaction: { droppedDecisions: 0, note: null },
   684	    dedupeRing: [],

   688	/**
   689	 * A STRICT, stateful fake git: unstubbed commands reject; worktree add/list and
   690	 * checkout -b are modeled so setup + preflight/postflight + merge cohere.
   691	 * `over` overrides specific responses (e.g. a merge conflict).
   692	 */
   693	function pipelineGit({ over = {}, log = CLEAN_LOG, existingBranches = [], dirtyCwds = [], mergedBranches = [] } = {}) {
   694	  const added = /** @type {Map<string,string>} */ (new Map()); // path -> branch
   695	  const branchAt = /** @type {Map<string,string>} */ (new Map()); // cwd -> current branch
   696	  const branches = new Set(existingBranches); // stale/pre-existing namespaced branches
   697	  const dirty = new Set(dirtyCwds); // seat cwds whose index is dirty
   698	  const deleted = new Set(); // seats listed by git but raw-deleted on disk
   699	  const merged = new Set(mergedBranches); // branches already merged into main (ancestor; empty main..branch)
   700	  const calls = /** @type {any[]} */ ([]);
   701	  const lockProbeRef = { fn: /** @type {null | (() => boolean)} */ (null) };
   702	  let mainSha = MAIN_SHA; // mutable so a child can "move main" mid-subtask
   703	
   704	  const list = () => {
   705	    const entries = [{ path: '/repo', branch: 'main' }];
   706	    for (const [path, branch] of added) entries.push({ path, branch: branchAt.get(path) ?? branch });
   707	    return entries.map((e) => `worktree ${e.path}\nHEAD ${MAIN_SHA}\nbranch refs/heads/${e.branch}\n`).join('\n');
   708	  };
   709	
   710	  const fn = (/** @type {string} */ cmd, /** @type {string[]} */ args = [], /** @type {any} */ opts = {}) => {
   711	    const argstr = args.join(' ');
   712	    calls.push({ cmd, args, argstr, cwd: opts?.cwd, lockPresent: lockProbeRef.fn ? lockProbeRef.fn() : undefined });
   713	    if (cmd !== 'git') return Promise.reject(Object.assign(new Error('spawn ENOENT'), { code: 'ENOENT' }));
   714	
   715	    // Explicit overrides win.
   716	    const okey = Object.keys(over).sort((a, b) => b.length - a.length).find((k) => argstr.includes(k));
   717	    if (okey) {
   718	      const r = over[okey];
   719	      if (r?.reject) return Promise.reject(Object.assign(new Error(r.stderr || 'git failed'), { code: r.code ?? 1, stdout: r.stdout ?? '', stderr: r.stderr ?? '' }));
   720	      return Promise.resolve({ stdout: r?.stdout ?? '', stderr: r?.stderr ?? '' });
   721	    }
   722	
   723	    // A raw-deleted seat: git still LISTS it, but any command IN that cwd fails
   724	    // (the directory is gone). Only self-heal (prune + re-add) recovers it.
   725	    if (opts?.cwd && deleted.has(opts.cwd) && !argstr.startsWith('worktree ')) {
   726	      return Promise.reject(Object.assign(new Error(`fatal: cannot chdir to '${opts.cwd}': No such file or directory`), { code: 128, stderr: 'No such file or directory\n' }));
   727	    }
   728	    if (argstr.startsWith('worktree list')) return Promise.resolve({ stdout: list(), stderr: '' });
   729	    if (argstr.startsWith('worktree add')) {
   730	      const path = args.find((a) => a.startsWith('/repo/.worktrees/'));
   731	      const bi = args.indexOf('-b');
   732	      const branch = bi >= 0 ? args[bi + 1] : 'baton/wt-x/base';
   733	      if (path) { added.set(path, branch); branchAt.set(path, branch); deleted.delete(path); }
   734	      return Promise.resolve({ stdout: '', stderr: '' });
   735	    }
   736	    if (argstr.startsWith('worktree prune')) { deleted.clear(); return Promise.resolve({ stdout: '', stderr: '' }); }
   737	    if (argstr.startsWith('worktree remove')) return Promise.resolve({ stdout: '', stderr: '' });
   738	    if (argstr.startsWith('checkout -b')) {
   739	      const target = args[args.indexOf('-b') + 1];
   740	      if (branches.has(target)) return Promise.reject(Object.assign(new Error(`fatal: a branch named '${target}' already exists`), { code: 128, stderr: `fatal: a branch named '${target}' already exists\n` }));
   741	      branches.add(target);
   742	      if (opts?.cwd) branchAt.set(opts.cwd, target);
   743	      return Promise.resolve({ stdout: '', stderr: '' });
   744	    }
   745	    if (argstr.startsWith('status --porcelain')) return Promise.resolve({ stdout: dirty.has(opts?.cwd) ? ' M dirty.txt\n' : '', stderr: '' });
   746	    if (argstr === 'rev-parse --abbrev-ref HEAD') return Promise.resolve({ stdout: `${branchAt.get(opts?.cwd) ?? 'main'}\n`, stderr: '' });
   747	    if (argstr.startsWith('rev-parse')) return Promise.resolve({ stdout: `${mainSha}\n`, stderr: '' });
   748	    // merge-base --is-ancestor <branch> main : exit 0 iff <branch> is already in main.
   749	    if (argstr.startsWith('merge-base --is-ancestor')) {
   750	      const b = args[args.indexOf('--is-ancestor') + 1];
   751	      return merged.has(b) ? Promise.resolve({ stdout: '', stderr: '' }) : Promise.reject(Object.assign(new Error('not an ancestor'), { code: 1, stdout: '', stderr: '' }));
   752	    }
   753	    if (argstr.startsWith('log ')) {
   754	      // A merged branch has NO commits ahead of main (empty main..<branch>).
   755	      const range = args.find((a) => /\.\./.test(String(a))) ?? '';
   756	      const branch = String(range).split('..')[1];
   757	      if (branch && merged.has(branch)) return Promise.resolve({ stdout: '', stderr: '' });
   758	      return Promise.resolve({ stdout: log, stderr: '' });
   759	    }
   760	    if (argstr.startsWith('merge')) return Promise.resolve({ stdout: 'Fast-forward', stderr: '' });
   761	    if (argstr.startsWith('branch -D')) return Promise.resolve({ stdout: '', stderr: '' });
   762	    return Promise.reject(Object.assign(new Error(`unstubbed git: ${argstr}`), { code: 'ENOSTUB' }));
   763	  };
   764	  fn.calls = calls;
   765	  fn.git = () => calls.filter((c) => c.cmd === 'git');
   766	  fn.issued = (/** @type {RegExp} */ re) => calls.some((c) => c.cmd === 'git' && re.test(c.argstr));
   767	  fn.matching = (/** @type {RegExp} */ re) => calls.filter((c) => c.cmd === 'git' && re.test(c.argstr));
   768	  fn.withCwd = (/** @type {RegExp} */ re, /** @type {string} */ cwd) => calls.filter((c) => c.cmd === 'git' && re.test(c.argstr) && c.cwd === cwd);
   769	  fn.setLockProbe = (/** @type {() => boolean} */ f) => { lockProbeRef.fn = f; };
   770	  fn.moveMain = (/** @type {string} */ sha) => { mainSha = sha; }; // a child "moves main" mid-subtask
   771	  fn.setBranch = (/** @type {string} */ cwd, /** @type {string} */ branch) => { branchAt.set(cwd, branch); };
   772	  // Explicit seed: git LISTS this worktree; `raw` marks its dir as deleted on
   773	  // disk (any in-cwd command fails until prune + re-add self-heals it).
   774	  fn.seedWorktree = (/** @type {string} */ path, /** @type {string} */ branch, /** @type {{raw?: boolean}} */ o = {}) => {
   775	    added.set(path, branch);
   776	    branchAt.set(path, branch);
   777	    if (o.raw) deleted.add(path);
   778	  };
   779	  return fn;
   780	}
   781	
   782	function fakeRunner(io, script) {
   783	  const queue = [...script];
   784	  const calls = /** @type {any[]} */ ([]);
   785	  const fn = (/** @type {any} */ spec, /** @type {any} */ opts = {}) => {
   786	    calls.push({ spec, opts, command: spec?.command, args: spec?.args ?? [], env: spec?.env });
   787	    const r = queue.shift() ?? { verdict: 'APPROVED' };
   788	    if (typeof r.logContent === 'string' && opts.logPath) {
   789	      const dir = opts.logPath.slice(0, opts.logPath.lastIndexOf('/'));
   790	      io.fs.mkdirSync(dir, { recursive: true });
   791	      io.fs.writeFileSync(opts.logPath, r.logContent);
   792	    }
   793	    // A child may perturb the world (e.g. move main) between spawn and postflight.
   794	    if (typeof r.effect === 'function') r.effect();
   795	    return Promise.resolve({ timedOut: false, exitCode: r.exitCode ?? 0, verdict: r.verdict ?? 'APPROVED', findings: r.findings ?? '', logPath: opts.logPath });
   796	  };
   797	  fn.calls = calls;
   798	  return fn;
   799	}
   800	
   801	function makePipeRepo({ spec = pipelineSpec(), config = PIPELINE_CONFIG, files = {}, git, runner, env = {} } = {}) {
   802	  const io = makeIo({
   803	    now: T0,
   804	    host: 'pipe-host',
   805	    pid: 4242,
   806	    startTime: 111000,
   807	    env,
   808	    files: {
   809	      [SIG_ABS]: SIG_CONTENT,
   810	      '/repo/.git/HEAD': 'ref: refs/heads/main\n',
   811	      '/repo/baton.config.json': JSON.stringify(config, null, 2),
   812	      ...(spec ? { '/repo/loop.json': JSON.stringify(spec, null, 2) + '\n' } : {}),
   813	      ...files,
   814	    },
   815	  });
   816	  const g = git ?? pipelineGit();
   817	  g.setLockProbe(() => io.fs.existsSync(MERGE_LOCK));
   818	  io.execFile = g;
   819	  const fake = runner ?? fakeRunner(io, []);
   820	  io.superviseChild = fake;
   821	  io.__git = g;
   822	  io.__runner = fake;
   823	  return io;
   824	}

   826	// A full clean subtask = writer APPROVED, reviewer APPROVED, merger APPROVED.
   827	const cleanSubtask = () => [{ verdict: 'APPROVED' }, { verdict: 'APPROVED' }, { verdict: 'APPROVED' }];
   828	const argsOf = (call) => (call?.args ?? []).map(String);
   829	const valAfter = (arr, flag) => { const i = arr.indexOf(flag); return i >= 0 ? arr[i + 1] : undefined; };
   830	const childWithModel = (runner, model) => runner.calls.filter((c) => argsOf(c).includes(model));
   831	// Writers = the write-capable children (in call order). Reviewers = read-only
   832	// children whose prompt carries the 'subtask-reviewer' role identity (so the
   833	// merger, also read-only, is excluded).
   834	const writersOf = (runner) => runner.calls.filter((c) => valAfter(argsOf(c), '-s') === 'workspace-write');
   835	const reviewersOf = (runner) => runner.calls.filter((c) => valAfter(argsOf(c), '-s') === 'read-only' && argsOf(c).join(' ').includes('subtask-reviewer'));
   836

   867	   470	
   868	   471	  it('BATON_SUPERVISED_CHILD does NOT suppress loop run (supervisor-side)', async () => {
   869	   472	    const io = makeLoopRepo({ env: { BATON_SUPERVISED_CHILD: '1' }, runner: undefined });
   870	   473	    io.superviseChild = fakeRunner(io, [{ verdict: 'APPROVED' }, { verdict: 'APPROVED' }]);
   871	   474	    io.__runner = io.superviseChild;
   872	   475	    const code = await cmdLoop(['run'], io);
   873	   476	    assert.equal(code, 0, 'the guard is child-only; the supervisor command still runs');
   874	   477	    assert.ok(io.__runner.calls.length >= 1, 'children were spawned under the guard env');
   875	   478	  });
   876	   479	});
   877	   480	
   878	   481	// ===========================================================================
   879	   482	// B8 (G2) — the run-lock acquisition must be ATOMIC, not check-then-write.
   880	   483	describe('loop run — atomic run-lock acquisition (B8)', () => {
   881	   484	  const DIR = `${loopPaths('/repo').dir}`;
   882	   485	  const LOCK = `${DIR}/supervisor.lock`;
   883	   486	
   884	   487	  it('RED (B8): implementation-neutral — the competitor materializes AT the exclusive-create; wx/mkdir-first refuses, check-then-write clobbers (RED)', async () => {
   885	   488	    const io = makeLoopRepo({ runner: undefined });
   886	   489	    io.superviseChild = fakeRunner(io, [{ verdict: 'APPROVED' }, { verdict: 'APPROVED' }]);
   887	   490	    io.__runner = io.superviseChild;
   888	   491	    io.processAlive = (p) => p === 55555; // the competitor supervisor is LIVE
   889	   492	
   890	   493	    const realWrite = io.fs.writeFileSync.bind(io.fs);
   891	   494	    const realMkdir = io.fs.mkdirSync.bind(io.fs);
   892	   495	    const realRename = io.fs.renameSync.bind(io.fs);
   893	   496	    const COMPETITOR = JSON.stringify({ host: 'loop-host', pid: 55555, startTime: 222, runId: 'competitor' });
   894	   497	
   895	   498	    // The competitor wins the lock the INSTANT before the supervisor's own write
   896	   499	    // to LOCK lands — injected at whichever primitive the acquire uses to create
   897	   500	    // it. NO readFileSync hook, so a wx/mkdir-FIRST acquire (which never reads
   898	   501	    // before creating) cannot dodge the race.
   899	   502	    let injected = false;
   900	   503	    const injectBefore = (/** @type {any} */ target) => {
   901	   504	      if (injected || String(target) !== LOCK) return;
   902	   505	      injected = true;
   903	   506	      realMkdir(DIR, { recursive: true });
   904	   507	      realWrite(LOCK, COMPETITOR);
   905	   508	    };
   906	   509	
   907	   510	    // Exclusive create (flag wx/ax) enforces the O_EXCL semantics memfs lacks:
   908	   511	    // once the competitor exists, an exclusive write throws EEXIST.
   909	   512	    io.fs.writeFileSync = (/** @type {any} */ p, /** @type {any} */ data, /** @type {any} */ opts) => {
   910	   513	      const flag = typeof opts === 'string' ? undefined : opts?.flag;
   911	   514	      const exclusive = flag === 'wx' || flag === 'ax' || flag === 'wx+' || flag === 'ax+';
   912	   515	      injectBefore(p);
   913	   516	      if (exclusive && String(p) === LOCK && io.fs.existsSync(p)) throw Object.assign(new Error(`EEXIST: file already exists, open '${p}'`), { code: 'EEXIST' });
   914	   517	      return realWrite(p, data, opts); // a PLAIN write to LOCK clobbers the competitor
   915	   518	    };
   916	   519	    // mkdirSync(LOCK) as an exclusive lock dir: memfs already throws EEXIST when a
   917	   520	    // node exists at the path (the injected competitor file).
   918	   521	    io.fs.mkdirSync = (/** @type {any} */ p, /** @type {any} */ opts) => { injectBefore(p); return realMkdir(p, opts); };
   919	   522	    // A tmp+rename publish (atomicWriteJson) clobbers the competitor on rename.
   920	   523	    io.fs.renameSync = (/** @type {any} */ from, /** @type {any} */ to) => { injectBefore(to); return realRename(from, to); };
   921	   524	
   922	   525	    const code = await cmdLoop(['run'], io);
   923	   526	    assert.equal(code, 1, 'a wx/mkdir-first exclusive acquire observes EEXIST and refuses the run');
   924	   527	    assert.equal(io.__runner.calls.length, 0, 'no child spawned when the lock was lost to the competitor');
   925	   528	    assert.match(JSON.parse(io.files()[LOCK]).runId, /competitor/, "the competitor's lock is not clobbered (a check-then-write path fails here)");
   926	   529	  });
   927	   530	});
   928	   531	
   929	   532	// ===========================================================================
   930	   533	// G4 (A4) — record in-flight child groups; kill recorded orphans on reclaim.
   931	   534	describe('loop run — child-group recording + kill-on-reclaim (G4)', () => {
   932	   535	  const DIR = `${loopPaths('/repo').dir}`;
   933	   536	
   934	   537	  const readChildren = (io) => {
   935	   538	    const raw = io.files()[`${DIR}/children.ndjson`];
   936	   539	    if (typeof raw !== 'string') return [];
   937	   540	    return raw.split('\n').filter((l) => l.trim() !== '').map((l) => JSON.parse(l));
   938	   541	  };
   939	   542	
   940	   543	  it('RED (G4): a child-start record {childId, pid, pgid, startedAt} exists on disk WHILE the child is in flight (before completion)', async () => {
   941	   544	    const io = makeLoopRepo({ runner: undefined });
   942	   545	    let n = 0;
   943	   546	    const inflightOk = [];
   944	   547	    // The runner reports the child's group via opts.onStart (spawn time); the
   945	   548	    // supervisor must persist the record synchronously in that handler — BEFORE
   946	   549	    // the child completes — so a mid-run crash leaves the orphan reap-able.
   947	   550	    io.superviseChild = (/** @type {any} */ _spec, /** @type {any} */ opts) => {
   948	   551	      n += 1;
   949	   552	      const info = { pid: 8000 + n, pgid: 9000 + n };
   950	   553	      if (typeof opts.onStart === 'function') opts.onStart(info);
   951	   554	      const rec = readChildren(io).find((r) => r.pgid === info.pgid);
   952	   555	      inflightOk.push(
   953	   556	        !!rec && typeof rec.childId === 'string' && rec.childId.length > 0 && rec.pid === info.pid && typeof rec.startedAt === 'string' && rec.startedAt.length > 0,
   954	   557	      );
   955	   558	      return Promise.resolve({ timedOut: false, exitCode: 0, verdict: 'APPROVED', findings: '', logPath: opts.logPath, ...info });
   956	   559	    };
   957	   560	    const code = await cmdLoop(['run'], io);
   958	   561	    assert.equal(code, 0);
   959	   562	    assert.ok(inflightOk.length >= 1, 'at least one child ran');
   960	   563	    assert.ok(inflightOk.every(Boolean), 'each child-start record ({childId,pid,pgid,startedAt}) existed on disk while its child was in flight');
   961	   564	  });
   962	   565	
   963	   566	  it('RED (G5/G4): on dead-lock reclaim, a still-alive recorded orphan group is KILLED (io.processKill) BEFORE the first new spawn', async () => {
   964	   567	    const io = makeLoopRepo({ runner: undefined });
   965	   568	    const events = []; // ordered: kills and spawns interleaved
   966	   569	    io.processKill = (/** @type {number} */ pid, /** @type {any} */ sig) => { events.push({ kind: 'kill', pid, sig }); return true; };
   967	   570	    const baseRunner = fakeRunner(io, [{ verdict: 'APPROVED' }, { verdict: 'APPROVED' }]);
   968	   571	    io.superviseChild = (/** @type {any} */ spec, /** @type {any} */ opts) => { events.push({ kind: 'spawn' }); return baseRunner(spec, opts); };
   969	   572	    io.__runner = baseRunner;
   970	   573	    // The dead supervisor's orphan group (pgid 9001) is still alive; its lock is dead.
   971	   574	    io.processAlive = (/** @type {number} */ p) => p === 9001;
   972	   575	    io.fs.mkdirSync(DIR, { recursive: true });
   973	   576	    io.fs.writeFileSync(`${DIR}/supervisor.lock`, JSON.stringify({ host: 'loop-host', pid: 999999, startTime: 7, runId: 'crashed' }));
   974	   577	    io.fs.writeFileSync(`${DIR}/children.ndjson`, JSON.stringify({ childId: '001-plan', pid: 8001, pgid: 9001, startedAt: T0 }) + '\n');
   975	   578	
   976	   579	    const code = await cmdLoop(['run'], io);
   977	   580	    assert.equal(code, 0, `the reclaiming run completes after reaping orphans; stderr: ${io.stderrText()}`);
   978	   581	    const firstSpawn = events.findIndex((e) => e.kind === 'spawn');
   979	   582	    const killIdx = events.findIndex((e) => e.kind === 'kill' && Math.abs(e.pid) === 9001);
   980	   583	    assert.ok(killIdx >= 0, `the orphan group 9001 was killed on reclaim; events: ${JSON.stringify(events)}`);
   981	   584	    assert.ok(firstSpawn === -1 || killIdx < firstSpawn, 'the orphan group was killed BEFORE the first new child spawned');
   982	   585	  });
   983	   586	});
   984	   587	
   985	   588	// ===========================================================================
   986	   589	// G6 (B6) — `baton loop resume`: PARKED -> running; ESCALATED refused.
   987	   590	describe('loop run — resume (G6)', () => {

  1007	   610

  1020	   623

  1078	   681	    return raw.split('\n').filter((l) => l.trim() !== '').map((l) => JSON.parse(l));

  1087	   690	    io.superviseChild = (/** @type {any} */ _spec, /** @type {any} */ opts) => {

  1094	   884	// H5 (B4) — state flavor + spec binding across loop/pipeline.
  1095	   885	describe('pipeline — H5: state flavor + spec-digest binding', () => {
  1096	   886	  it('RED (H5a): a state.json created by `loop run` (flavor loop) is REFUSED by pipeline run (exit 2 naming the mismatch), zero spawns', async () => {

  1121	   911	
  1122	   912	// ===========================================================================
  1123	   913	// I1 (A1/B1) — auto-advance requires a supervisor-owned MERGE RECEIPT, not just
  1124	   914	// is-ancestor: a stale empty branch at main's tip is is-ancestor-true too, and
  1125	   915	// must PARK, not silently skip the subtask.
  1126	   916	//
  1127	   917	// Merge-receipt shape (I1 design): append-only `.handoff/loop/merges.ndjson`,
  1128	   918	// one line per merged subtask `{ subtaskId, branch, mergedAt }`. Auto-advance on
  1129	   919	// resume requires is-ancestor(branch, main) AND a receipt for that subtask.
  1130	   920	describe('pipeline — I1: merge-receipt-gated auto-advance', () => {
  1131	   921	  const MERGES = `${loopPaths('/repo').dir}/merges.ndjson`;
  1132	   922	  const seedReceipt = (io, subtaskId, branch) => {
  1133	   923	    io.fs.mkdirSync(loopPaths('/repo').dir, { recursive: true });
  1134	   924	    io.fs.writeFileSync(MERGES, JSON.stringify({ subtaskId, branch, mergedAt: T0 }) + '\n');
  1135	   925	  };
  1136	   926	  const receipts = (io) => {
  1137	   927	    const raw = io.files()[MERGES];
  1138	   928	    return typeof raw === 'string' ? raw.split('\n').filter((l) => l.trim() !== '').map((l) => JSON.parse(l)) : [];
  1139	   929	  };
  1140	   930	
  1141	   931	  it('RED (I1b): a receipt-backed already-merged subtask ADVANCES on resume (no writer, no empty-branch park)', async () => {
  1142	   932	    const subtasks = [{ id: 't1', title: 'first' }, { id: 't2', title: 'second' }];
  1143	   933	    // t1 is genuinely merged: is-ancestor true, empty main..t1, AND a receipt.
  1144	   934	    const git = pipelineGit({ mergedBranches: ['baton/wt-a/subtask-t1'] });
  1145	   935	    const io = makePipeRepo({ spec: pipelineSpec({ subtasks }), git, runner: undefined });
  1146	   936	    io.superviseChild = fakeRunner(io, cleanSubtask()); // enough for t2 only
  1147	   937	    io.__runner = io.superviseChild;
  1148	   938	    seedPipelineState(io, { flavor: 'pipeline', specDigest: dedupeKey(subtasks), phaseIndex: 0, phaseCount: 2 });
  1149	   939	    seedReceipt(io, 't1', 'baton/wt-a/subtask-t1');
  1150	   940	
  1151	   941	    const code = await run(['pipeline', 'run'], io);
  1152	   942	    assert.equal(code, 0, `a receipt-backed merged subtask advances and the run completes; stderr: ${io.stderrText()}`);
  1153	   943	    assert.doesNotMatch(io.stderrText() + io.stdoutText(), /EMPTY branch|nothing to review or merge|stale/i, 'a receipt-backed subtask is not mistaken for empty/stale');
  1154	   944	    const writers = writersOf(io.__runner);
  1155	   945	    assert.ok(writers.length >= 1, 'the unmerged subtask still runs a writer');
  1156	   946	    assert.equal(valAfter(argsOf(writers[0]), '-C'), WT_B, 'the first writer is t2 (wt-b) — t1 was recognized as merged (receipt) and skipped');
  1157	   947	  });
  1158	   948	
  1159	   949	  it('RED (I1a): a stale EMPTY branch at main tip (is-ancestor true, NO receipt) PARKS as stale — never auto-advances', async () => {
  1160	   950	    const subtasks = [{ id: 't1', title: 'first' }, { id: 't2', title: 'second' }];
  1161	   951	    // t1's branch is empty at main's tip: is-ancestor true, empty main..t1 — but
  1162	   952	    // NO merge receipt (the writer crashed before its first commit).
  1163	   953	    const git = pipelineGit({ mergedBranches: ['baton/wt-a/subtask-t1'] });
  1164	   954	    const io = makePipeRepo({ spec: pipelineSpec({ subtasks }), git, runner: undefined });
  1165	   955	    io.superviseChild = fakeRunner(io, [...cleanSubtask(), ...cleanSubtask()]);
  1166	   956	    io.__runner = io.superviseChild;
  1167	   957	    seedPipelineState(io, { flavor: 'pipeline', specDigest: dedupeKey(subtasks), phaseIndex: 0, phaseCount: 2 });
  1168	   958	    // No receipt seeded.
  1169	   959	
  1170	   960	    const code = await run(['pipeline', 'run'], io);
  1171	   961	    assert.equal(code, 4, 'a receipt-less is-ancestor branch parks as stale rather than silently skipping the subtask');
  1172	   962	    const out = io.stderrText() + io.stdoutText();
  1173	   963	    assert.match(out, /stale/i, 'the park names the stale branch');
  1174	   964	    assert.equal(io.__git.matching(/^merge baton\//).length, 0, 'nothing is merged for a stale empty branch');
  1175	   965	    // J2: the message must hedge BOTH causes — each pinned SEPARATELY so a
  1176	   966	    // single-cause misdiagnosis cannot pass — point at the range to inspect, and
  1177	   967	    // account for the branch still being checked out in the writer seat (a bare
  1178	   968	    // "delete the branch" fails while it is checked out).
  1179	   969	    assert.match(out, /crashed before committing|before.*first commit|pre-?commit/i, 'names the pre-commit-crash cause');
  1180	   970	    assert.match(out, /merged without a receipt|merge.*receipt.*missing|not.*recorded/i, 'ALSO names the merged-without-receipt cause (no single-cause misdiagnosis)');
  1181	   971	    assert.match(out, /git log main\.\.baton\/wt-a\/subtask-t1/, 'points at `git log main..<branch>` to disambiguate');
  1182	   972	    assert.match(out, /seat|worktree|checked out|checkout/i, 'remediation accounts for the branch still being checked out in the writer seat');
  1183	   973	  });
  1184	   974	
  1185	   975	  it('GUARD (I1 conjunction): a receipt WITHOUT is-ancestor does NOT auto-advance — the subtask is re-run, never silently skipped', async () => {
  1186	   976	    const subtasks = [{ id: 't1', title: 'first' }, { id: 't2', title: 'second' }];
  1187	   977	    // A receipt exists for t1, but the branch is NOT an ancestor of main (the
  1188	   978	    // merge did not actually land — e.g. main was rewound). A receipt-ONLY
  1189	   979	    // advance would skip t1; the required conjunction re-runs it.
  1190	   980	    const git = pipelineGit({ mergedBranches: [] }); // is-ancestor FALSE for every branch
  1191	   981	    const io = makePipeRepo({ spec: pipelineSpec({ subtasks }), git, runner: undefined });
  1192	   982	    io.superviseChild = fakeRunner(io, [...cleanSubtask(), ...cleanSubtask()]);
  1193	   983	    io.__runner = io.superviseChild;
  1194	   984	    seedPipelineState(io, { flavor: 'pipeline', specDigest: dedupeKey(subtasks), phaseIndex: 0, phaseCount: 2 });
  1195	   985	    seedReceipt(io, 't1', 'baton/wt-a/subtask-t1'); // receipt present, ancestor absent
  1196	   986	
  1197	   987	    const code = await run(['pipeline', 'run'], io);
  1198	   988	    assert.equal(code, 0, `the run completes by re-running t1; stderr: ${io.stderrText()}`);
  1199	   989	    const writers = writersOf(io.__runner);
  1200	   990	    assert.ok(writers.length >= 1, 'a writer ran');
  1201	   991	    assert.equal(valAfter(argsOf(writers[0]), '-C'), WT_A, 'the FIRST writer is t1 (wt-a) — a receipt alone did NOT skip it (is-ancestor is a required conjunct)');
  1202	   992	  });
  1203	   993	
  1204	   994	  it('RED (I1c): a clean merge PERSISTS a durable receipt {subtaskId, branch, mergedAt} BEFORE the phase-advance is journaled', async () => {
  1205	   995	    const io = makePipeRepo({ spec: pipelineSpec({ subtasks: [{ id: 't1', title: 'only' }] }), runner: undefined });
  1206	   996	    io.superviseChild = fakeRunner(io, cleanSubtask());
  1207	   997	    io.__runner = io.superviseChild;
  1208	   998	
  1209	   999	    // Ordering hook: at each phase-advance journal write, capture whether the t1
  1210	  1000	    // receipt is ALREADY on disk (durable before the advance, not merely by EOR).
  1211	  1001	    const journalPath = `${loopPaths('/repo').dir}/journal.ndjson`;
  1212	  1002	    const receiptAtAdvance = [];
  1213	  1003	    const realAppend = io.fs.appendFileSync.bind(io.fs);
  1214	  1004	    io.fs.appendFileSync = (/** @type {any} */ path, /** @type {any} */ data) => {
  1215	  1005	      if (String(path) === journalPath && /"type":"phase-advance"/.test(String(data))) {
  1216	  1006	        receiptAtAdvance.push(receipts(io).some((r) => r.subtaskId === 't1'));
  1217	  1007	      }
  1218	  1008	      return realAppend(path, data);
  1219	  1009	    };
  1220	  1010	
  1221	  1011	    const code = await run(['pipeline', 'run'], io);
  1222	  1012	    assert.equal(code, 0);
  1223	  1013	    const rec = receipts(io).find((r) => r.subtaskId === 't1');
  1224	  1014	    assert.ok(rec, 'a merge receipt is persisted after the subtask merges');
  1225	  1015	    assert.equal(rec.branch, 'baton/wt-a/subtask-t1', 'the receipt names the merged branch');
  1226	  1016	    assert.ok(typeof rec.mergedAt === 'string' && rec.mergedAt.length > 0, 'the receipt records mergedAt');
  1227	  1017	    assert.ok(receiptAtAdvance.length >= 1 && receiptAtAdvance.every(Boolean), 'the receipt is durable on disk BEFORE the phase advance is journaled (crash-safe)');
  1228	  1018	  });
  1229	  1019	});
  1230	  1020	
  1231	  1021	// ===========================================================================
  1232	  1022	// I3 (A2/B4) — an EXISTING unstamped state (no flavor/specDigest) is refused by
  1233	  1023	// pipeline run, with the archive instruction, zero spawns.
  1234	  1024	describe('pipeline — I3: unstamped legacy state is refused', () => {
  1235	  1025	  it('RED (I3): an unstamped state.json (no flavor/specDigest) refuses exit 2 with the archive instruction, zero spawns', async () => {
  1236	  1026	    const io = makePipeRepo({ spec: pipelineSpec({ subtasks: [{ id: 't1', title: 'a' }, { id: 't2', title: 'b' }] }), runner: undefined });

  1256	  1046	// J1 (B1) — `baton pipeline resume` mirrors G6's loop resume: PARKED -> running
  1257	  1047	// (cap counters intact), ESCALATED refused, flavor-guarded.
  1258	  1048	describe('pipeline — J1: pipeline resume', () => {
  1259	  1049	  const gate = 'subtask-t1-review';
  1260	  1050	
  1261	  1051	  it('RED (J1a): resume transitions a PARKED run to running — the same subtask re-runs its writer, gate counters intact (no refund)', async () => {
  1262	  1052	    const subtasks = [{ id: 't1', title: 'only' }];
  1263	  1053	    const io = makePipeRepo({ spec: pipelineSpec({ subtasks }), runner: undefined });
  1264	  1054	    io.superviseChild = fakeRunner(io, cleanSubtask());
  1265	  1055	    io.__runner = io.superviseChild;
  1266	  1056	    // A parked run mid-subtask t1 with the gate already iterated 3 times.
  1267	  1057	    seedPipelineState(io, {
  1268	  1058	      flavor: 'pipeline', specDigest: dedupeKey(subtasks), phaseIndex: 0, phaseCount: 1,
  1269	  1059	      status: 'parked', parkReason: 'operator paused', iterations: { [gate]: 3 },
  1270	  1060	    });
  1271	  1061	
  1272	  1062	    const code = await run(['pipeline', 'resume'], io);
  1273	  1063	    assert.notEqual(code, 2, 'resume is a recognized subcommand (not a usage error)');
  1274	  1064	    const writers = writersOf(io.__runner);
  1275	  1065	    assert.ok(writers.length >= 1, 'resume re-runs the parked subtask — its writer spawns again');
  1276	  1066	    assert.equal(valAfter(argsOf(writers[0]), '-C'), WT_A, 'the re-run writer is t1 in wt-a (not skipped)');
  1277	  1067	    assert.ok((await loadLoopState('/repo', io)).state.iterations[gate] >= 3, 'the gate cap counter survives resume — no refund');
  1278	  1068	    // J1a: resume must pin SUCCESSFUL continuation of the clean case, not merely
  1279	  1069	    // "not-unknown + writer respawns". The clean subtask completes: exit 0, the
  1280	  1070	    // run reaches DONE, and the journal records a RESUME event BEFORE completion.
  1281	  1071	    assert.equal(code, 0, `resume completes the clean subtask; stderr: ${io.stderrText()}`);
  1282	  1072	    assert.equal((await loadLoopState('/repo', io)).state.status, LOOP_STATUS.DONE, 'the resumed pipeline reaches DONE');
  1283	  1073	    const journal = (io.files()[loopPaths('/repo').journal] ?? '')
  1284	  1074	      .split('\n').filter((l) => l.trim() !== '').map((l) => JSON.parse(l));
  1285	  1075	    const resumeIdx = journal.findIndex((e) => e.type === LOOP_EVENT.RESUME);
  1286	  1076	    assert.ok(resumeIdx >= 0, 'the resume is journaled (a RESUME event is appended)');
  1287	  1077	    const advanceIdx = journal.findIndex((e) => e.type === LOOP_EVENT.PHASE_ADVANCE);
  1288	  1078	    assert.ok(advanceIdx > resumeIdx, 'the RESUME event precedes the phase-advance that completes the run');
  1289	  1079	  });
  1290	  1080	
  1291	  1081	  it('RED (J1b): resume refuses an ESCALATED run (exit 3, operator-only), zero spawns', async () => {
  1292	  1082	    const subtasks = [{ id: 't1', title: 'only' }];

  1316	  1106	  });

  1394	    29	
  1395	    30	- J1: GENUINE. cmdPipeline accepts 'resume' (pipeline.mjs:69) and marks it
  1396	    31	  via an internal flags.__resume (71) — parseFlagsStrict rejects unknown
  1397	    32	  flags, so `--__resume` from the CLI is "unknown flag" exit 2; the
  1398	    33	  back-channel is not injectable. drivePipeline handles resume AFTER the
  1399	    34	  flavor/stamp/digest guards (159–169) and BEFORE the status refusals:
  1400	    35	  ESCALATED → operator-only refusal, exit 3, before any spawn or worktree
  1401	    36	  setup (188–191); PARKED → a journaled RESUME transition then normal
  1402	    37	  continuation (192–195). State is loadLoopState-or-init (154–157) — an
  1403	    38	  existing state is never re-inited, so gate cap counters survive resume
  1404	    39	  with no refund; the RESUME reducer (state.mjs:97–100) touches only
  1405	    40	  status/parkReason and is PARKED-guarded, so the journaled event is legal
  1406	    41	  and replay-safe. The non-resume PARKED refusal now names `baton pipeline
  1407	    42	  resume` (203). Pins: J1a (parked mid-t1 with the gate at 3 → resume →
  1408	    43	  the SAME subtask's writer respawns in wt-a, counter still >= 3, exit 0,
  1409	    44	  DONE, and the journal's RESUME precedes the completing PHASE_ADVANCE),
  1410	    45	  J1b (escalated → exit 3, zero spawns, stays ESCALATED), J1c
  1411	    46	  (loop-flavored state → exit 2, zero spawns), J1d (loop resume still
  1412	    47	  refuses a pipeline-flavored state — the boundary holds both ways). All
  1413	    48	  four run green in the live suite. The park → delete branch → resume →
  1414	    49	  re-run flow is now real end-to-end; an operator who resumes WITHOUT
  1415	    50	  deleting the branch re-parks loudly with the same message — safe.
  1416	    51	- J2: GENUINE. The ancestor-without-receipt park (295–297) now hedges both
  1417	    52	  causes ("either its writer crashed before committing, or the branch was
  1418	    53	  merged without a receipt being recorded"), points at `git log
  1419	    54	  main..<branch>` to disambiguate, tells the operator the branch may still
  1420	    55	  be checked out in its writer seat worktree (checkout the seat's base
  1421	    56	  branch first), and ends with `baton pipeline resume`. The pin asserts
  1422	    57	  each cause with a SEPARATE assertion (no single-cause misdiagnosis can
  1423	    58	  pass), plus the git-log pointer and the seat-checkout hedge.
  1424	    59	
  1425	    60	## Fresh-eyes pass — the new resume code
  1426	    61	
  1427	    62	Probed specifically: resume vs the H5/I3 stamp guards (resume sits after
  1428	    63	them — a loop-flavored, unstamped, or digest-drifted state refuses exit 2
  1429	    64	before RESUME can fire); reducer legality (RESUME is only emitted when
  1430	    65	status === PARKED; from any other status the reducer is an identity, and
  1431	    66	replay applies the journaled event deterministically); a crash between the
  1432	    67	RESUME journal append and the snapshot write (replay folds RESUME in —
  1433	    68	consistent); double-resume (second resume finds RUNNING, no spurious event,
  1434	    69	behaves as run); resume under the supervisor lock (runPipeline acquires it
  1435	    70	before drivePipeline; the escalated return is inside the try/finally, so
  1436	    71	the lock releases — the I4 class does not recur); resume on DONE (falls
  1437	    72	through, loop body skipped, exits 0). No blocking defect found. Three
  1438	    73	note-level observations (findings 1–3 below): the park message's "at
  1439	    74	main's tip" wording is imprecise once main advances past the stale tip
  1440	    75	(is-ancestor matches any ancestor); `pipeline resume` with NO persisted
  1441	    76	state silently initializes and starts a fresh run (mirrors G6's approved
  1442	    77	loop-resume shape, but "resume" acting as "run" in a wrong directory is a
  1443	    78	surprise worth refusing); and the loop-side non-resume PARKED refusal
  1444	    79	(loop.mjs:350) still does not name `baton loop resume` while pipeline's
  1445	    80	now does.

  1448	    83

  1450	    85	  still lands before PHASE_ADVANCE (455–458); torn receipts still
  1451	    86	  fail-toward-park (222–229). The strengthened I1a pin (now also carrying
  1452	    87	  the J2 assertions) still asserts exit 4 + nothing merged.
  1453	    88	- I2/I4/I5: loop.mjs is byte-identical across the fold range — truncation

  1696	The Gate-2 notes map to the three intended pins: stale-message wording is deliberately left to existing I1a coverage, and the two behavioral note items are covered by the new J1e/G6 tests.   1698	/bin/zsh -lc "nl -ba tests/helpers/fakeio.mjs | sed -n '1,260p'" in /Users/saksham/baton
  1699	 succeeded in 0ms:
  1700	     1	// Fake `io` object matching the injected-io contract from docs/design/core.md:
  1701	     2	// { cwd, env, stdin, stdout, stderr, fs, execFile, now }. Backed by memfs.
  1702	     3	// stdout/stderr collect writes; io.stdoutText()/io.stderrText() flatten them.
  1703	     4	// now() returns a settable ISO string (io.setNow). execFile resolves from an
  1704	     5	// execResults map keyed by the joined command string.
  1705	     6	//
  1706	     7	// Wave-B2 additions (bundle lock layer) — all optional, defaulted, and purely
  1707	     8	// additive so no prior consumer is affected:
  1708	     9	//   host / pid / startTime  — the process-identity facts the lock model writes
  1709	    10	//     into owner.json and compares on contention (see plan §Concurrency, Lock
  1710	    11	//     model iterations 3+4).
  1711	    12	//   processAlive(pid, startTime) — the "provably dead" oracle. Default: only
  1712	    13	//     THIS io's own {pid, startTime} is alive; every other descriptor reads as
  1713	    14	//     dead. Override per test to model a live foreign owner or a pid/start-time
  1714	    15	//     mismatch (reused pid).
  1715	    16	//   newFencingToken() — the injected per-acquisition token source. Deterministic
  1716	    17	//     monotonic counter ("tok-1", "tok-2", …), matching the injected-io
  1717	    18	//     discipline already used for now()/execFile so lock acquisition is
  1718	    19	//     reproducible in tests rather than drawing from Math.random.
  1719	    20	
  1720	    21	import { makeMemfs } from './memfs.mjs';
  1721	    22	
  1722	    23	export function makeIo({
  1723	    24	  files = {},
  1724	    25	  env = {},
  1725	    26	  stdin = '',
  1726	    27	  execResults = {},
  1727	    28	  now = '2026-07-11T00:00:00.000Z',
  1728	    29	  host = 'host-A',
  1729	    30	  pid = 4242,
  1730	    31	  startTime = 111000,
  1731	    32	  processAlive = null,
  1732	    33	  fencingSeed = 1,
  1733	    34	  execPath = process.execPath,
  1734	    35	} = {}) {
  1735	    36	  const { fs, files: filesSnapshot } = makeMemfs(files);
  1736	    37	  const outChunks = [];
  1737	    38	  const errChunks = [];
  1738	    39	  let currentNow = now;
  1739	    40	  let tokenCounter = fencingSeed - 1;
  1740	    41	  const defaultAlive = (p, s) => p === pid && s === startTime;
  1741	    42	
  1742	    43	  const stdout = { write: (s) => { outChunks.push(String(s)); return true; } };
  1743	    44	  const stderr = { write: (s) => { errChunks.push(String(s)); return true; } };
  1744	    45	
  1745	    46	  const execFile = (cmd, args = [], /* opts */ _opts = {}) => {
  1746	    47	    const key = [cmd, ...(Array.isArray(args) ? args : [])].join(' ');
  1747	    48	    return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
  1748	    49	      if (!(key in execResults)) {
  1749	    50	        const e = new Error(`spawn ${cmd} ENOENT`);
  1750	    51	        e.code = 'ENOENT';
  1751	    52	        return reject(e);
  1752	    53	      }
  1753	    54	      const r = execResults[key];
  1754	    55	      const code = r.code ?? 0;
  1755	    56	      const out = r.stdout ?? '';
  1756	    57	      const err = r.stderr ?? '';
  1757	    58	      if (code !== 0) {
  1758	    59	        // util.promisify(execFile) rejects on nonzero exit; error carries code + streams.
  1759	    60	        const e = new Error(`Command failed: ${key}`);

  1973	     2	 * Loop run state — snapshot + journal + replay for the Layer-2 supervisor,

  1980	     9	import { appendEntry, readAllTolerant } from '../util/jsonl.mjs';
  1981	    10	import { atomicWriteJson, safeReadJson, ensureDir } from '../util/fsx.mjs';
  1982	    11	import { dedupeKey } from '../util/ids.mjs';
  1983	    12	
  1984	    13	export const LOOP_STATE_SCHEMA = 'baton/loop-state@1';
  1985	    14	
  1986	    15	/** The frozen status vocabulary. */
  1987	    16	export const LOOP_STATUS = Object.freeze({
  1988	    17	  RUNNING: 'running',
  1989	    18	  AWAITING_SMOKE_APPROVAL: 'awaiting-smoke-approval',
  1990	    19	  PARKED: 'parked',
  1991	    20	  ESCALATED: 'escalated',
  1992	    21	  DONE: 'done',
  1993	    22	});
  1994	    23	
  1995	    24	/** The frozen journal event vocabulary. */
  1996	    25	export const LOOP_EVENT = Object.freeze({
  1997	    26	  PHASE_ADVANCE: 'phase-advance',
  1998	    27	  GATE_ITERATION: 'gate-iteration',
  1999	    28	  PARK: 'park',
  2000	    29	  RESUME: 'resume',
  2001	    30	  SMOKE_AWAIT: 'smoke-await',
  2002	    31	  SMOKE_APPROVE: 'smoke-approve',
  2003	    32	});
  2004	    33	
  2005	    34	// Every gate loops at most 5 times — a repo-wide hard invariant (AGENTS.md
  2006	    35	// §Review gates); the reducer refuses a 6th and escalates instead.
  2007	    36	const GATE_ITERATION_CAP = 5;
  2008	    37	
  2009	    38	/** @param {string} root */
  2010	    39	export function loopPaths(root) {
  2011	    40	  const dir = `${root}/.handoff/loop`;
  2012	    41	  return { dir, state: `${dir}/state.json`, journal: `${dir}/journal.ndjson` };
  2013	    42	}
  2014	    43	
  2015	    44	/**
  2016	    45	 * Fresh run state — pure and deterministic from the injected io (identity
  2017	    46	 * facts + clock only; no Date.now/Math.random, which would break replay).
  2018	    47	 * @param {any} spec a VALIDATED loop spec @param {any} io
  2019	    48	 */
  2020	    49	export function initLoopState(spec, io) {
  2021	    50	  const runId = `loop-${dedupeKey({ host: io.host, pid: io.pid, startTime: io.startTime, at: io.now() }).slice(0, 12)}`;
  2022	    51	  /** @type {Record<string, number>} */
  2023	    52	  const iterations = {};
  2024	    53	  for (const p of spec.phases ?? []) {
  2025	    54	    if (/gate/.test(String(p.id))) iterations[p.id] = 0;
  2026	    55	  }
  2027	    56	  return {
  2028	    57	    schema: LOOP_STATE_SCHEMA,
  2029	    58	    runId,
  2030	    59	    goal: spec.goal,
  2031	    60	    phaseCount: Array.isArray(spec.phases) ? spec.phases.length : 0,
  2032	    61	    phaseIndex: 0,
  2033	    62	    iterations,
  2034	    63	    status: LOOP_STATUS.RUNNING,
  2035	    64	    parkReason: null,
  2036	    65	    escalation: null,
  2037	    66	    smokeApproval: null,
  2038	    67	    createdAt: io.now(),
  2039	    68	    journalSeq: 0,
  2040	    69	  };
  2041	    70	}
  2042	    71	
  2043	    72	/**
  2044	    73	 * The pure transition reducer. Never mutates its input; unknown events and
  2045	    74	 * refused transitions return the input state unchanged (a new reference is
  2046	    75	 * only produced for a real transition).
  2047	    76	 * @param {any} state @param {any} ev
  2048	    77	 */
  2049	    78	export function applyLoopEvent(state, ev) {
  2050	    79	  switch (ev?.type) {
  2051	    80	    case LOOP_EVENT.PHASE_ADVANCE: {
  2052	    81	      const next = state.phaseIndex + 1;
  2053	    82	      if (next >= state.phaseCount) return { ...state, phaseIndex: state.phaseCount, status: LOOP_STATUS.DONE };
  2054	    83	      return { ...state, phaseIndex: next };
  2055	    84	    }
  2056	    85	    case LOOP_EVENT.GATE_ITERATION: {
  2057	    86	      const gate = String(ev.gate);
  2058	    87	      const current = state.iterations?.[gate] ?? 0;
  2059	    88	      // The 6th attempt is REFUSED: the counter stays at the cap and the run
  2060	    89	      // escalates with a record naming the gate — never a 6th run.
  2061	    90	      if (current >= GATE_ITERATION_CAP) {
  2062	    91	        return { ...state, status: LOOP_STATUS.ESCALATED, escalation: { gate, iteration: current } };
  2063	    92	      }
  2064	    93	      return { ...state, iterations: { ...state.iterations, [gate]: current + 1 } };
  2065	    94	    }
  2066	    95	    case LOOP_EVENT.PARK:
  2067	    96	      return { ...state, status: LOOP_STATUS.PARKED, parkReason: ev.reason ?? null };
  2068	    97	    case LOOP_EVENT.RESUME:
  2069	    98	      // Only a PARK is resumable; an escalation is an operator decision.
  2070	    99	      if (state.status !== LOOP_STATUS.PARKED) return state;
  2071	   100	      return { ...state, status: LOOP_STATUS.RUNNING, parkReason: null };
  2072	   101	    case LOOP_EVENT.SMOKE_AWAIT:
  2073	   102	      return { ...state, status: LOOP_STATUS.AWAITING_SMOKE_APPROVAL };
  2074	   103	    case LOOP_EVENT.SMOKE_APPROVE: {
  2075	   104	      // Guarded transition: only meaningful while awaiting, and only with the
  2076	   105	      // caller's verifySmokeToken result stamped verified === true. A missing
  2077	   106	      // or false `verified` is unverified — it never resumes the run.
  2078	   107	      if (state.status !== LOOP_STATUS.AWAITING_SMOKE_APPROVAL) return state;
  2079	   108	      if (ev.verified !== true) return state;
  2080	   109	      return { ...state, status: LOOP_STATUS.RUNNING, smokeApproval: { token: ev.token, at: ev.ts ?? null } };
  2081	   110	    }
  2082	   111	    default:
  2083	   112	      return state;
  2084	   113	  }
  2085	   114	}
  2086	   115	
  2087	   116	/**
  2088	   117	 * Atomic snapshot write (tmp + rename under .handoff/loop).
  2089	   118	 * @param {string} root @param {any} state @param {any} io
  2090	   119	 */
  2091	   120	export async function writeLoopState(root, state, io) {
  2092	   121	  const p = loopPaths(root);
  2093	   122	  ensureDir(io.fs, p.dir);
  2094	   123	  atomicWriteJson(io.fs, p.state, state);
  2095	   124	}
  2096	   125	
  2097	   126	/**
  2098	   127	 * Append one journal event; allocates the next seq past
  2099	   128	 * max(snapshot.journalSeq, journal tail) unless the entry carries one.
  2100	   129	 * @param {string} root @param {any} ev @param {any} io
  2101	   130	 * @returns {Promise<number>} the persisted seq
  2102	   131	 */
  2103	   132	export async function appendLoopEvent(root, ev, io) {
  2104	   133	  const p = loopPaths(root);
  2105	   134	  ensureDir(io.fs, p.dir);
  2106	   135	  let seq = ev.seq;
  2107	   136	  if (typeof seq !== 'number') {
  2108	   137	    const snap = safeReadJson(io.fs, p.state);
  2109	   138	    const snapSeq = snap.ok && typeof snap.value?.journalSeq === 'number' ? snap.value.journalSeq : 0;
  2110	   139	    let tailSeq = 0;
  2111	   140	    if (io.fs.existsSync(p.journal)) {
  2112	   141	      for (const e of readAllTolerant(io.fs, p.journal).entries) {
  2113	   142	        if (typeof e.seq === 'number' && e.seq > tailSeq) tailSeq = e.seq;
  2114	   143	      }
  2115	   144	    }
  2116	   145	    seq = Math.max(snapSeq, tailSeq) + 1;
  2117	   146	  }
  2118	   147	  const ts = typeof ev.ts === 'string' ? ev.ts : io.now();
  2119	   148	  const entry = {
  2120	   149	    ...ev,
  2121	   150	    seq,
  2122	   151	    ts,
  2123	   152	    dedupeKey: typeof ev.dedupeKey === 'string' && ev.dedupeKey.length > 0 ? ev.dedupeKey : dedupeKey({ seq, ts, type: ev.type, gate: ev.gate ?? null }),
  2124	   153	  };
  2125	   154	  appendEntry(io.fs, p.journal, entry);
  2126	   155	  return seq;
  2127	   156	}
  2128	   157	
  2129	   158	/**
  2130	   159	 * Load = snapshot + torn-tail-tolerant replay of journal events PAST the
  2131	   160	 * snapshot's seq. Duplicate seqs/dedupeKeys apply exactly once, so a
  2132	   161	 * supervisor-death recovery never re-runs a completed iteration.
  2133	   162	 * @param {string} root @param {any} io
  2134	   163	 * @returns {Promise<{state: any, warnings: string[]}>}
  2135	   164	 */
  2136	   165	export async function loadLoopState(root, io) {
  2137	   166	  const p = loopPaths(root);
  2138	   167	  /** @type {string[]} */

  2281	   114	/**
  2282	   115	 * Acquire the supervisor run lock ATOMICALLY (exclusive create — never
  2283	   116	 * check-then-write, which loses a race by clobbering the winner). A live
  2284	   117	 * owner refuses; a provably-dead one is reclaimed: its recorded in-flight
  2285	   118	 * child groups (.handoff/loop/children.ndjson) are KILLED first so no
  2286	   119	 * orphan survives the takeover, then the lock is retried once.
  2287	   120	 * Shared by `loop run` and `pipeline run`.
  2288	   121	 * @param {string} root @param {any} io @param {string} cmdLabel
  2289	   122	 * @returns {Promise<{ok: true, release: () => void} | {ok: false, code: number}>}
  2290	   123	 */
  2291	   124	export async function acquireSupervisorLock(root, io, cmdLabel) {
  2292	   125	  const p = loopPaths(root);
  2293	   126	  const lockPath = `${p.dir}/supervisor.lock`;
  2294	   127	  const childrenPath = `${p.dir}/children.ndjson`;
  2295	   128	  ensureDir(io.fs, p.dir);
  2296	   129	  const payload = JSON.stringify({ host: io.host, pid: io.pid, startTime: io.startTime, runId: `sup-${io.pid}` });
  2297	   130	
  2298	   131	  const tryExclusive = () => {
  2299	   132	    // Fast-path an already-present lock (fakes without O_EXCL semantics rely
  2300	   133	    // on this); the wx flag below is the REAL exclusivity on a POSIX fs — a
  2301	   134	    // competitor that lands between the check and the write throws EEXIST
  2302	   135	    // instead of being clobbered.
  2303	   136	    if (io.fs.existsSync(lockPath)) return false;
  2304	   137	    try {
  2305	   138	      io.fs.writeFileSync(lockPath, payload, { flag: 'wx' });
  2306	   139	      return true;
  2307	   140	    } catch (err) {
  2308	   141	      if (/** @type {any} */ (err)?.code === 'EEXIST') return false;
  2309	   142	      throw err;
  2310	   143	    }
  2311	   144	  };
  2312	   145	
  2313	   146	  for (let attempt = 1; attempt <= 2; attempt += 1) {
  2314	   147	    if (tryExclusive()) {
  2315	   148	      // A fresh acquisition owns a fresh child registry: prior invocations'
  2316	   149	      // start/retire records are truncated so a stale retire can never mask a
  2317	   150	      // later in-flight child that reuses the same childId (I2).
  2318	   151	      try {
  2319	   152	        if (io.fs.existsSync(childrenPath)) io.fs.unlinkSync(childrenPath);
  2320	   153	      } catch {
  2321	   154	        // Best effort.
  2322	   155	      }
  2323	   156	      return {
  2324	   157	        ok: true,
  2325	   158	        release: () => {
  2326	   159	          try {
  2327	   160	            if (io.fs.existsSync(lockPath)) io.fs.unlinkSync(lockPath);
  2328	   161	          } catch {
  2329	   162	            // Best effort — a stale lock is recoverable via the dead path.
  2330	   163	          }
  2331	   164	        },
  2332	   165	      };
  2333	   166	    }
  2334	   167	    const existing = safeReadJson(io.fs, lockPath);
  2335	   168	    const other = existing.ok ? existing.value : null;
  2336	   169	    // Liveness verifies the (pid, startTime) PAIR — a recycled pid without the
  2337	   170	    // recorded start time is a dead owner, not a live supervisor (I5).
  2338	   171	    const alive = other && typeof other.pid === 'number' && typeof io.processAlive === 'function' ? io.processAlive(other.pid, other.startTime) : true;
  2339	   172	    const sameProcess = other && other.pid === io.pid && other.startTime === io.startTime;
  2340	   173	    if (!sameProcess && (!other || other.host !== io.host || alive)) {
  2341	   174	      io.stderr.write(`baton ${cmdLabel}: another supervisor (pid ${other?.pid ?? 'unknown'} on ${other?.host ?? 'unknown'}, run ${other?.runId ?? '?'}) holds the run lock — refusing a second supervisor\n`);
  2342	   175	      return { ok: false, code: 1 };
  2343	   176	    }
  2344	   177	    // Provably dead (or our own stale lock): reap its recorded in-flight
  2345	   178	    // child groups BEFORE anything else runs, then reclaim.
  2346	   179	    if (io.fs.existsSync(childrenPath)) {
  2347	   180	      const lines = String(io.fs.readFileSync(childrenPath, 'utf8')).split('\n').filter((l) => l.trim() !== '');
  2348	   181	      // Match retires to starts IN ORDER per childId — a resumed invocation
  2349	   182	      // reuses childIds, and a prior run's retire must never mask the newer
  2350	   183	      // in-flight start of the same id (I2). Whatever start remains unmatched
  2351	   184	      // is genuinely in-flight.
  2352	   185	      /** @type {Map<string, any[]>} */
  2353	   186	      const inflight = new Map();
  2354	   187	      for (const line of lines) {
  2355	   188	        try {
  2356	   189	          const rec = JSON.parse(line);
  2357	   190	          if (typeof rec?.endedAt === 'string') inflight.get(rec.childId)?.shift();
  2358	   191	          else if (typeof rec?.pgid === 'number') {
  2359	   192	            if (!inflight.has(rec.childId)) inflight.set(rec.childId, []);
  2360	   193	            inflight.get(rec.childId)?.push(rec);
  2361	   194	          }
  2362	   195	        } catch {
  2363	   196	          // A torn record is unreapable — skip it.
  2364	   197	        }
  2365	   198	      }
  2366	   199	      // Kill ONLY genuinely in-flight children (start with no matching
  2367	   200	      // retire): a retired child's pgid may have been recycled by an
  2368	   201	      // unrelated live process — killing it would be a pid-reuse casualty (H3).
  2369	   202	      for (const recs of inflight.values()) {
  2370	   203	        for (const rec of recs) {
  2371	   204	          if (typeof io.processAlive === 'function' && io.processAlive(rec.pgid)) {
  2372	   205	            if (typeof io.processKill === 'function') io.processKill(-rec.pgid, 'SIGKILL');
  2373	   206	          }
  2374	   207	        }
  2375	   208	      }
  2376	   209	      try {
  2377	   210	        io.fs.unlinkSync(childrenPath);
  2378	   211	      } catch {
  2379	   212	        // Best effort.
  2380	   213	      }
  2381	   214	    }
  2382	   215	    io.stderr.write(`baton ${cmdLabel}: reclaimed the run lock from a provably-dead supervisor (pid ${other?.pid})\n`);
  2383	   216	    try {
  2384	   217	      io.fs.unlinkSync(lockPath);
  2385	   218	    } catch {
  2386	   219	      // Already gone.
  2387	   220	    }
  2388	   221	  }
  2389	   222	  io.stderr.write(`baton ${cmdLabel}: could not acquire the run lock after reclaiming — another supervisor keeps winning\n`);
  2390	   223	  return { ok: false, code: 1 };
  2391	   224	}
  2392	   225	
  2393	   226	/**
  2394	   227	 * The supervisor state machine: drives the spec's phases through headless
  2395	   228	 * role children, records every transition in the journal-replayable loop
  2396	   229	 * state, enforces the 5-cap, pauses at the smoke gate, and hands limit
  2397	   230	 * deaths to the failover transaction. Children run via the injectable seam
  2398	   231	 * `io.superviseChild ?? superviseChild`.
  2399	   232	 * @param {Record<string, string | boolean>} flags @param {any} io
  2400	   233	 * @returns {Promise<number>}
  2401	   234	 */
  2402	   235	async function runLoop(flags, io) {
  2403	   236	  const root = resolveRoot(io, flags);
  2404	   237	  const specPath = `${root}/loop.json`;
  2405	   238	  const p = loopPaths(root);
  2406	   239	  const lockPath = `${p.dir}/supervisor.lock`;
  2407	   240	
  2408	   241	  // Preconditions — before any lock or spawn.
  2409	   242	  const rawSpec = safeReadJson(io.fs, specPath);
  2410	   243	  if (!rawSpec.ok) {
  2411	   244	    return usageError(io, flags, 'loop', `no loop.json at ${specPath} — scaffold one with: baton loop init "<goal>"`);
  2412	   245	  }
  2413	   246	  const { config } = loadConfig(root, io);

  2415	   248	  const validated = validateLoopSpec(rawSpec.value, config);
  2416	   249	  if (validated.ok !== true) {
  2417	   250	    return usageError(io, flags, 'loop', `loop.json is invalid:\n  - ${validated.errors.join('\n  - ')}`);
  2418	   251	  }
  2419	   252	  const spec = validated.spec;
  2420	   253	
  2421	   254	  // Supervisor run lock — atomic exclusive acquisition; provably-dead owners
  2422	   255	  // are reclaimed with their recorded child groups reaped first (B8 + G4).
  2423	   256	  const lock = await acquireSupervisorLock(root, io, 'loop run');
  2424	   257	  if (lock.ok !== true) return lock.code;
  2425	   258	
  2426	   259	  // Load or initialize the run state — stamped with {flavor, specDigest} so
  2427	   260	  // cross-flavor, changed-spec, or pre-stamp resumes refuse instead of
  2428	   261	  // misaligning (H5 + I3). These refusals release the lock explicitly — an
  2429	   262	  // early return here must not leave supervisor.lock behind (I4).
  2430	   263	  const specDigest = dedupeKey(spec.phases);
  2431	   264	  let { state } = await loadLoopState(root, io);
  2432	   265	  if (state === null) {
  2433	   266	    state = { ...initLoopState(spec, io), flavor: 'loop', specDigest };
  2434	   267	    await writeLoopState(root, state, io);
  2435	   268	  } else {
  2436	   269	    if (typeof state.flavor === 'string' && state.flavor !== 'loop') {
  2437	   270	      lock.release();
  2438	   271	      return usageError(io, flags, 'loop', `the persisted run state is flavor '${state.flavor}' — a ${state.flavor} run cannot be resumed as a loop (flavor mismatch); archive .handoff/loop or finish the ${state.flavor} run first`);
  2439	   272	    }
  2440	   273	    if (typeof state.flavor !== 'string' || typeof state.specDigest !== 'string') {
  2441	   274	      lock.release();
  2442	   275	      return usageError(io, flags, 'loop', 'the persisted run state is missing its {flavor, specDigest} stamp (pre-stamp legacy state) — resuming it could misalign the run; archive .handoff/loop to start fresh');
  2443	   276	    }
  2444	   277	    if (state.specDigest !== specDigest) {
  2445	   278	      lock.release();
  2446	   279	      return usageError(io, flags, 'loop', 'loop.json phases changed since this run started (spec digest mismatch) — resuming would misalign the phase position; archive .handoff/loop to start fresh');
  2447	   280	    }
  2448	   281	  }
  2449	   282	  const runId = state.runId;
  2450	   283	  // The lock payload's runId now correlates with the run it guards (H8).
  2451	   284	  io.fs.writeFileSync(`${p.dir}/supervisor.lock`, JSON.stringify({ host: io.host, pid: io.pid, startTime: io.startTime, runId }));
  2452	   285	
  2453	   286	  /** Apply one event, journal it, persist the snapshot — one transition. */
  2454	   287	  const transition = async (/** @type {any} */ ev) => {
  2455	   288	    const next = applyLoopEvent(state, ev);
  2456	   289	    const seq = await appendLoopEvent(root, ev, io);
  2457	   290	    state = { ...next, journalSeq: seq };
  2458	   291	    await writeLoopState(root, state, io);
  2459	   292	    return state;
  2460	   293	  };

  2463	   296	  const runner = io.superviseChild ?? superviseChild;
  2464	   297	  const sessionHint = `loop-${runId}`;

  2466	   299	  let avoid = [];
  2467	   300	  /** @type {Array<{platform: string, model: string}>} */
  2468	   301	  let avoidEntries = [];
  2469	   302	  let childSeq = 0;
  2470	   303	
  2471	   304	  try {
  2472	   305	    // `loop resume` — a PARK is resumable; an escalation is operator-only.
  2473	   306	    if (flags.__resume === true) {
  2474	   307	      if (state.status === LOOP_STATUS.ESCALATED) {
  2475	   308	        io.stderr.write(`baton loop resume: the run is ESCALATED (gate ${state.escalation?.gate}) — escalation is an operator decision; resolve the findings and start a fresh gate instead\n`);
  2476	   309	        return EXIT_ESCALATED;

  2478	   311	      if (state.status === LOOP_STATUS.PARKED) {
  2479	   312	        await transition({ type: LOOP_EVENT.RESUME });

  2481	   314	      }
  2482	   315	    }

  2487	     1	/**
  2488	     2	 * `baton pipeline run` — the Layer-3 dual-worktree preset over the loop
  2489	     3	 * engine. Subtasks alternate writer seats (index 0 → wt-a/worker-a, 1 →
  2490	     4	 * wt-b/worker-b, …); the reviewer runs READ-ONLY from the OTHER seat with

  2529	    43	 * @param {string[]} args @param {any} io
  2530	    44	 * @returns {Promise<number>}
  2531	    45	 */
  2532	    46	export async function cmdPipeline(args, io) {
  2533	    47	  /** @type {string[]} */
  2534	    48	  const positionals = [];
  2535	    49	  /** @type {string[]} */
  2536	    50	  const flagTokens = [];
  2537	    51	  for (let i = 0; i < args.length; i += 1) {
  2538	    52	    const a = args[i];
  2539	    53	    if (typeof a === 'string' && a.startsWith('--')) {
  2540	    54	      flagTokens.push(a);
  2541	    55	      if (STRING_FLAGS.has(a.slice(2)) && i + 1 < args.length) {
  2542	    56	        i += 1;
  2543	    57	        flagTokens.push(args[i]);
  2544	    58	      }
  2545	    59	    } else {
  2546	    60	      positionals.push(a);
  2547	    61	    }
  2548	    62	  }
  2549	    63	  const parsed = parseFlagsStrict(flagTokens, {});
  2550	    64	  if (parsed.error !== undefined) return usageError(io, parsed.flags, 'pipeline', parsed.error);
  2551	    65	  const flags = parsed.flags;
  2552	    66	
  2553	    67	  const sub = positionals[0];
  2554	    68	  if (sub === undefined) return usageError(io, flags, 'pipeline', 'a subcommand is required — try: baton pipeline run');
  2555	    69	  if (sub !== 'run' && sub !== 'resume') return usageError(io, flags, 'pipeline', `unknown subcommand '${sub}' (supported: run, resume)`);
  2556	    70	  if (positionals.length > 1) return usageError(io, flags, 'pipeline', `unexpected argument '${positionals[1]}'`);
  2557	    71	  if (sub === 'resume') flags.__resume = true;
  2558	    72	
  2559	    73	  return runPipeline(flags, io);
  2560	    74	}

  2562	    76	/** Validate the spec's subtasks — every rejection names its offender.
  2563	    77	 * @param {any} subtasks @returns {string | null} */
  2564	    78	function validateSubtasks(subtasks) {
  2565	    79	  if (!Array.isArray(subtasks) || subtasks.length === 0) {
  2566	    80	    return 'loop.json needs a non-empty `subtasks` array ([{id, title}]) for a pipeline run';
  2567	    81	  }
  2568	    82	  const seen = new Set();
  2569	    83	  for (let i = 0; i < subtasks.length; i += 1) {
  2570	    84	    const st = subtasks[i];
  2571	    85	    if (typeof st?.id !== 'string' || st.id.length === 0) return `subtasks[${i}] is missing a string id`;
  2572	    86	    if (typeof st?.title !== 'string' || st.title.length === 0) return `subtasks[${i}] ('${st.id}') is missing a string title`;
  2573	    87	    if (seen.has(st.id)) return `subtasks carry a duplicate id '${st.id}' — ids name branches and must be unique`;
  2574	    88	    seen.add(st.id);
  2575	    89	  }
  2576	    90	  return null;
  2577	    91	}
