  2580	    94	 * @param {Record<string, string | boolean>} flags @param {any} io
  2581	    95	 * @returns {Promise<number>}
  2582	    96	 */
  2583	    97	async function runPipeline(flags, io) {
  2584	    98	  const root = resolveRoot(io, flags);
  2585	    99	  const p = loopPaths(root);
  2586	   100	
  2587	   101	  const rawSpec = safeReadJson(io.fs, `${root}/loop.json`);
  2588	   102	  if (!rawSpec.ok) return usageError(io, flags, 'pipeline', `no loop.json at ${root} — scaffold one with: baton loop init "<goal>"`);
  2589	   103	  const spec = rawSpec.value;
  2590	   104	  const subtaskError = validateSubtasks(spec?.subtasks);
  2591	   105	  if (subtaskError !== null) return usageError(io, flags, 'pipeline', subtaskError);
  2592	   106	  const { config } = loadConfig(root, io);
  2593	   107	  if (!config) return usageError(io, flags, 'pipeline', `no readable baton.config.json at ${root} — the role matrix is required`);
  2594	   108	  // The 5-iteration cap is a hard invariant (Gate-2 fold G2): a spec asking
  2595	   109	  // for more is rejected BEFORE anything spawns.
  2596	   110	  const rawCap = spec?.budgets?.iterationCap;
  2597	   111	  if (rawCap !== undefined && (typeof rawCap !== 'number' || rawCap < 1 || rawCap > 5)) {
  2598	   112	    return usageError(io, flags, 'pipeline', `budgets.iterationCap must be a number between 1 and 5 (the 5-iteration cap is a hard invariant; got ${JSON.stringify(rawCap)})`);
  2599	   113	  }

  2601	   115	  const timeoutMs = (typeof spec?.budgets?.perRoleTimeoutMin === 'number' ? spec.budgets.perRoleTimeoutMin : 30) * 60_000;
  2602	   116	
  2603	   117	  // One supervisor at a time — the same atomic run lock as `loop run`, with
  2604	   118	  // dead-owner reclaim + orphan reaping (Gate-2 fold G2/B8).
  2605	   119	  const lock = await acquireSupervisorLock(root, io, 'pipeline run');
  2606	   120	  if (lock.ok !== true) return lock.code;

  2609	   123	  } finally {
  2610	   124	    lock.release();
  2611	   125	  }
  2612	   126	}

  2757	    54	/**
  2758	    55	 * The --json contract: exactly one compact envelope line on stdout, nothing else.
  2759	    56	 * @param {any} io
  2760	    57	 * @param {{ok: boolean, data?: any, warnings?: any[], error?: {code: string, msg: string} | null}} env
  2761	    58	 */
  2762	    59	function emitEnvelope(io, env) {
  2763	    60	  const full = { ok: env.ok, data: env.data ?? null, warnings: env.warnings ?? [], error: env.error ?? null };
  2764	    61	  io.stdout.write(JSON.stringify(full) + '\n');
  2765	    62	}

  2767	    64	/**
  2768	    65	 * @param {string[]} argv process args after the binary
  2769	    66	 * @param {any} io injected {cwd, stdout, stderr}
  2770	    67	 * @returns {number | Promise<number>} exit code
  2771	    68	 */
  2772	    69	export function run(argv, io) {
  2773	    70	  const args = argv.filter((a) => a !== '');
  2774	    71	  // Global --json intent is parsed BEFORE dispatch (gate-2 iter-2 M6): a
  2775	    72	  // top-level `--json` with no command, or --help/--version, must still emit
  2776	    73	  // exactly one envelope rather than raw text.
  2777	    74	  const jsonWanted = args.includes('--json');
  2778	    75	  const cmdIndex = args.findIndex((a) => !a.startsWith('--'));
  2779	    76	  const cmd = cmdIndex === -1 ? undefined : args[cmdIndex];
  2780	    77	
  2781	    78	  if (cmd === undefined) {
  2782	    79	    // No command — just flags (or nothing).
  2783	    80	    if (args.includes('--version')) {
  2784	    81	      if (jsonWanted) emitEnvelope(io, { ok: true, data: { version: version() } });
  2785	    82	      else io.stdout.write(version() + '\n');
  2786	    83	      return 0;
  2787	    84	    }
  2788	    85	    if (args.includes('--help')) {
  2789	    86	      if (jsonWanted) emitEnvelope(io, { ok: true, data: { usage: USAGE.trim() } });
  2790	    87	      else io.stdout.write(USAGE);
  2791	    88	      return 0;
  2792	    89	    }
  2793	    90	    // Bare invocation (or a lone --json): a usage error.
  2794	    91	    if (jsonWanted) emitEnvelope(io, { ok: false, error: { code: 'usage', msg: 'no command given' } });
  2795	    92	    else io.stdout.write(USAGE);
  2796	    93	    return 2;
  2797	    94	  }
  2798	    95	
  2799	    96	  // All flags (before and after the command token) reach the command; only the
  2800	    97	  // command token itself is stripped, so a leading `--json` isn't lost.
  2801	    98	  const rest = args.filter((_, i) => i !== cmdIndex);
  2802	    99	
  2803	   100	  // Usage errors honor the --json envelope contract too (gate-2 fix 11 + M6:
  2804	   101	  // global --json counts, wherever it sits).
  2805	   102	  const wantsJson = jsonWanted;
  2806	   103	  if (cmd === 'wrap') {
  2807	   104	    if (wantsJson) emitEnvelope(io, { ok: false, error: { code: 'usage', msg: 'baton wrap is reserved for a future release (PTY supervisor); not available in v1' } });
  2808	   105	    else io.stderr.write(`baton wrap is reserved for a future release (PTY supervisor); not available in v1.\n${USAGE}`);
  2809	   106	    return 2;
  2810	   107	  }
  2811	   108	  if (!COMMANDS.includes(cmd)) {
  2812	   109	    if (wantsJson) emitEnvelope(io, { ok: false, error: { code: 'usage', msg: `unknown command '${cmd}'` } });
  2813	   110	    else io.stderr.write(`baton: unknown command '${cmd}'\n${USAGE}`);
  2814	   111	    return 2;
  2815	   112	  }
  2816	   113	
  2817	   114	  if (rest.includes('--help')) {

  2819	   116	    // --json it must emit exactly one envelope, never raw usage text.
  2820	   117	    const usage = `usage: baton ${cmd} [options]\n\nSee README for the ${cmd} contract.`;
  2821	   118	    if (wantsJson) emitEnvelope(io, { ok: true, data: { usage } });
  2822	   119	    else io.stdout.write(`${usage}\n`);
  2823	   120	    return 0;

  3006	    96	 * next result off the queue, writes its logContent to opts.logPath (so the
  3007	    97	 * command reads the transcript from the child's LOG file), and records the
  3008	    98	 * spawn spec.
  3009	    99	 */
  3010	   100	function fakeRunner(io, script) {
  3011	   101	  const queue = [...script];
  3012	   102	  const calls = /** @type {any[]} */ ([]);
  3013	   103	  const fn = (/** @type {any} */ spec, /** @type {any} */ opts = {}) => {
  3014	   104	    calls.push({ spec, opts, args: spec?.args ?? [], command: spec?.command, env: spec?.env });
  3015	   105	    const r = queue.shift() ?? { timedOut: false, exitCode: 0, verdict: 'APPROVED', findings: '' };
  3016	   106	    // No fallback: the command MUST pass a real logPath (asserted below).
  3017	   107	    const logPath = opts.logPath;
  3018	   108	    if (typeof r.logContent === 'string') {
  3019	   109	      const dir = logPath.slice(0, logPath.lastIndexOf('/'));
  3020	   110	      io.fs.mkdirSync(dir, { recursive: true });
  3021	   111	      io.fs.writeFileSync(logPath, r.logContent);
  3022	   112	    }
  3023	   113	    return Promise.resolve({ timedOut: r.timedOut ?? false, exitCode: r.exitCode ?? 0, verdict: r.verdict ?? 'APPROVED', findings: r.findings ?? '', logPath });
  3024	   114	  };
  3025	   115	  fn.calls = calls;
  3026	   116	  return fn;
  3027	   117	}

  3127	   214	    }

    99	    git: null,
   100	    handoff:
   101	      status === 'sealed'
   102	        ? { status: 'sealed', reason: 'seal', reasonClass: 'usage-limit', toPlatformHint: null, finalizedAt: T0, receive_log: [] }
   103	        : { status: 'open', reason: null, reasonClass: null, toPlatformHint: null, finalizedAt: null, receive_log: [] },
   104	    journalSeq: 0,
   105	    compaction: { droppedDecisions: 0, note: null },
   106	    dedupeRing: [],
   107	  };
   108	}
   109	
   110	/**
   111	 * A STRICT, stateful fake git: unstubbed commands reject; worktree add/list and
   112	 * checkout -b are modeled so setup + preflight/postflight + merge cohere.
   113	 * `over` overrides specific responses (e.g. a merge conflict).
   114	 */
   115	function pipelineGit({ over = {}, log = CLEAN_LOG, existingBranches = [], dirtyCwds = [], mergedBranches = [] } = {}) {
   116	  const added = /** @type {Map<string,string>} */ (new Map()); // path -> branch
   117	  const branchAt = /** @type {Map<string,string>} */ (new Map()); // cwd -> current branch
   118	  const branches = new Set(existingBranches); // stale/pre-existing namespaced branches
   119	  const dirty = new Set(dirtyCwds); // seat cwds whose index is dirty
   120	  const deleted = new Set(); // seats listed by git but raw-deleted on disk
   121	  const merged = new Set(mergedBranches); // branches already merged into main (ancestor; empty main..branch)
   122	  const calls = /** @type {any[]} */ ([]);
   123	  const lockProbeRef = { fn: /** @type {null | (() => boolean)} */ (null) };
   124	  let mainSha = MAIN_SHA; // mutable so a child can "move main" mid-subtask
   125	
   126	  const list = () => {
   127	    const entries = [{ path: '/repo', branch: 'main' }];
   128	    for (const [path, branch] of added) entries.push({ path, branch: branchAt.get(path) ?? branch });
   129	    return entries.map((e) => `worktree ${e.path}\nHEAD ${MAIN_SHA}\nbranch refs/heads/${e.branch}\n`).join('\n');
   130	  };
   131	
   132	  const fn = (/** @type {string} */ cmd, /** @type {string[]} */ args = [], /** @type {any} */ opts = {}) => {
   133	    const argstr = args.join(' ');
   134	    calls.push({ cmd, args, argstr, cwd: opts?.cwd, lockPresent: lockProbeRef.fn ? lockProbeRef.fn() : undefined });
   135	    if (cmd !== 'git') return Promise.reject(Object.assign(new Error('spawn ENOENT'), { code: 'ENOENT' }));
   136	
   137	    // Explicit overrides win.
   138	    const okey = Object.keys(over).sort((a, b) => b.length - a.length).find((k) => argstr.includes(k));
   139	    if (okey) {
   140	      const r = over[okey];
   141	      if (r?.reject) return Promise.reject(Object.assign(new Error(r.stderr || 'git failed'), { code: r.code ?? 1, stdout: r.stdout ?? '', stderr: r.stderr ?? '' }));
   142	      return Promise.resolve({ stdout: r?.stdout ?? '', stderr: r?.stderr ?? '' });
   143	    }
   144	
   145	    // A raw-deleted seat: git still LISTS it, but any command IN that cwd fails
   146	    // (the directory is gone). Only self-heal (prune + re-add) recovers it.
   147	    if (opts?.cwd && deleted.has(opts.cwd) && !argstr.startsWith('worktree ')) {
   148	      return Promise.reject(Object.assign(new Error(`fatal: cannot chdir to '${opts.cwd}': No such file or directory`), { code: 128, stderr: 'No such file or directory\n' }));
   149	    }
   150	    if (argstr.startsWith('worktree list')) return Promise.resolve({ stdout: list(), stderr: '' });
   151	    if (argstr.startsWith('worktree add')) {
   152	      const path = args.find((a) => a.startsWith('/repo/.worktrees/'));
   153	      const bi = args.indexOf('-b');
   154	      const branch = bi >= 0 ? args[bi + 1] : 'baton/wt-x/base';
   155	      if (path) { added.set(path, branch); branchAt.set(path, branch); deleted.delete(path); }
   156	      return Promise.resolve({ stdout: '', stderr: '' });
   157	    }
   158	    if (argstr.startsWith('worktree prune')) { deleted.clear(); return Promise.resolve({ stdout: '', stderr: '' }); }
   159	    if (argstr.startsWith('worktree remove')) return Promise.resolve({ stdout: '', stderr: '' });
   160	    if (argstr.startsWith('checkout -b')) {
   161	      const target = args[args.indexOf('-b') + 1];
   162	      if (branches.has(target)) return Promise.reject(Object.assign(new Error(`fatal: a branch named '${target}' already exists`), { code: 128, stderr: `fatal: a branch named '${target}' already exists\n` }));
   163	      branches.add(target);
   164	      if (opts?.cwd) branchAt.set(opts.cwd, target);
   165	      return Promise.resolve({ stdout: '', stderr: '' });
   166	    }
   167	    if (argstr.startsWith('status --porcelain')) return Promise.resolve({ stdout: dirty.has(opts?.cwd) ? ' M dirty.txt\n' : '', stderr: '' });
   168	    if (argstr === 'rev-parse --abbrev-ref HEAD') return Promise.resolve({ stdout: `${branchAt.get(opts?.cwd) ?? 'main'}\n`, stderr: '' });
   169	    if (argstr.startsWith('rev-parse')) return Promise.resolve({ stdout: `${mainSha}\n`, stderr: '' });
   170	    // merge-base --is-ancestor <branch> main : exit 0 iff <branch> is already in main.
   171	    if (argstr.startsWith('merge-base --is-ancestor')) {
   172	      const b = args[args.indexOf('--is-ancestor') + 1];
   173	      return merged.has(b) ? Promise.resolve({ stdout: '', stderr: '' }) : Promise.reject(Object.assign(new Error('not an ancestor'), { code: 1, stdout: '', stderr: '' }));
   174	    }
   175	    if (argstr.startsWith('log ')) {
   176	      // A merged branch has NO commits ahead of main (empty main..<branch>).
   177	      const range = args.find((a) => /\.\./.test(String(a))) ?? '';
   178	      const branch = String(range).split('..')[1];
   179	      if (branch && merged.has(branch)) return Promise.resolve({ stdout: '', stderr: '' });
   180	      return Promise.resolve({ stdout: log, stderr: '' });
   181	    }
   182	    if (argstr.startsWith('merge')) return Promise.resolve({ stdout: 'Fast-forward', stderr: '' });
   183	    if (argstr.startsWith('branch -D')) return Promise.resolve({ stdout: '', stderr: '' });
   184	    return Promise.reject(Object.assign(new Error(`unstubbed git: ${argstr}`), { code: 'ENOSTUB' }));
   185	  };
   186	  fn.calls = calls;
   187	  fn.git = () => calls.filter((c) => c.cmd === 'git');
   188	  fn.issued = (/** @type {RegExp} */ re) => calls.some((c) => c.cmd === 'git' && re.test(c.argstr));
   189	  fn.matching = (/** @type {RegExp} */ re) => calls.filter((c) => c.cmd === 'git' && re.test(c.argstr));
   190	  fn.withCwd = (/** @type {RegExp} */ re, /** @type {string} */ cwd) => calls.filter((c) => c.cmd === 'git' && re.test(c.argstr) && c.cwd === cwd);
   191	  fn.setLockProbe = (/** @type {() => boolean} */ f) => { lockProbeRef.fn = f; };
   192	  fn.moveMain = (/** @type {string} */ sha) => { mainSha = sha; }; // a child "moves main" mid-subtask
   193	  fn.setBranch = (/** @type {string} */ cwd, /** @type {string} */ branch) => { branchAt.set(cwd, branch); };
   194	  // Explicit seed: git LISTS this worktree; `raw` marks its dir as deleted on
   195	  // disk (any in-cwd command fails until prune + re-add self-heals it).
   196	  fn.seedWorktree = (/** @type {string} */ path, /** @type {string} */ branch, /** @type {{raw?: boolean}} */ o = {}) => {
   197	    added.set(path, branch);
   198	    branchAt.set(path, branch);
   199	    if (o.raw) deleted.add(path);
   200	  };
   201	  return fn;
   202	}
   203	
   204	function fakeRunner(io, script) {
   205	  const queue = [...script];
   206	  const calls = /** @type {any[]} */ ([]);
   207	  const fn = (/** @type {any} */ spec, /** @type {any} */ opts = {}) => {
   208	    calls.push({ spec, opts, command: spec?.command, args: spec?.args ?? [], env: spec?.env });
   209	    const r = queue.shift() ?? { verdict: 'APPROVED' };
   210	    if (typeof r.logContent === 'string' && opts.logPath) {
   211	      const dir = opts.logPath.slice(0, opts.logPath.lastIndexOf('/'));
   212	      io.fs.mkdirSync(dir, { recursive: true });
   213	      io.fs.writeFileSync(opts.logPath, r.logContent);
   214	    }
   215	    // A child may perturb the world (e.g. move main) between spawn and postflight.
   216	    if (typeof r.effect === 'function') r.effect();
   217	    return Promise.resolve({ timedOut: false, exitCode: r.exitCode ?? 0, verdict: r.verdict ?? 'APPROVED', findings: r.findings ?? '', logPath: opts.logPath });
   218	  };
   219	  fn.calls = calls;
   220	  return fn;
   221	}
   222	
   223	function makePipeRepo({ spec = pipelineSpec(), config = PIPELINE_CONFIG, files = {}, git, runner, env = {} } = {}) {
   224	  const io = makeIo({
   225	    now: T0,
   226	    host: 'pipe-host',
   227	    pid: 4242,
   228	    startTime: 111000,
   229	    env,
   230	    files: {
   231	      [SIG_ABS]: SIG_CONTENT,
   232	      '/repo/.git/HEAD': 'ref: refs/heads/main\n',
   233	      '/repo/baton.config.json': JSON.stringify(config, null, 2),
   234	      ...(spec ? { '/repo/loop.json': JSON.stringify(spec, null, 2) + '\n' } : {}),
   235	      ...files,
   236	    },
   237	  });
   238	  const g = git ?? pipelineGit();
   239	  g.setLockProbe(() => io.fs.existsSync(MERGE_LOCK));
   240	  io.execFile = g;
   241	  const fake = runner ?? fakeRunner(io, []);
   242	  io.superviseChild = fake;
   243	  io.__git = g;
   244	  io.__runner = fake;
   245	  return io;
   246	}
   247	
   248	// A full clean subtask = writer APPROVED, reviewer APPROVED, merger APPROVED.
   249	const cleanSubtask = () => [{ verdict: 'APPROVED' }, { verdict: 'APPROVED' }, { verdict: 'APPROVED' }];
   250	const argsOf = (call) => (call?.args ?? []).map(String);
   251	const valAfter = (arr, flag) => { const i = arr.indexOf(flag); return i >= 0 ? arr[i + 1] : undefined; };
   252	const childWithModel = (runner, model) => runner.calls.filter((c) => argsOf(c).includes(model));
   253	// Writers = the write-capable children (in call order). Reviewers = read-only
   254	// children whose prompt carries the 'subtask-reviewer' role identity (so the
   255	// merger, also read-only, is excluded).
   256	const writersOf = (runner) => runner.calls.filter((c) => valAfter(argsOf(c), '-s') === 'workspace-write');
   257	const reviewersOf = (runner) => runner.calls.filter((c) => valAfter(argsOf(c), '-s') === 'read-only' && argsOf(c).join(' ').includes('subtask-reviewer'));
   258	
   259	// ===========================================================================
   260	describe('pipeline — CLI routing & flags', () => {
   261	  it('no subcommand -> usage error (exit 2) naming a subcommand, not "unknown command"', async () => {
   262	    const io = makePipeRepo();
   263	    const code = await run(['pipeline'], io);
   264	    assert.equal(code, 2);
   265	    const out = io.stderrText() + io.stdoutText();
   266	    assert.match(out, /subcommand|run/i, 'the error guides toward a subcommand');
   267	    assert.doesNotMatch(out, /unknown command/i, 'pipeline IS a known command');
   268	  });
   269	
   270	  it('an unknown subcommand exits 2 naming it', async () => {
   271	    const io = makePipeRepo();
   272	    const code = await run(['pipeline', 'frobnicate'], io);
   273	    assert.equal(code, 2);
   274	    assert.match(io.stderrText() + io.stdoutText(), /frobnicate/, 'the error names the bad subcommand');
   275	  });
   276	
   277	  it('a stray positional / unknown flag exits 2 with zero spawns', async () => {
   278	    const io1 = makePipeRepo();
   279	    assert.equal(await run(['pipeline', 'run', 'stray'], io1), 2);
   280	    assert.equal(io1.__runner.calls.length, 0, 'a garbled invocation spawns nothing');
   281	    const io2 = makePipeRepo();
   282	    assert.equal(await run(['pipeline', 'run', '--no-such-flag'], io2), 2);
   283	    assert.equal(io2.__runner.calls.length, 0, 'an unknown flag spawns nothing');
   284	  });
   285	
   286	  it('BATON_SUPERVISED_CHILD does NOT suppress pipeline run (supervisor-side)', async () => {
   287	    const io = makePipeRepo({ env: { BATON_SUPERVISED_CHILD: '1' }, runner: undefined });
   288	    io.superviseChild = fakeRunner(io, [...cleanSubtask(), ...cleanSubtask()]);
   289	    io.__runner = io.superviseChild;
   290	    const code = await run(['pipeline', 'run'], io);
   291	    assert.equal(code, 0, 'the guard is child-only; the supervisor command still runs');
   292	    assert.ok(io.__runner.calls.length >= 1, 'children spawned under the guard env');
   293	  });
   294	});
   295	
   296	// ===========================================================================
   297	describe('pipeline — preconditions', () => {
   298	  it('no loop.json -> exit 2 naming loop.json, zero spawns', async () => {
   299	    const io = makePipeRepo({ spec: null });
   300	    const code = await run(['pipeline', 'run'], io);
   301	    assert.equal(code, 2);
   302	    const out = io.stderrText() + io.stdoutText();
   303	    assert.match(out, /loop\.json/i, 'the error names the missing spec file');
   304	    assert.doesNotMatch(out, /unknown command/i, 'pipeline is routed — the error is about the missing spec, not the command');
   305	    assert.equal(io.__runner.calls.length, 0, 'no child spawned');
   306	  });
   307	
   308	  it('missing / empty subtasks -> exit 2 naming subtasks, zero spawns', async () => {
   309	    for (const spec of [pipelineSpec({ subtasks: undefined }), pipelineSpec({ subtasks: [] })]) {
   310	      const io = makePipeRepo({ spec });
   311	      const code = await run(['pipeline', 'run'], io);
   312	      assert.equal(code, 2, 'a pipeline needs subtasks');
   313	      assert.match(io.stderrText() + io.stdoutText(), /subtasks/i, 'the error names subtasks');
   314	      assert.equal(io.__runner.calls.length, 0, 'no child spawned without subtasks');
   315	    }
   316	  });
   317	
   318	  it('malformed subtask items exit 2 naming the offender, with zero spawns', async () => {
   319	    const CASES = [
   320	      ['missing id', [{ title: 'no id' }], /id/i],
   321	      ['missing title', [{ id: 't1' }], /title/i],
   322	      ['non-string id', [{ id: 7, title: 'ok' }], /id/i],
   323	      ['non-string title', [{ id: 't1', title: 5 }], /title/i],
   324	      ['duplicate ids', [{ id: 't1', title: 'a' }, { id: 't1', title: 'b' }], /duplicate|t1/i],
   325	    ];
   326	    for (const [label, subtasks, needle] of CASES) {
   327	      const io = makePipeRepo({ spec: pipelineSpec({ subtasks }) });
   328	      const code = await run(['pipeline', 'run'], io);
   329	      assert.equal(code, 2, `${label} is a usage error`);
   330	      assert.match(io.stderrText() + io.stdoutText(), needle, `${label}: the error names the offender`);
   331	      assert.equal(io.__runner.calls.length, 0, `${label}: zero spawns`);
   332	    }
   333	  });
   334	
   335	  it('setupWorktrees runs before subtask 1 (worktree adds recorded) and only once', async () => {
   336	    const io = makePipeRepo({ runner: undefined });
   337	    io.superviseChild = fakeRunner(io, [...cleanSubtask(), ...cleanSubtask()]);
   338	    io.__runner = io.superviseChild;
   339	    await run(['pipeline', 'run'], io);
   340	    const adds = io.__git.matching(/^worktree add/);
   341	    assert.equal(adds.length, 2, 'both seat worktrees are added exactly once, before the subtask cycle');
   342	    assert.ok(adds.some((c) => c.argstr.includes(WT_A)) && adds.some((c) => c.argstr.includes(WT_B)), 'wt-a and wt-b are set up');
   343	  });
   344	});
   345	
   346	// ===========================================================================
   347	describe('pipeline — seat alternation (writer)', () => {
   348	  it('subtask 1 writes in wt-a on baton/wt-a/subtask-t1 (worker-a model); subtask 2 writes in wt-b on baton/wt-b/subtask-t2 (worker-b model)', async () => {
   349	    const io = makePipeRepo({ runner: undefined });
   350	    io.superviseChild = fakeRunner(io, [...cleanSubtask(), ...cleanSubtask()]);
   351	    io.__runner = io.superviseChild;
   352	    const code = await run(['pipeline', 'run'], io);
   353	    assert.equal(code, 0);
   354	
   355	    // Branch creation recorded in each seat's own cwd.
   356	    assert.equal(io.__git.withCwd(/^checkout -b baton\/wt-a\/subtask-t1$/, WT_A).length, 1, 'subtask 1 branch created in wt-a');
   357	    assert.equal(io.__git.withCwd(/^checkout -b baton\/wt-b\/subtask-t2$/, WT_B).length, 1, 'subtask 2 branch created in wt-b');
   358	
   359	    // The WRITERS are exactly the two write-capable children, in subtask order —
   360	    // isolating by -s workspace-write means a read-only reviewer can never
   361	    // satisfy this (finding 1).
   362	    const writers = writersOf(io.__runner);
   363	    assert.equal(writers.length, 2, 'exactly two writer children (one per subtask)');
   364	    assert.equal(valAfter(argsOf(writers[0]), '--model'), 'wa-model', 'subtask 1 writer uses the worker-a model');
   365	    assert.equal(valAfter(argsOf(writers[0]), '-C'), WT_A, 'subtask 1 writer works in wt-a');
   366	    assert.equal(valAfter(argsOf(writers[1]), '--model'), 'wb-model', 'subtask 2 writer uses the worker-b model');
   367	    assert.equal(valAfter(argsOf(writers[1]), '-C'), WT_B, 'subtask 2 writer works in wt-b');
   368	  });
   369	});
   370	
   371	// ===========================================================================
   372	describe('pipeline — reviewer seat (read-only, other seat)', () => {
   373	  it("both reviewers, in order: t1 = wb-model/WT_B/read-only, t2 = wa-model/WT_A/read-only, each under the subtask-reviewer role", async () => {
   374	    const io = makePipeRepo({ runner: undefined });
   375	    io.superviseChild = fakeRunner(io, [...cleanSubtask(), ...cleanSubtask()]);
   376	    io.__runner = io.superviseChild;
   377	    await run(['pipeline', 'run'], io);
   378	
   379	    const reviewers = reviewersOf(io.__runner);
   380	    assert.equal(reviewers.length, 2, 'exactly two reviewer children (one per subtask), provable via the subtask-reviewer role identity in the prompt');
   381	    // t1 reviewer: OTHER seat is b -> wb-model in wt-b.
   382	    assert.equal(valAfter(argsOf(reviewers[0]), '--model'), 'wb-model', 'subtask 1 reviewer carries the OTHER seat (worker-b) model');
   383	    assert.equal(valAfter(argsOf(reviewers[0]), '-C'), WT_B, 'subtask 1 reviewer reviews from wt-b');
   384	    assert.equal(valAfter(argsOf(reviewers[0]), '-s'), 'read-only', 'subtask 1 reviewer cannot write');
   385	    // t2 reviewer: OTHER seat is a -> wa-model in wt-a.
   386	    assert.equal(valAfter(argsOf(reviewers[1]), '--model'), 'wa-model', 'subtask 2 reviewer carries the OTHER seat (worker-a) model');
   387	    assert.equal(valAfter(argsOf(reviewers[1]), '-C'), WT_A, 'subtask 2 reviewer reviews from wt-a');
   388	    assert.equal(valAfter(argsOf(reviewers[1]), '-s'), 'read-only', 'subtask 2 reviewer cannot write');
   389	  });
   390	
   391	  it('RED (G7): the reviewer prompt instructs the git RANGE review (git diff main..baton/wt-<seat>/subtask-<id>)', async () => {
   392	    const io = makePipeRepo({ spec: pipelineSpec({ subtasks: [{ id: 't1', title: 'only' }] }), runner: undefined });
   393	    io.superviseChild = fakeRunner(io, cleanSubtask());
   394	    io.__runner = io.superviseChild;
   395	    await run(['pipeline', 'run'], io);
   396	    const rev = reviewersOf(io.__runner)[0];
   397	    assert.ok(rev, 'a reviewer child ran');
   398	    const prompt = argsOf(rev).join(' ');
   399	    // Refs are shared across worktrees, so the reviewer must be told to inspect
   400	    // the exact range with git (not left to guess "the diff").
   401	    assert.match(prompt, /git diff main\.\.baton\/wt-a\/subtask-t1/, 'the reviewer prompt names the exact `git diff main..<branch>` range to review');
   402	  });
   403	});
   404	
   405	// ===========================================================================
   406	describe('pipeline — verdict flow', () => {
   407	  it('reviewer APPROVED -> the MERGER child runs (merger model); on its APPROVED the SUPERVISOR merges via mergeSubtask', async () => {
   408	    const io = makePipeRepo({ runner: undefined });
   409	    io.superviseChild = fakeRunner(io, [...cleanSubtask(), ...cleanSubtask()]);
   410	    io.__runner = io.superviseChild;
   411	    await run(['pipeline', 'run'], io);
   412	
   413	    // The merger child ran on the merger chain model and is NON-git-capable
   414	    // (read-only) — it can re-check but never merge itself (finding 4).
   415	    const mergers = childWithModel(io.__runner, 'mg-model');
   416	    assert.ok(mergers.length >= 1, 'the merger role child runs after APPROVED');
   417	    assert.equal(valAfter(argsOf(mergers[0]), '-s'), 'read-only', 'the merger child is read-only — it cannot write/merge itself');
   418	
   419	    // The REAL merge happened through mergeSubtask (the supervisor). EVERY merge
   420	    // and ff-only call carries the supervisor shape: merge at /repo, ff-only in a
   421	    // seat cwd, all under the acquired merge lock.
   422	    const m1 = io.__git.matching(/^merge baton\/wt-a\/subtask-t1$/);
   423	    assert.equal(m1.length, 1, 'the subtask-1 branch is merged exactly once');
   424	    for (const c of io.__git.matching(/^merge baton\//)) {
   425	      assert.equal(c.cwd, '/repo', 'every subtask merge runs at the repo root');
   426	      assert.equal(c.lockPresent, true, 'every subtask merge runs under the merge lock');
   427	    }
   428	    const ff = io.__git.matching(/^merge --ff-only main$/);
   429	    assert.ok(ff.length >= 2, 'both worktrees fast-forward to main after each merge');
   430	    for (const c of ff) {
   431	      assert.ok(c.cwd === WT_A || c.cwd === WT_B, 'every ff-only sync runs in a seat worktree cwd');
   432	      assert.equal(c.lockPresent, true, 'every ff-only sync runs under the merge lock');
   433	    }
   434	  });
   435	
   436	  it('reviewer BLOCKED -> the SAME writer seat re-runs with the findings in its prompt', async () => {
   437	    const io = makePipeRepo({ runner: undefined });
   438	    // subtask 1: writer APPROVED, reviewer BLOCKED (findings), then writer re-run APPROVED, reviewer APPROVED, merger APPROVED; then subtask 2 clean.
   439	    io.superviseChild = fakeRunner(io, [
   440	      { verdict: 'APPROVED' }, // writer 1
   441	      { verdict: 'BLOCKED', findings: 'REVIEW-FINDING-X' }, // reviewer 1
   442	      { verdict: 'APPROVED' }, // writer 1 re-run
   443	      { verdict: 'APPROVED' }, // reviewer 1 again
   444	      { verdict: 'APPROVED' }, // merger 1
   445	      ...cleanSubtask(), // subtask 2
   446	    ]);
   447	    io.__runner = io.superviseChild;
   448	    await run(['pipeline', 'run'], io);
   449	    // The re-run writer (2nd wa-model writer in wt-a) carries the findings.
   450	    const writers = childWithModel(io.__runner, 'wa-model').filter((c) => valAfter(argsOf(c), '-s') === 'workspace-write');
   451	    assert.ok(writers.length >= 2, 'the writer seat re-ran after the BLOCKED review');
   452	    assert.match(argsOf(writers[1]).join(' '), /REVIEW-FINDING-X/, "the re-run writer's prompt carries the review findings");
   453	  });
   454	
   455	  it('review-gate cap exhaustion -> escalated, ESCALATION.md, exit 3, no 6th spawn', async () => {
   456	    const io = makePipeRepo({ runner: undefined });
   457	    // Reviewer BLOCKS five times; the 6th writer/reviewer pair must never spawn.
   458	    io.superviseChild = fakeRunner(io, [
   459	      { verdict: 'APPROVED' }, { verdict: 'BLOCKED', findings: 'f1' },
   460	      { verdict: 'APPROVED' }, { verdict: 'BLOCKED', findings: 'f2' },
   461	      { verdict: 'APPROVED' }, { verdict: 'BLOCKED', findings: 'f3' },
   462	      { verdict: 'APPROVED' }, { verdict: 'BLOCKED', findings: 'f4' },
   463	      { verdict: 'APPROVED' }, { verdict: 'BLOCKED', findings: 'f5' },
   464	      { verdict: 'APPROVED' }, { verdict: 'BLOCKED', findings: 'f6' }, // must NEVER be consumed
   465	    ]);
   466	    io.__runner = io.superviseChild;
   467	    const code = await run(['pipeline', 'run'], io);
   468	    assert.equal(code, 3, 'a per-subtask review gate at the cap escalates');
   469	    assert.ok(loopFile(io, 'ESCALATION.md'), 'an ESCALATION.md is written');
   470	    // Exactly five writer+reviewer cycles ran — the 6th pair (and the f6 result)
   471	    // are never consumed, and the merger never runs (finding 3).
   472	    assert.equal(io.__runner.calls.length, 10, 'exactly 5 writers + 5 reviewers before escalation — no 6th spawn');
   473	    assert.equal(writersOf(io.__runner).length, 5, 'five writer attempts');
   474	    assert.equal(reviewersOf(io.__runner).length, 5, 'five review attempts');
   475	    assert.equal(childWithModel(io.__runner, 'mg-model').length, 0, 'the merger never runs for an escalated subtask');
   476	    const { state } = await loadLoopState('/repo', io);
   477	    assert.equal(state.status, LOOP_STATUS.ESCALATED, 'the escalation is persisted to loop state');
   478	  });
   479	
   480	  it('merger BLOCKED -> the writer retries (same gate-cap discipline)', async () => {
   481	    const io = makePipeRepo({ runner: undefined });
   482	    io.superviseChild = fakeRunner(io, [
   483	      { verdict: 'APPROVED' }, // writer 1
   484	      { verdict: 'APPROVED' }, // reviewer 1
   485	      { verdict: 'BLOCKED', findings: 'MERGER-FINDING-Y' }, // merger 1 rejects
   486	      { verdict: 'APPROVED' }, // writer re-run
   487	      { verdict: 'APPROVED' }, // reviewer
   488	      { verdict: 'APPROVED' }, // merger approves
   489	      ...cleanSubtask(),
   490	    ]);
   491	    io.__runner = io.superviseChild;
   492	    const code = await run(['pipeline', 'run'], io);
   493	    assert.equal(code, 0, 'the subtask recovers after a merger rejection + writer retry');
   494	    assert.ok(childWithModel(io.__runner, 'wa-model').filter((c) => valAfter(argsOf(c), '-s') === 'workspace-write').length >= 2, 'the writer retried after the merger BLOCKED');
   495	  });
   496	});

   499	describe('pipeline — merge failures park', () => {
   500	  it('a merge CONFLICT from mergeSubtask parks the run (exit 4)', async () => {
   501	    const git = pipelineGit({ over: { 'merge baton/wt-a/subtask-t1': { reject: true, code: 1, stdout: 'CONFLICT (content): Merge conflict in x' }, 'merge --abort': { stdout: '' } } });
   502	    const io = makePipeRepo({ git, runner: undefined });

   508	    const { state } = await loadLoopState('/repo', io);
   509	    assert.equal(state.status, LOOP_STATUS.PARKED);

   513	    const bad = logStdout(REC('badc0de', NAME, EMAIL, 'Rebase Bot', 'bot@ci.example', 'foreign committer'));
   514	    const git = pipelineGit({ log: bad });
   515	    const io = makePipeRepo({ git, runner: undefined });
   516	    io.superviseChild = fakeRunner(io, cleanSubtask());
   517	    io.__runner = io.superviseChild;
   518	    const code = await run(['pipeline', 'run'], io);
   519	    assert.equal(code, 4, 'a failed attribution scan parks the pipeline');
   520	    assert.equal(io.__git.matching(/^merge baton\//).length, 0, 'no subtask merge (any seat) is issued when the attribution scan fails');
   521	    const { state } = await loadLoopState('/repo', io);
   522	    assert.equal(state.status, LOOP_STATUS.PARKED);
   523	  });
   524	});

   526	// ===========================================================================
   527	describe('pipeline — worktree guard composition (preflight/postflight)', () => {
   528	  it('(a) a DIRTY writer seat -> preflight refusal BEFORE any spawn: zero children, parked, exit 4', async () => {
   529	    const git = pipelineGit({ over: { 'status --porcelain': { stdout: ' M src/x.js\n' } } });
   530	    const io = makePipeRepo({ git, runner: undefined });
   531	    io.superviseChild = fakeRunner(io, [...cleanSubtask(), ...cleanSubtask()]);
   532	    io.__runner = io.superviseChild;
   533	    const code = await run(['pipeline', 'run'], io);
   534	    assert.equal(code, 4, 'a dirty seat parks the pipeline');
   535	    assert.equal(io.__runner.calls.length, 0, 'preflight refuses BEFORE any child spawns');
   536	    assert.ok(!io.__git.issued(/^merge baton\//), 'no merge on a preflight refusal');
   537	    const { state } = await loadLoopState('/repo', io);
   538	    assert.equal(state.status, LOOP_STATUS.PARKED, 'the parked state is persisted');
   539	  });
   540	
   541	  it('(b) a WRONG-branch writer seat -> preflight refusal BEFORE any spawn: zero children, parked, exit 4', async () => {
   542	    const git = pipelineGit({ over: { 'rev-parse --abbrev-ref HEAD': { stdout: 'some-other-branch\n' } } });
   543	    const io = makePipeRepo({ git, runner: undefined });
   544	    io.superviseChild = fakeRunner(io, [...cleanSubtask(), ...cleanSubtask()]);
   545	    io.__runner = io.superviseChild;
   546	    const code = await run(['pipeline', 'run'], io);
   547	    assert.equal(code, 4, 'a wrong-branch seat parks the pipeline');
   548	    assert.equal(io.__runner.calls.length, 0, 'preflight refuses BEFORE any child spawns');
   549	    assert.ok(!io.__git.issued(/^merge baton\//), 'no merge on a preflight refusal');
   550	    const { state } = await loadLoopState('/repo', io);
   551	    assert.equal(state.status, LOOP_STATUS.PARKED);
   552	  });
   553	
   554	  it('(c) POSTFLIGHT drift (the writer child moves main) -> parked, exit 4, no reviewer/merger, no merge', async () => {
   555	    const git = pipelineGit();
   556	    const io = makePipeRepo({ git, runner: undefined });
   557	    // The writer child (first spawn) moves main between preflight-capture and postflight.
   558	    io.superviseChild = fakeRunner(io, [
   559	      { verdict: 'APPROVED', effect: () => git.moveMain('bbbbbbbbbbbbbbbbbbbbbbbbbbbbbbbbbbbbbbbb') },
   560	      ...cleanSubtask(),
   561	    ]);
   562	    io.__runner = io.superviseChild;
   563	    const code = await run(['pipeline', 'run'], io);
   564	    assert.equal(code, 4, 'postflight drift parks the pipeline');
   565	    // Exactly one child ran (the writer); postflight refuses before the reviewer.
   566	    assert.equal(io.__runner.calls.length, 1, 'only the writer ran — no reviewer/merger after postflight refusal');
   567	    assert.equal(reviewersOf(io.__runner).length, 0, 'no reviewer spawned');
   568	    assert.equal(childWithModel(io.__runner, 'mg-model').length, 0, 'no merger spawned');
   569	    assert.ok(!io.__git.issued(/^merge baton\//), 'no merge on a postflight refusal');
   570	    const { state } = await loadLoopState('/repo', io);
   571	    assert.equal(state.status, LOOP_STATUS.PARKED);
   572	  });
   573	});

   586	    assert.equal(state.status, LOOP_STATUS.DONE, 'the pipeline completed');

   593	// G1 (A1/B1/B2) — a claude worker seat's writer child carries its model + cwd.

   605	    assert.ok(claudeWriter, 'the wt-b writer ran as a claude child on the resolved model');
   606	    assert.equal(claudeWriter.spec.cwd, WT_B, 'the claude writer runs in its seat worktree (spec.cwd), not the supervisor cwd');

   611	// ===========================================================================
   612	// G2 (A2/B3) — pipeline preconditions, caps, resume, run-lock.
   613	describe('pipeline — G2: cap validation / resume / run-lock / stale branch', () => {
   614	  it('RED (G2): iterationCap 6 in loop.json is REJECTED (exit 2) BEFORE any spawn (the 5-cap is a hard invariant)', async () => {
   615	    const io = makePipeRepo({ spec: pipelineSpec({ budgets: { iterationCap: 6, perRoleTimeoutMin: 30, maxChildrenPerPhase: 10 } }) });
   616	    const code = await run(['pipeline', 'run'], io);
   617	    assert.equal(code, 2, 'a spec asking for a 6th iteration is invalid');
   618	    assert.match(io.stderrText() + io.stdoutText(), /iterationCap|cap/i, 'the error names the cap');
   619	    assert.equal(io.__runner.calls.length, 0, 'no child spawns for an invalid spec');
   620	  });
   621	
   622	  it('RED (G2): a LIVE supervisor.lock refuses pipeline run (exit 1), zero spawns', async () => {
   623	    const io = makePipeRepo();
   624	    io.processAlive = (p) => p === 55555;
   625	    const p = loopPaths('/repo');
   626	    io.fs.mkdirSync(p.dir, { recursive: true });
   627	    io.fs.writeFileSync(`${p.dir}/supervisor.lock`, JSON.stringify({ host: 'pipe-host', pid: 55555, startTime: 222, runId: 'other' }));
   628	    const code = await run(['pipeline', 'run'], io);
   629	    assert.equal(code, 1, 'a live supervisor blocks a concurrent pipeline run');
   630	    assert.match(io.stderrText() + io.stdoutText(), /55555|lock|supervisor/i, 'the refusal names the live supervisor');
   631	    assert.equal(io.__runner.calls.length, 0, 'no child spawned while another supervisor is live');
   632	  });
   633	
   634	  it('RED (G2): a stale pre-existing subtask branch PARKS (exit 4) instead of crashing', async () => {
   635	    const git = pipelineGit({ existingBranches: ['baton/wt-a/subtask-t1'] });
   636	    const io = makePipeRepo({ git, runner: undefined });
   637	    io.superviseChild = fakeRunner(io, [...cleanSubtask(), ...cleanSubtask()]);
   638	    io.__runner = io.superviseChild;
   639	    let code;
   640	    await assert.doesNotReject(async () => { code = await run(['pipeline', 'run'], io); }, 'a stale branch must not crash the supervisor');
   641	    assert.equal(code, 4, 'a stale subtask branch parks the run');
   642	    assert.equal((await loadLoopState('/repo', io)).state.status, LOOP_STATUS.PARKED);
   643	  });
   644	
   645	  it('RED (G2): a second pipeline run RESUMES loadLoopState — cap counters survive re-invocation (no refund)', async () => {
   646	    // A strict runner that NEVER falls back to default-APPROVED: it throws once
   647	    // the script is exhausted, so a re-initing (refunding) impl is caught rather
   648	    // than masked. The checkout -b branch state is neutralized by giving each run
   649	    // a fresh git (a resumed run legitimately reuses persisted worktree state).
   650	    const strictRunner = (io, script) => {
   651	      const queue = [...script];
   652	      const calls = [];
   653	      const fn = (spec, opts) => {
   654	        if (queue.length === 0) throw new Error('strictRunner: queue exhausted (no default-APPROVED fallback)');
   655	        calls.push({ spec, opts, command: spec?.command, args: spec?.args ?? [] });
   656	        const r = queue.shift();
   657	        return Promise.resolve({ timedOut: false, exitCode: 0, verdict: r.verdict, findings: r.findings ?? '', logPath: opts.logPath });
   658	      };
   659	      fn.calls = calls;
   660	      return fn;
   661	    };
   662	    const freshGit = () => { const g = pipelineGit(); g.setLockProbe(() => io.fs.existsSync(MERGE_LOCK)); return g; };
   663	
   664	    const spec = pipelineSpec({ subtasks: [{ id: 't1', title: 'only' }] });
   665	    const io = makePipeRepo({ spec, runner: undefined });
   666	
   667	    // Run 1 crashes at EXACTLY 3 persisted review iterations: writer+reviewer(BLOCKED)
   668	    // x3, then the 4th writer spawn throws (a supervisor crash mid-run).
   669	    io.execFile = freshGit();
   670	    const runner1 = strictRunner(io, [
   671	      { verdict: 'APPROVED' }, { verdict: 'BLOCKED', findings: 'x' },
   672	      { verdict: 'APPROVED' }, { verdict: 'BLOCKED', findings: 'x' },
   673	      { verdict: 'APPROVED' }, { verdict: 'BLOCKED', findings: 'x' },
   674	    ]);
   675	    io.superviseChild = runner1;
   676	    io.__runner = runner1;
   677	    let firstCode = 'ran';
   678	    try { firstCode = await run(['pipeline', 'run'], io); } catch { firstCode = 'crashed'; }
   679	    assert.equal((await loadLoopState('/repo', io)).state.iterations['subtask-t1-review'], 3, 'exactly 3 review iterations persisted before the crash');
   680	
   681	    // Run 2 resumes: two more BLOCKED reviews reach the 5-cap; the 6th escalates.
   682	    // The strict runner throws on a 5th call, so a REFUNDING (re-init) impl — which
   683	    // would need more attempts before escalating — fails to reach exit 3.
   684	    io.execFile = freshGit();
   685	    const runner2 = strictRunner(io, [
   686	      { verdict: 'APPROVED' }, { verdict: 'BLOCKED', findings: 'x' }, // iteration 4
   687	      { verdict: 'APPROVED' }, { verdict: 'BLOCKED', findings: 'x' }, // iteration 5 -> next spawn is refused by the cap
   688	    ]);
   689	    io.superviseChild = runner2;
   690	    io.__runner = runner2;
   691	    let second = 'ran';
   692	    try { second = await run(['pipeline', 'run'], io); } catch { second = 'crashed'; }
   693	    assert.equal(second, 3, 'the cap counter survived re-invocation — cumulative attempts (3+2) escalate, not refund');
   694	    assert.equal((await loadLoopState('/repo', io)).state.status, LOOP_STATUS.ESCALATED);
   695	  });
   696	});
   697	
   698	// ===========================================================================
   699	// G3 (A3/B4) — pipeline consumes writer results/classification.
   700	describe('pipeline — G3: writer result handling', () => {
   701	  it('RED (G3): a writer BLOCKED retries the WRITER without spawning a reviewer for the failed attempt', async () => {
   702	    const io = makePipeRepo({ spec: pipelineSpec({ subtasks: [{ id: 't1', title: 'only' }] }), runner: undefined });
   703	    io.superviseChild = fakeRunner(io, [
   704	      { verdict: 'BLOCKED', findings: 'writer-not-done' }, // writer 1 fails its own self-check
   705	      { verdict: 'APPROVED' }, // writer retry
   706	      { verdict: 'APPROVED' }, // reviewer
   707	      { verdict: 'APPROVED' }, // merger
   708	    ]);
   709	    io.__runner = io.superviseChild;
   710	    await run(['pipeline', 'run'], io);
   711	    // Two write-capable writer attempts ran; no reviewer ran until the writer passed.
   712	    assert.equal(writersOf(io.__runner).length, 2, 'the BLOCKED writer retried');
   713	    // The FIRST reviewer spawn must come AFTER the second (passing) writer — never
   714	    // a review of the first, failed writer attempt.
   715	    const firstReviewerIdx = io.__runner.calls.findIndex((c) => valAfter(argsOf(c), '-s') === 'read-only');
   716	    const writerIdxs = io.__runner.calls.map((c, i) => (valAfter(argsOf(c), '-s') === 'workspace-write' ? i : -1)).filter((i) => i >= 0);
   717	    assert.ok(firstReviewerIdx > writerIdxs[1], 'no reviewer spawned for the failed writer attempt (review only follows a passing writer)');
   718	  });
   719	
   720	  it('RED (G3): a writer whose LOG carries the claude session-limit banner routes to failover (not a plain review)', async () => {
   721	    const io = makePipeRepo({
   722	      spec: pipelineSpec({ subtasks: [{ id: 't1', title: 'only' }] }),
   723	      files: { '/repo/.handoff/bundle.json': JSON.stringify(ownedBundle('claude-code', 'open'), null, 2) + '\n' },
   724	      config: { ...PIPELINE_CONFIG, roles: { ...PIPELINE_CONFIG.roles, 'worker-a': ['claude-code/cc-w', 'codex/cx-w@xhigh'] } },
   725	      runner: undefined,
   726	    });
   727	    io.superviseChild = fakeRunner(io, [
   728	      { verdict: 'BLOCKED', exitCode: 1, logContent: `implementing...\n${CC_LIMIT}\n` }, // writer hits a limit
   729	      { verdict: 'APPROVED' }, // relaunched writer
   730	      { verdict: 'APPROVED' }, // reviewer
   731	      { verdict: 'APPROVED' }, // merger
   732	    ]);
   733	    io.__runner = io.superviseChild;
   734	    await run(['pipeline', 'run'], io);
   735	    // Failover was consulted: the relaunched writer ran on the OTHER platform
   736	    // (codex/cx-w — the failover-resolved model), and the failed (limit) writer
   737	    // NEVER handed off to a reviewer.
   738	    const relaunch = io.__runner.calls.find((c) => c.command === 'codex' && argsOf(c).includes('cx-w'));
   739	    assert.ok(relaunch, 'the limit death was classified and the writer relaunched on the failover model (codex/cx-w)');
   740	    const firstReviewerIdx = io.__runner.calls.findIndex((c) => valAfter(argsOf(c), '-s') === 'read-only');
   741	    const relaunchIdx = io.__runner.calls.indexOf(relaunch);
   742	    assert.ok(firstReviewerIdx === -1 || firstReviewerIdx > relaunchIdx, 'the limit-failed writer never handed off to a reviewer before the relaunch');
   743	  });
   744	
   745	  it('RED (G3): a writer that commits NOTHING (empty branch) is refused/parked — an empty branch never merges', async () => {
   746	    // The stateful git returns an EMPTY main..branch log (no commits ahead).
   747	    const git = pipelineGit({ log: '' });
   748	    const io = makePipeRepo({ spec: pipelineSpec({ subtasks: [{ id: 't1', title: 'only' }] }), git, runner: undefined });
   749	    io.superviseChild = fakeRunner(io, cleanSubtask());
   750	    io.__runner = io.superviseChild;
   751	    const code = await run(['pipeline', 'run'], io);
   752	    assert.equal(code, 4, 'an empty subtask branch parks rather than merging nothing');
   753	    assert.equal(io.__git.matching(/^merge baton\//).length, 0, 'no empty branch is merged to main');
   754	  });
   755	});
   756	
   757	// ===========================================================================
   758	// G5 (A5/B5) — reviewer-seat preflight + self-heal wiring.
   759	describe('pipeline — G5: reviewer-seat preflight + self-heal', () => {
   760	  it('RED (G5): a DIRTY reviewer seat parks BEFORE the reviewer spawns', async () => {
   761	    // subtask 1 reviewer runs from wt-b; a dirty wt-b must be caught by preflight.
   762	    const git = pipelineGit({ dirtyCwds: [WT_B] });
   763	    const io = makePipeRepo({ spec: pipelineSpec({ subtasks: [{ id: 't1', title: 'only' }] }), git, runner: undefined });
   764	    io.superviseChild = fakeRunner(io, cleanSubtask());
   765	    io.__runner = io.superviseChild;
   766	    const code = await run(['pipeline', 'run'], io);
   767	    assert.equal(code, 4, 'a dirty reviewer seat parks the run');
   768	    assert.equal(reviewersOf(io.__runner).length, 0, 'the reviewer never spawned into a dirty seat');
   769	  });
   770	
   771	  it('RED (G5): a listed-but-raw-deleted seat self-heals (prune + re-add), not a crash', async () => {
   772	    // git genuinely LISTS wt-a (explicit seed) but its dir is raw-deleted, so any
   773	    // in-cwd git in wt-a fails until self-heal prunes + re-adds it.
   774	    const git = pipelineGit();
   775	    git.seedWorktree(WT_A, 'baton/wt-a/base', { raw: true });
   776	    const io = makePipeRepo({ spec: pipelineSpec({ subtasks: [{ id: 't1', title: 'only' }] }), git, runner: undefined });
   777	    io.superviseChild = fakeRunner(io, cleanSubtask());
   778	    io.__runner = io.superviseChild;
   779	    let threw = false;
   780	    try {
   781	      await run(['pipeline', 'run'], io);
   782	    } catch {
   783	      threw = true;
   784	    }
   785	    assert.equal(threw, false, 'a raw-deleted seat must self-heal, not crash the supervisor');
   786	    assert.ok(io.__git.issued(/worktree prune/), 'the stale worktree metadata was pruned during self-heal');
   787	    assert.ok(io.__git.matching(/worktree add/).some((c) => c.argstr.includes(WT_A)), 'the raw-deleted seat was re-added');
   788	  });
   789	});
   790	
   791	// A persisted loop-state fixture. `flavor`/`specDigest` are the state-binding
   792	// stamp (H5 design): flavor ∈ {'loop','pipeline'}; specDigest = dedupeKey of the
   793	// driving spec (subtasks for pipeline, phases for loop).
   794	function seedPipelineState(io, over = {}) {
   795	  const state = {
   796	    schema: 'baton/loop-state@1',
   797	    runId: 'loop-seeded',
   798	    goal: 'Ship the pipeline',
   799	    phaseCount: 2,
   800	    phaseIndex: 0,
   801	    iterations: {},
   802	    status: 'running',
   803	    parkReason: null,
   804	    escalation: null,
   805	    smokeApproval: null,
   806	    createdAt: T0,
   807	    journalSeq: 0,
   808	    flavor: 'pipeline',
   809	    ...over,
   810	  };
   811	  io.fs.mkdirSync(loopPaths('/repo').dir, { recursive: true });
   812	  io.fs.writeFileSync(loopPaths('/repo').state, JSON.stringify(state, null, 2) + '\n');
   813	  return state;
   814	}
   815	
   816	// ===========================================================================
   817	// H1 (B1) — the WRITER prompt must carry the verdict-tail contract.
   818	describe('pipeline — H1: writer prompt carries the verdict-tail contract', () => {
   819	  it('RED (H1): the writer prompt contains "End with exactly" and "VERDICT:" (a writer with no verdict parses BLOCKED-unparseable)', async () => {
   820	    const io = makePipeRepo({ spec: pipelineSpec({ subtasks: [{ id: 't1', title: 'only' }] }), runner: undefined });
   821	    io.superviseChild = fakeRunner(io, cleanSubtask());
   822	    io.__runner = io.superviseChild;
   823	    await run(['pipeline', 'run'], io);
   824	    const writer = writersOf(io.__runner)[0];
   825	    assert.ok(writer, 'a writer child ran');
   826	    const prompt = argsOf(writer).join(' ');
   827	    assert.match(prompt, /End with exactly/, 'the writer prompt states the required tail');
   828	    assert.match(prompt, /VERDICT:/, 'the writer prompt names the VERDICT line it must emit');
   829	  });
   830	});
   831	
   832	// ===========================================================================
   833	// H2 (A1/B2) — bounded failover: avoidEntries/avoid carry; exhaustion PARKS.
   834	describe('pipeline — H2: bounded failover (avoid carries; exhaustion parks)', () => {
   835	  // A recording runner that emits a platform-appropriate death banner and THROWS
   836	  // once a bound is exceeded, so an unbounded ping-pong terminates the test RED.
   837	  function boundedDeathRunner(io, { bound, banner }) {
   838	    const calls = [];
   839	    const fn = (/** @type {any} */ spec, /** @type {any} */ opts) => {
   840	      calls.push({ spec, command: spec?.command, args: (spec?.args ?? []).map(String) });
   841	      if (calls.length > bound) throw new Error(`unbounded failover: exceeded ${bound} child spawns (ping-pong)`);
   842	      const text = typeof banner === 'function' ? banner(spec) : banner;
   843	      io.fs.mkdirSync(opts.logPath.slice(0, opts.logPath.lastIndexOf('/')), { recursive: true });
   844	      io.fs.writeFileSync(opts.logPath, `working...\n${text}\n`);
   845	      return Promise.resolve({ timedOut: false, exitCode: 1, verdict: 'BLOCKED', findings: '', logPath: opts.logPath });
   846	    };
   847	    fn.calls = calls;
   848	    return fn;
   849	  }
   850	  const modelsOf = (runner) => runner.calls.filter((c) => valAfter(c.args, '-s') === 'workspace-write').map((c) => valAfter(c.args, '--model'));
   851	
   852	  it('RED (H2a): always-model-unavailable on a two-entry codex chain never re-picks a rejected model; all rejected -> PARK (bounded)', async () => {
   853	    const config = { ...PIPELINE_CONFIG, roles: { ...PIPELINE_CONFIG.roles, 'worker-a': ['codex/wa1@xhigh', 'codex/wa2@xhigh'] } };
   854	    const io = makePipeRepo({ spec: pipelineSpec({ subtasks: [{ id: 't1', title: 'only' }] }), config, runner: undefined });
   855	    const runner = boundedDeathRunner(io, { bound: 3, banner: MODEL_UNAVAIL }); // correct impl parks at 2 writers
   856	    io.superviseChild = runner;
   857	    io.__runner = runner;
   858	    let code = 'ran';
   859	    try { code = await run(['pipeline', 'run'], io); } catch { code = 'unbounded'; }
   860	    assert.equal(code, 4, 'both models rejected -> the subtask parks (bounded, no infinite ping-pong)');
   861	    // BOTH entries are attempted, in order, exactly once — then it parks.
   862	    assert.deepEqual(modelsOf(runner), ['wa1', 'wa2'], `each chain entry is tried once, in order; got ${JSON.stringify(modelsOf(runner))}`);
   863	    assert.ok(runner.calls.length <= 3, `the runner stayed within the throwing bound (no ping-pong); calls=${runner.calls.length}`);
   864	  });
   865	
   866	  it('RED (H2b): always-usage-limit across a cross-platform chain parks within a bounded child budget (no infinite loop)', async () => {
   867	    const config = { ...PIPELINE_CONFIG, roles: { ...PIPELINE_CONFIG.roles, 'worker-a': ['claude-code/wa-cc', 'codex/wa-cx@xhigh'] } };
   868	    const io = makePipeRepo({
   869	      spec: pipelineSpec({ subtasks: [{ id: 't1', title: 'only' }] }),
   870	      config,
   871	      files: { '/repo/.handoff/bundle.json': JSON.stringify(ownedBundle('claude-code', 'open'), null, 2) + '\n' },
   872	      runner: undefined,
   873	    });
   874	    const runner = boundedDeathRunner(io, { bound: 4, banner: (spec) => (spec.command === 'claude' ? CC_LIMIT : CODEX_LIMIT) });
   875	    io.superviseChild = runner;
   876	    io.__runner = runner;
   877	    let code = 'ran';
   878	    try { code = await run(['pipeline', 'run'], io); } catch { code = 'unbounded'; }
   879	    assert.equal(code, 4, 'a repeatedly-limit-dying subtask parks within the child budget, never loops forever');
   880	  });
   881	});
   882	
   883	// ===========================================================================
   884	// H5 (B4) — state flavor + spec binding across loop/pipeline.
   885	describe('pipeline — H5: state flavor + spec-digest binding', () => {
   886	  it('RED (H5a): a state.json created by `loop run` (flavor loop) is REFUSED by pipeline run (exit 2 naming the mismatch), zero spawns', async () => {
   887	    const io = makePipeRepo({ spec: pipelineSpec({ subtasks: [{ id: 't1', title: 'only' }] }), runner: undefined });
   888	    io.superviseChild = fakeRunner(io, cleanSubtask());
   889	    io.__runner = io.superviseChild;
   890	    seedPipelineState(io, { flavor: 'loop', phaseCount: 1 });
   891	    const code = await run(['pipeline', 'run'], io);
   892	    assert.equal(code, 2, 'a loop-flavored state cannot be resumed as a pipeline');
   893	    assert.match(io.stderrText() + io.stdoutText(), /flavor|loop|mismatch/i, 'the refusal names the flavor mismatch');
   894	    assert.equal(io.__runner.calls.length, 0, 'no child spawned on a flavor mismatch');
   895	  });
   896	
   897	  it('RED (H5b): a changed subtasks list (stale specDigest) on resume REFUSES (exit 2), rather than misaligning phaseIndex', async () => {
   898	    const io = makePipeRepo({ spec: pipelineSpec({ subtasks: [{ id: 't1', title: 'a' }, { id: 't2', title: 'b' }] }), runner: undefined });
   899	    io.superviseChild = fakeRunner(io, [...cleanSubtask(), ...cleanSubtask()]);
   900	    io.__runner = io.superviseChild;
   901	    // A prior run over a DIFFERENT subtask list: its specDigest no longer matches
   902	    // the current subtasks, so resuming its phaseIndex would misalign.
   903	    const oldSubtasks = [{ id: 't1', title: 'a' }, { id: 'REMOVED', title: 'gone' }, { id: 't2', title: 'b' }];
   904	    seedPipelineState(io, { flavor: 'pipeline', specDigest: dedupeKey(oldSubtasks), phaseIndex: 1 });
   905	    const code = await run(['pipeline', 'run'], io);
   906	    assert.equal(code, 2, 'a resume against a changed subtask list refuses rather than resuming a misaligned position');
   907	    assert.match(io.stderrText() + io.stdoutText(), /subtask|spec|changed|digest/i, 'the refusal names the spec change');
   908	    assert.equal(io.__runner.calls.length, 0, 'no child spawned on a spec-digest mismatch');
   909	  });
   910	});
   911	
   912	// ===========================================================================
   913	// I1 (A1/B1) — auto-advance requires a supervisor-owned MERGE RECEIPT, not just
   914	// is-ancestor: a stale empty branch at main's tip is is-ancestor-true too, and
   915	// must PARK, not silently skip the subtask.
   916	//
   917	// Merge-receipt shape (I1 design): append-only `.handoff/loop/merges.ndjson`,
   918	// one line per merged subtask `{ subtaskId, branch, mergedAt }`. Auto-advance on
   919	// resume requires is-ancestor(branch, main) AND a receipt for that subtask.
   920	describe('pipeline — I1: merge-receipt-gated auto-advance', () => {
   921	  const MERGES = `${loopPaths('/repo').dir}/merges.ndjson`;
   922	  const seedReceipt = (io, subtaskId, branch) => {
   923	    io.fs.mkdirSync(loopPaths('/repo').dir, { recursive: true });
   924	    io.fs.writeFileSync(MERGES, JSON.stringify({ subtaskId, branch, mergedAt: T0 }) + '\n');
   925	  };
   926	  const receipts = (io) => {
   927	    const raw = io.files()[MERGES];
   928	    return typeof raw === 'string' ? raw.split('\n').filter((l) => l.trim() !== '').map((l) => JSON.parse(l)) : [];
   929	  };
   930	
   931	  it('RED (I1b): a receipt-backed already-merged subtask ADVANCES on resume (no writer, no empty-branch park)', async () => {
   932	    const subtasks = [{ id: 't1', title: 'first' }, { id: 't2', title: 'second' }];
   933	    // t1 is genuinely merged: is-ancestor true, empty main..t1, AND a receipt.
   934	    const git = pipelineGit({ mergedBranches: ['baton/wt-a/subtask-t1'] });
   935	    const io = makePipeRepo({ spec: pipelineSpec({ subtasks }), git, runner: undefined });
   936	    io.superviseChild = fakeRunner(io, cleanSubtask()); // enough for t2 only
   937	    io.__runner = io.superviseChild;
   938	    seedPipelineState(io, { flavor: 'pipeline', specDigest: dedupeKey(subtasks), phaseIndex: 0, phaseCount: 2 });
   939	    seedReceipt(io, 't1', 'baton/wt-a/subtask-t1');
   940	
   941	    const code = await run(['pipeline', 'run'], io);
   942	    assert.equal(code, 0, `a receipt-backed merged subtask advances and the run completes; stderr: ${io.stderrText()}`);
   943	    assert.doesNotMatch(io.stderrText() + io.stdoutText(), /EMPTY branch|nothing to review or merge|stale/i, 'a receipt-backed subtask is not mistaken for empty/stale');
   944	    const writers = writersOf(io.__runner);
   945	    assert.ok(writers.length >= 1, 'the unmerged subtask still runs a writer');
   946	    assert.equal(valAfter(argsOf(writers[0]), '-C'), WT_B, 'the first writer is t2 (wt-b) — t1 was recognized as merged (receipt) and skipped');
   947	  });
   948	
   949	  it('RED (I1a): a stale EMPTY branch at main tip (is-ancestor true, NO receipt) PARKS as stale — never auto-advances', async () => {
   950	    const subtasks = [{ id: 't1', title: 'first' }, { id: 't2', title: 'second' }];
   951	    // t1's branch is empty at main's tip: is-ancestor true, empty main..t1 — but
   952	    // NO merge receipt (the writer crashed before its first commit).
   953	    const git = pipelineGit({ mergedBranches: ['baton/wt-a/subtask-t1'] });
   954	    const io = makePipeRepo({ spec: pipelineSpec({ subtasks }), git, runner: undefined });
   955	    io.superviseChild = fakeRunner(io, [...cleanSubtask(), ...cleanSubtask()]);
   956	    io.__runner = io.superviseChild;
   957	    seedPipelineState(io, { flavor: 'pipeline', specDigest: dedupeKey(subtasks), phaseIndex: 0, phaseCount: 2 });
   958	    // No receipt seeded.
   959	
   960	    const code = await run(['pipeline', 'run'], io);
   961	    assert.equal(code, 4, 'a receipt-less is-ancestor branch parks as stale rather than silently skipping the subtask');
   962	    const out = io.stderrText() + io.stdoutText();
   963	    assert.match(out, /stale/i, 'the park names the stale branch');
   964	    assert.equal(io.__git.matching(/^merge baton\//).length, 0, 'nothing is merged for a stale empty branch');
   965	    // J2: the message must hedge BOTH causes — each pinned SEPARATELY so a
   966	    // single-cause misdiagnosis cannot pass — point at the range to inspect, and
   967	    // account for the branch still being checked out in the writer seat (a bare
   968	    // "delete the branch" fails while it is checked out).
   969	    assert.match(out, /crashed before committing|before.*first commit|pre-?commit/i, 'names the pre-commit-crash cause');
   970	    assert.match(out, /merged without a receipt|merge.*receipt.*missing|not.*recorded/i, 'ALSO names the merged-without-receipt cause (no single-cause misdiagnosis)');
   971	    assert.match(out, /git log main\.\.baton\/wt-a\/subtask-t1/, 'points at `git log main..<branch>` to disambiguate');
   972	    assert.match(out, /seat|worktree|checked out|checkout/i, 'remediation accounts for the branch still being checked out in the writer seat');
   973	  });
   974	
   975	  it('GUARD (I1 conjunction): a receipt WITHOUT is-ancestor does NOT auto-advance — the subtask is re-run, never silently skipped', async () => {
   976	    const subtasks = [{ id: 't1', title: 'first' }, { id: 't2', title: 'second' }];
   977	    // A receipt exists for t1, but the branch is NOT an ancestor of main (the
   978	    // merge did not actually land — e.g. main was rewound). A receipt-ONLY
   979	    // advance would skip t1; the required conjunction re-runs it.
   980	    const git = pipelineGit({ mergedBranches: [] }); // is-ancestor FALSE for every branch
   981	    const io = makePipeRepo({ spec: pipelineSpec({ subtasks }), git, runner: undefined });
   982	    io.superviseChild = fakeRunner(io, [...cleanSubtask(), ...cleanSubtask()]);
   983	    io.__runner = io.superviseChild;
   984	    seedPipelineState(io, { flavor: 'pipeline', specDigest: dedupeKey(subtasks), phaseIndex: 0, phaseCount: 2 });
   985	    seedReceipt(io, 't1', 'baton/wt-a/subtask-t1'); // receipt present, ancestor absent
   986	
   987	    const code = await run(['pipeline', 'run'], io);
   988	    assert.equal(code, 0, `the run completes by re-running t1; stderr: ${io.stderrText()}`);
   989	    const writers = writersOf(io.__runner);
   990	    assert.ok(writers.length >= 1, 'a writer ran');
   991	    assert.equal(valAfter(argsOf(writers[0]), '-C'), WT_A, 'the FIRST writer is t1 (wt-a) — a receipt alone did NOT skip it (is-ancestor is a required conjunct)');
   992	  });
   993	
   994	  it('RED (I1c): a clean merge PERSISTS a durable receipt {subtaskId, branch, mergedAt} BEFORE the phase-advance is journaled', async () => {
   995	    const io = makePipeRepo({ spec: pipelineSpec({ subtasks: [{ id: 't1', title: 'only' }] }), runner: undefined });
   996	    io.superviseChild = fakeRunner(io, cleanSubtask());
   997	    io.__runner = io.superviseChild;
   998	
   999	    // Ordering hook: at each phase-advance journal write, capture whether the t1
  1000	    // receipt is ALREADY on disk (durable before the advance, not merely by EOR).
  1001	    const journalPath = `${loopPaths('/repo').dir}/journal.ndjson`;
  1002	    const receiptAtAdvance = [];
  1003	    const realAppend = io.fs.appendFileSync.bind(io.fs);
  1004	    io.fs.appendFileSync = (/** @type {any} */ path, /** @type {any} */ data) => {
  1005	      if (String(path) === journalPath && /"type":"phase-advance"/.test(String(data))) {
  1006	        receiptAtAdvance.push(receipts(io).some((r) => r.subtaskId === 't1'));
  1007	      }
  1008	      return realAppend(path, data);
  1009	    };
  1010	
  1011	    const code = await run(['pipeline', 'run'], io);
  1012	    assert.equal(code, 0);
  1013	    const rec = receipts(io).find((r) => r.subtaskId === 't1');
  1014	    assert.ok(rec, 'a merge receipt is persisted after the subtask merges');
  1015	    assert.equal(rec.branch, 'baton/wt-a/subtask-t1', 'the receipt names the merged branch');
  1016	    assert.ok(typeof rec.mergedAt === 'string' && rec.mergedAt.length > 0, 'the receipt records mergedAt');
  1017	    assert.ok(receiptAtAdvance.length >= 1 && receiptAtAdvance.every(Boolean), 'the receipt is durable on disk BEFORE the phase advance is journaled (crash-safe)');
  1018	  });
  1019	});
  1020	
  1021	// ===========================================================================
  1022	// I3 (A2/B4) — an EXISTING unstamped state (no flavor/specDigest) is refused by
  1023	// pipeline run, with the archive instruction, zero spawns.
  1024	describe('pipeline — I3: unstamped legacy state is refused', () => {
  1025	  it('RED (I3): an unstamped state.json (no flavor/specDigest) refuses exit 2 with the archive instruction, zero spawns', async () => {
  1026	    const io = makePipeRepo({ spec: pipelineSpec({ subtasks: [{ id: 't1', title: 'a' }, { id: 't2', title: 'b' }] }), runner: undefined });
  1027	    io.superviseChild = fakeRunner(io, [...cleanSubtask(), ...cleanSubtask()]);
  1028	    io.__runner = io.superviseChild;
  1029	    io.fs.mkdirSync(loopPaths('/repo').dir, { recursive: true });
  1030	    io.fs.writeFileSync(
  1031	      loopPaths('/repo').state,
  1032	      JSON.stringify({
  1033	        schema: 'baton/loop-state@1', runId: 'legacy', goal: 'Ship the pipeline', phaseCount: 2, phaseIndex: 1,
  1034	        iterations: {}, status: 'running', parkReason: null, escalation: null, smokeApproval: null, createdAt: T0, journalSeq: 0,
  1035	        // NO flavor, NO specDigest.
  1036	      }, null, 2) + '\n',
  1037	    );
  1038	    const code = await run(['pipeline', 'run'], io);
  1039	    assert.equal(code, 2, 'an unstamped state is refused, not resumed');
  1040	    assert.match(io.stderrText() + io.stdoutText(), /archive/i, 'the refusal tells the operator to archive the state');
  1041	    assert.equal(io.__runner.calls.length, 0, 'no child spawned on an unstamped-state refusal');
  1042	  });
  1043	});
  1044	
  1045	// ===========================================================================
  1046	// J1 (B1) — `baton pipeline resume` mirrors G6's loop resume: PARKED -> running
  1047	// (cap counters intact), ESCALATED refused, flavor-guarded.
  1048	describe('pipeline — J1: pipeline resume', () => {
  1049	  const gate = 'subtask-t1-review';
  1050	
  1051	  it('RED (J1a): resume transitions a PARKED run to running — the same subtask re-runs its writer, gate counters intact (no refund)', async () => {
  1052	    const subtasks = [{ id: 't1', title: 'only' }];
  1053	    const io = makePipeRepo({ spec: pipelineSpec({ subtasks }), runner: undefined });
  1054	    io.superviseChild = fakeRunner(io, cleanSubtask());
  1055	    io.__runner = io.superviseChild;
  1056	    // A parked run mid-subtask t1 with the gate already iterated 3 times.
  1057	    seedPipelineState(io, {
  1058	      flavor: 'pipeline', specDigest: dedupeKey(subtasks), phaseIndex: 0, phaseCount: 1,
  1059	      status: 'parked', parkReason: 'operator paused', iterations: { [gate]: 3 },
  1060	    });
  1061	
  1062	    const code = await run(['pipeline', 'resume'], io);
  1063	    assert.notEqual(code, 2, 'resume is a recognized subcommand (not a usage error)');
  1064	    const writers = writersOf(io.__runner);
  1065	    assert.ok(writers.length >= 1, 'resume re-runs the parked subtask — its writer spawns again');
  1066	    assert.equal(valAfter(argsOf(writers[0]), '-C'), WT_A, 'the re-run writer is t1 in wt-a (not skipped)');
  1067	    assert.ok((await loadLoopState('/repo', io)).state.iterations[gate] >= 3, 'the gate cap counter survives resume — no refund');
  1068	    // J1a: resume must pin SUCCESSFUL continuation of the clean case, not merely
  1069	    // "not-unknown + writer respawns". The clean subtask completes: exit 0, the
  1070	    // run reaches DONE, and the journal records a RESUME event BEFORE completion.
  1071	    assert.equal(code, 0, `resume completes the clean subtask; stderr: ${io.stderrText()}`);
  1072	    assert.equal((await loadLoopState('/repo', io)).state.status, LOOP_STATUS.DONE, 'the resumed pipeline reaches DONE');
  1073	    const journal = (io.files()[loopPaths('/repo').journal] ?? '')
  1074	      .split('\n').filter((l) => l.trim() !== '').map((l) => JSON.parse(l));
  1075	    const resumeIdx = journal.findIndex((e) => e.type === LOOP_EVENT.RESUME);
  1076	    assert.ok(resumeIdx >= 0, 'the resume is journaled (a RESUME event is appended)');
  1077	    const advanceIdx = journal.findIndex((e) => e.type === LOOP_EVENT.PHASE_ADVANCE);
  1078	    assert.ok(advanceIdx > resumeIdx, 'the RESUME event precedes the phase-advance that completes the run');
  1079	  });
  1080	
  1081	  it('RED (J1b): resume refuses an ESCALATED run (exit 3, operator-only), zero spawns', async () => {
  1082	    const subtasks = [{ id: 't1', title: 'only' }];
  1083	    const io = makePipeRepo({ spec: pipelineSpec({ subtasks }), runner: undefined });
  1084	    io.superviseChild = fakeRunner(io, cleanSubtask());
  1085	    io.__runner = io.superviseChild;
  1086	    seedPipelineState(io, {
  1087	      flavor: 'pipeline', specDigest: dedupeKey(subtasks), phaseIndex: 0, phaseCount: 1,
  1088	      status: 'escalated', escalation: { gate, iteration: 5 },
  1089	    });
  1090	    const code = await run(['pipeline', 'resume'], io);
  1091	    assert.equal(code, 3, 'an escalated run cannot be resumed (operator-only)');
  1092	    assert.equal(io.__runner.calls.length, 0, 'resume never spawns on an escalated run');
  1093	    assert.equal((await loadLoopState('/repo', io)).state.status, LOOP_STATUS.ESCALATED, 'the run stays escalated');
  1094	  });
  1095	
  1096	  it('RED (J1c): pipeline resume refuses a loop-flavored state (exit 2, flavor guard), zero spawns', async () => {
  1097	    const subtasks = [{ id: 't1', title: 'only' }];
  1098	    const io = makePipeRepo({ spec: pipelineSpec({ subtasks }), runner: undefined });
  1099	    io.superviseChild = fakeRunner(io, cleanSubtask());
  1100	    io.__runner = io.superviseChild;
  1101	    seedPipelineState(io, { flavor: 'loop', specDigest: dedupeKey(subtasks), phaseIndex: 0, phaseCount: 1, status: 'parked', parkReason: 'x' });
  1102	    const code = await run(['pipeline', 'resume'], io);
  1103	    assert.equal(code, 2, 'a loop-flavored state cannot be resumed by pipeline');
  1104	    assert.match(io.stderrText() + io.stdoutText(), /flavor|loop|mismatch/i, 'the refusal names the flavor mismatch');
  1105	    assert.equal(io.__runner.calls.length, 0, 'no child spawned on a flavor mismatch');
  1106	  });
  1107	
  1108	  it('GREEN (J1d companion): `baton loop resume` still refuses a pipeline-flavored state (exit 2), zero spawns — the boundary holds both ways', async () => {
  1109	    // A loop repo (loop.json with phases) whose state was stamped by a pipeline run.
  1110	    const loopJson = { schema: 'baton/loop@1', goal: 'boundary', constraints: [], budgets: { iterationCap: 5, perRoleTimeoutMin: 30, maxChildrenPerPhase: 10 }, phases: [{ id: 'plan', role: 'planner' }, { id: 'gate-1', role: 'plan-reviewer' }] };
  1111	    const io = makePipeRepo({ spec: loopJson, runner: undefined });
  1112	    io.superviseChild = fakeRunner(io, cleanSubtask());
  1113	    io.__runner = io.superviseChild;
  1114	    io.fs.mkdirSync(loopPaths('/repo').dir, { recursive: true });
  1115	    io.fs.writeFileSync(
  1116	      loopPaths('/repo').state,
  1117	      JSON.stringify({
  1118	        schema: 'baton/loop-state@1', runId: 'x', goal: 'boundary', phaseCount: 2, phaseIndex: 0,
  1119	        iterations: {}, status: 'parked', parkReason: 'x', escalation: null, smokeApproval: null, createdAt: T0, journalSeq: 0,
  1120	        flavor: 'pipeline', specDigest: dedupeKey(loopJson.phases),
  1121	      }, null, 2) + '\n',
  1122	    );
  1123	    const code = await run(['loop', 'resume'], io);
  1124	    assert.equal(code, 2, 'loop resume refuses a pipeline-flavored state');
  1125	    assert.match(io.stderrText() + io.stdoutText(), /flavor|pipeline|mismatch/i, 'the refusal names the flavor mismatch');
  1126	    assert.equal(io.__runner.calls.length, 0, 'no child spawned across the flavor boundary');
  1127	  });
  1128	
  1129	  it('RED (J1e): resume with NOTHING to resume REFUSES (exit 2) and never initializes a fresh run', async () => {
  1130	    // A repo with a spec (loop.json) but NO persisted .handoff/loop/state.json —
  1131	    // e.g. `resume` run in the wrong directory. Resuming must refuse, NOT quietly
  1132	    // init a new state and launch a full pipeline from scratch.
  1133	    const io = makePipeRepo({ spec: pipelineSpec({ subtasks: [{ id: 't1', title: 'only' }] }), runner: undefined });
  1134	    io.superviseChild = fakeRunner(io, cleanSubtask());
  1135	    io.__runner = io.superviseChild;
  1136	    assert.equal(io.fs.existsSync(loopPaths('/repo').state), false, 'precondition: no persisted run state exists');
  1137	
  1138	    const code = await run(['pipeline', 'resume'], io);
  1139	    assert.equal(code, 2, 'nothing to resume is a usage error, not a silent fresh run');
  1140	    const out = io.stderrText() + io.stdoutText();
  1141	    assert.match(out, /nothing to resume/i, 'the refusal states there is nothing to resume');
  1142	    assert.match(out, /baton pipeline run/, 'the refusal points at `baton pipeline run` to start a fresh run');
  1143	    assert.equal(io.__runner.calls.length, 0, 'a nothing-to-resume refusal spawns no children');
  1144	    // The teeth: a wrong-directory resume must not initialize and start over.
  1145	    assert.equal(io.fs.existsSync(loopPaths('/repo').state), false, 'refusing to resume never creates a state.json');
  1146	  });
  1147	});
  1148	
  1149	function loopFile(io, name) {
  1150	  return io.files()[`${loopPaths('/repo').dir}/${name}`];
  1151	}

    27	// today's generic exit 2. --detach is OUT of unit scope (deferred to e2e).

    94	/**
    95	 * Scripted fake child runner honoring the injection seam. Each call shifts the
    96	 * next result off the queue, writes its logContent to opts.logPath (so the
    97	 * command reads the transcript from the child's LOG file), and records the
    98	 * spawn spec.
    99	 */
   100	function fakeRunner(io, script) {
   101	  const queue = [...script];
   102	  const calls = /** @type {any[]} */ ([]);
   103	  const fn = (/** @type {any} */ spec, /** @type {any} */ opts = {}) => {
   104	    calls.push({ spec, opts, args: spec?.args ?? [], command: spec?.command, env: spec?.env });
   105	    const r = queue.shift() ?? { timedOut: false, exitCode: 0, verdict: 'APPROVED', findings: '' };
   106	    // No fallback: the command MUST pass a real logPath (asserted below).
   107	    const logPath = opts.logPath;
   108	    if (typeof r.logContent === 'string') {
   109	      const dir = logPath.slice(0, logPath.lastIndexOf('/'));
   110	      io.fs.mkdirSync(dir, { recursive: true });
   111	      io.fs.writeFileSync(logPath, r.logContent);
   112	    }
   113	    return Promise.resolve({ timedOut: r.timedOut ?? false, exitCode: r.exitCode ?? 0, verdict: r.verdict ?? 'APPROVED', findings: r.findings ?? '', logPath });
   114	  };
   115	  fn.calls = calls;
   116	  return fn;
   117	}

   119	function makeLoopRepo({ spec = loopSpec(), files = {}, runner, execFile, env = {} } = {}) {
   120	  const io = makeIo({
   121	    now: T0,
   122	    host: 'loop-host',
   123	    pid: 4242,
   124	    startTime: 111000,
   125	    fencingSeed: 1,
   126	    env,
   127	    files: {
   128	      [SIG_ABS]: SIG_CONTENT,
   129	      '/repo/baton.config.json': JSON.stringify(CONFIG, null, 2),
   130	      ...(spec ? { '/repo/loop.json': JSON.stringify(spec, null, 2) + '\n' } : {}),
   131	      ...files,
   132	    },
   133	  });
   134	  if (execFile) io.execFile = execFile;
   135	  const fake = runner ?? fakeRunner(io, []);
   136	  io.superviseChild = fake;
   137	  io.__runner = fake;
   138	  return io;
   139	}

   270	describe('loop run — smoke gate', () => {
   271	  const smokeSpec = loopSpec({
   272	    smoke: { cmd: 'npm run smoke', expect: 'ok' },
   273	    phases: [
   274	      { id: 'smoke-tests', role: 'test-author' },
   275	      { id: 'smoke-verify', role: 'test-verifier' },

   277	      { id: 'subtask-implement', role: 'implementer' },
