   280	
   281	  function smokeExecFile(rec) {
   282	    return (/** @type {string} */ cmd, /** @type {string[]} */ args = []) => {
   283	      rec.push([cmd, ...args].join(' '));
   284	      // git stays unavailable; the smoke command "passes".
   285	      if (cmd === 'git') return Promise.reject(Object.assign(new Error('spawn ENOENT'), { code: 'ENOENT' }));
   286	      return Promise.resolve({ stdout: 'ok\n', stderr: '' });
   287	    };
   288	  }
   289	
   290	  it('after smoke-build: runs smoke.cmd, writes SMOKE-REVIEW.md + a smoke-approval token, awaits, exit 0, no further child', async () => {
   291	    const rec = [];
   292	    const io = makeLoopRepo({ spec: smokeSpec, execFile: smokeExecFile(rec), runner: undefined });
   293	    io.superviseChild = fakeRunner(io, [{ verdict: 'APPROVED' }, { verdict: 'APPROVED' }, { verdict: 'APPROVED' }]);
   294	    io.__runner = io.superviseChild;
   295	
   296	    const code = await cmdLoop(['run'], io);
   297	    assert.equal(code, 0, 'pausing for smoke approval is a clean exit 0');
   298	    assert.equal(io.__runner.calls.length, 3, 'the smoke slice ran (3 phases); the post-smoke phase did NOT spawn');
   299	    assert.ok(rec.some((c) => /smoke/.test(c)), 'smoke.cmd was executed via io.execFile');
   300	
   301	    assert.ok(loopFile(io, 'SMOKE-REVIEW.md'), 'SMOKE-REVIEW.md was written');
   302	    const approval = loopFile(io, 'smoke-approval.json');
   303	    assert.ok(approval, 'a smoke-approval record was written');
   304	    const token = JSON.parse(approval).token;
   305	    assert.ok(typeof token === 'string' && token.startsWith('smk1.'), 'the approval carries a smokeApprovalToken over the six inputs');
   306	
   307	    const { state } = await loadLoopState('/repo', io);
   308	    assert.equal(state.status, LOOP_STATUS.AWAITING_SMOKE_APPROVAL, 'the run is awaiting smoke approval');
   309	  });
   310	
   311	  it('--approve-smoke <correct token> resumes into the next phase (a child spawns again)', async () => {
   312	    const rec = [];
   313	    const io = makeLoopRepo({ spec: smokeSpec, execFile: smokeExecFile(rec), runner: undefined });
   314	    io.superviseChild = fakeRunner(io, [{ verdict: 'APPROVED' }, { verdict: 'APPROVED' }, { verdict: 'APPROVED' }]);
   315	    io.__runner = io.superviseChild;
   316	    assert.equal(await cmdLoop(['run'], io), 0);
   317	    const token = JSON.parse(loopFile(io, 'smoke-approval.json')).token;
   318	
   319	    // Resume with the correct token — a FRESH runner for the continued phase.
   320	    const runner2 = fakeRunner(io, [{ verdict: 'APPROVED' }]);
   321	    io.superviseChild = runner2;
   322	    io.__runner = runner2;
   323	    const code = await cmdLoop(['run', '--approve-smoke', token], io);
   324	    assert.equal(code, 0);
   325	    assert.equal(runner2.calls.length, 1, 'a verified approval continues into the post-smoke phase');
   326	  });
   327	
   328	  it('--approve-smoke with the REAL issued token after a bound input drifted -> refused: exit 4, PARKED, zero spawns', async () => {
   329	    // A git-aware execFile whose HEAD is a mutable ref — HEAD is a bound token
   330	    // input, so moving it between issuance and approval is genuine drift.
   331	    let head = 'HEAD-A';
   332	    const gitAware = (/** @type {string} */ cmd, /** @type {string[]} */ args = []) => {
   333	      if (cmd === 'git') {
   334	        const key = args.join(' ');
   335	        const out = key === 'rev-parse HEAD' ? head : key === 'rev-parse --abbrev-ref HEAD' ? 'main' : '';
   336	        return Promise.resolve({ stdout: out + '\n', stderr: '' });
   337	      }
   338	      return Promise.resolve({ stdout: 'ok\n', stderr: '' }); // the smoke command
   339	    };
   340	    const io = makeLoopRepo({ spec: smokeSpec, execFile: gitAware, runner: undefined });
   341	    io.superviseChild = fakeRunner(io, [{ verdict: 'APPROVED' }, { verdict: 'APPROVED' }, { verdict: 'APPROVED' }]);
   342	    io.__runner = io.superviseChild;
   343	    assert.equal(await cmdLoop(['run'], io), 0);
   344	    const token = JSON.parse(loopFile(io, 'smoke-approval.json')).token; // the ACTUAL issued smk1 token
   345	
   346	    // Drift a bound input: HEAD moves after the human looked.
   347	    head = 'HEAD-B';
   348	    const runner2 = fakeRunner(io, [{ verdict: 'APPROVED' }]);
   349	    io.superviseChild = runner2;
   350	    io.__runner = runner2;
   351	    const code = await cmdLoop(['run', '--approve-smoke', token], io); // the OLD token, now stale
   352	    assert.equal(code, 4, 'the plan parks on smoke-token drift');
   353	    assert.equal(runner2.calls.length, 0, 'a drifted approval spawns no child');
   354	    const { state } = await loadLoopState('/repo', io);
   355	    assert.equal(state.status, LOOP_STATUS.PARKED, 'drift parks the run (not left silently awaiting)');
   356	  });
   357	});
   358	
   359	// ===========================================================================
   360	describe('loop run — failover integration', () => {
   361	  it('a usage-limit child (limit banner in its LOG) relaunches on the NEW platform (next child argv)', async () => {
   362	    // implementer chain: [claude-code, codex, cursor]; the claude-code child hits
   363	    // a session limit -> failover avoids claude-code -> relaunch on codex.
   364	    const spec = loopSpec({ phases: [{ id: 'subtask-implement', role: 'implementer' }] });
   365	    const io = makeLoopRepo({ spec, files: { '/repo/.handoff/bundle.json': JSON.stringify(ownedBundle('claude-code', 'open'), null, 2) + '\n' }, runner: undefined });
   366	    io.superviseChild = fakeRunner(io, [
   367	      { verdict: 'BLOCKED', exitCode: 1, logContent: `child transcript...\n${CC_LIMIT}\n` }, // the limit lives in the LOG
   368	      { verdict: 'APPROVED' }, // the relaunched child succeeds
   369	    ]);
   370	    io.__runner = io.superviseChild;
   371	
   372	    const code = await cmdLoop(['run'], io);
   373	    assert.equal(io.__runner.calls.length, 2, 'the failover relaunched exactly one follow-up child');
   374	    assert.equal(io.__runner.calls[0].command, 'claude', 'the first child ran on the claude-code chain head');
   375	    assert.equal(io.__runner.calls[1].command, 'codex', 'the relaunch ran on the failover target platform (codex)');
   376	    assert.equal(valAfter(argsOf(io, 1), '--model'), 'gpt-5.6-sol', 'the relaunch used the resolved next-entry model');
   377	    assert.equal(code, 0, 'the relaunched child completed the phase');
   378	  });
   379	
   380	  it('a failover PARK decision stops the run parked (single-entry CODEX role, model-unavailable)', async () => {
   381	    // The model-unavailable signature is CODEX-only, and the classifier filters
   382	    // by the child's platform — so the dead child must run on codex. A
   383	    // single-entry codex chain exhausts after its dead model -> runFailover parks.
   384	    const cfg = JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(CONFIG));
   385	    cfg.roles['solo-codex'] = ['codex/gpt-5.6-sol@xhigh'];
   386	    const spec = loopSpec({ phases: [{ id: 'work', role: 'solo-codex' }] });
   387	    const io = makeLoopRepo({
   388	      spec,
   389	      files: {
   390	        '/repo/baton.config.json': JSON.stringify(cfg, null, 2),
   391	        '/repo/.handoff/bundle.json': JSON.stringify(ownedBundle('codex', 'open'), null, 2) + '\n',
   392	      },
   393	      runner: undefined,
   394	    });
   395	    io.superviseChild = fakeRunner(io, [{ verdict: 'BLOCKED', exitCode: 1, logContent: `child said: ${MODEL_UNAVAIL}\n` }]);
   396	    io.__runner = io.superviseChild;
   397	
   398	    const code = await cmdLoop(['run'], io);
   399	    assert.equal(io.__runner.calls.length, 1, 'no relaunch when failover parks');
   400	    assert.equal(io.__runner.calls[0].command, 'codex', 'the dead child genuinely ran on codex (so the codex-only signature applies)');
   401	    assert.equal(code, 4, 'a parked run exits 4 (defined nonzero)');
   402	    const { state } = await loadLoopState('/repo', io);
   403	    assert.equal(state.status, LOOP_STATUS.PARKED, 'the run is parked');
   404	  });
   405	});
   406	
   407	// ===========================================================================
   408	describe('loop run — recovery from a mid-run crash', () => {
   409	  it('resumes from the journal position: only the REMAINING phase spawns a child', async () => {
   410	    const spec = loopSpec(); // plan -> gate-1
   411	    const io = makeLoopRepo({ spec, runner: undefined });
   412	    // Seed a mid-run state: plan already completed (phaseIndex 1), gate-1 pending,
   413	    // plus a matching journal and a DEAD supervisor lock.
   414	    const p = loopPaths('/repo');
   415	    io.fs.mkdirSync(p.dir, { recursive: true });
   416	    const midState = {
   417	      schema: 'baton/loop-state@1',
   418	      runId: 'loop-prior',
   419	      goal: 'Ship the loop',
   420	      phaseCount: 2,
   421	      phaseIndex: 1,
   422	      iterations: { 'gate-1': 0 },
   423	      status: LOOP_STATUS.RUNNING,
   424	      parkReason: null,
   425	      escalation: null,
   426	      smokeApproval: null,
   427	      createdAt: T0,
   428	      journalSeq: 1,
   429	      // I3 reconciliation: stamped so the resume is not refused as unstamped.
   430	      flavor: 'loop',
   431	      specDigest: dedupeKey(spec.phases),
   432	    };
   433	    io.fs.writeFileSync(p.state, JSON.stringify(midState, null, 2) + '\n');
   434	    io.fs.writeFileSync(p.journal, JSON.stringify({ seq: 1, ts: T0, type: 'phase-advance' }) + '\n');
   435	    io.fs.writeFileSync(`${p.dir}/supervisor.lock`, JSON.stringify({ host: 'loop-host', pid: 999999, startTime: 7, runId: 'loop-prior' }));
   436	
   437	    io.superviseChild = fakeRunner(io, [{ verdict: 'APPROVED' }]); // only gate-1 remains
   438	    io.__runner = io.superviseChild;
   439	
   440	    const code = await cmdLoop(['run'], io);
   441	    assert.equal(io.__runner.calls.length, 1, 'the completed plan phase is NOT re-run — only gate-1 spawns');
   442	    assert.equal(io.__runner.calls[0].command, 'codex', 'the resumed child is the gate-1 reviewer (codex)');
   443	    assert.equal(code, 0, 'resuming the remaining phase completes the run');
   444	    const { state } = await loadLoopState('/repo', io);
   445	    assert.equal(state.status, LOOP_STATUS.DONE);
   446	  });
   447	});

   481	// ===========================================================================
   482	// B8 (G2) — the run-lock acquisition must be ATOMIC, not check-then-write.
   483	describe('loop run — atomic run-lock acquisition (B8)', () => {
   484	  const DIR = `${loopPaths('/repo').dir}`;
   485	  const LOCK = `${DIR}/supervisor.lock`;
   486	
   487	  it('RED (B8): implementation-neutral — the competitor materializes AT the exclusive-create; wx/mkdir-first refuses, check-then-write clobbers (RED)', async () => {
   488	    const io = makeLoopRepo({ runner: undefined });
   489	    io.superviseChild = fakeRunner(io, [{ verdict: 'APPROVED' }, { verdict: 'APPROVED' }]);
   490	    io.__runner = io.superviseChild;
   491	    io.processAlive = (p) => p === 55555; // the competitor supervisor is LIVE
   492	
   493	    const realWrite = io.fs.writeFileSync.bind(io.fs);
   494	    const realMkdir = io.fs.mkdirSync.bind(io.fs);
   495	    const realRename = io.fs.renameSync.bind(io.fs);
   496	    const COMPETITOR = JSON.stringify({ host: 'loop-host', pid: 55555, startTime: 222, runId: 'competitor' });
   497	
   498	    // The competitor wins the lock the INSTANT before the supervisor's own write
   499	    // to LOCK lands — injected at whichever primitive the acquire uses to create
   500	    // it. NO readFileSync hook, so a wx/mkdir-FIRST acquire (which never reads
   501	    // before creating) cannot dodge the race.
   502	    let injected = false;
   503	    const injectBefore = (/** @type {any} */ target) => {
   504	      if (injected || String(target) !== LOCK) return;
   505	      injected = true;
   506	      realMkdir(DIR, { recursive: true });
   507	      realWrite(LOCK, COMPETITOR);
   508	    };
   509	
   510	    // Exclusive create (flag wx/ax) enforces the O_EXCL semantics memfs lacks:
   511	    // once the competitor exists, an exclusive write throws EEXIST.
   512	    io.fs.writeFileSync = (/** @type {any} */ p, /** @type {any} */ data, /** @type {any} */ opts) => {
   513	      const flag = typeof opts === 'string' ? undefined : opts?.flag;
   514	      const exclusive = flag === 'wx' || flag === 'ax' || flag === 'wx+' || flag === 'ax+';
   515	      injectBefore(p);
   516	      if (exclusive && String(p) === LOCK && io.fs.existsSync(p)) throw Object.assign(new Error(`EEXIST: file already exists, open '${p}'`), { code: 'EEXIST' });
   517	      return realWrite(p, data, opts); // a PLAIN write to LOCK clobbers the competitor
   518	    };
   519	    // mkdirSync(LOCK) as an exclusive lock dir: memfs already throws EEXIST when a
   520	    // node exists at the path (the injected competitor file).
   521	    io.fs.mkdirSync = (/** @type {any} */ p, /** @type {any} */ opts) => { injectBefore(p); return realMkdir(p, opts); };
   522	    // A tmp+rename publish (atomicWriteJson) clobbers the competitor on rename.
   523	    io.fs.renameSync = (/** @type {any} */ from, /** @type {any} */ to) => { injectBefore(to); return realRename(from, to); };
   524	
   525	    const code = await cmdLoop(['run'], io);
   526	    assert.equal(code, 1, 'a wx/mkdir-first exclusive acquire observes EEXIST and refuses the run');
   527	    assert.equal(io.__runner.calls.length, 0, 'no child spawned when the lock was lost to the competitor');
   528	    assert.match(JSON.parse(io.files()[LOCK]).runId, /competitor/, "the competitor's lock is not clobbered (a check-then-write path fails here)");
   529	  });
   530	});
   531	
   532	// ===========================================================================
   533	// G4 (A4) — record in-flight child groups; kill recorded orphans on reclaim.
   534	describe('loop run — child-group recording + kill-on-reclaim (G4)', () => {
   535	  const DIR = `${loopPaths('/repo').dir}`;
   536	
   537	  const readChildren = (io) => {
   538	    const raw = io.files()[`${DIR}/children.ndjson`];
   539	    if (typeof raw !== 'string') return [];
   540	    return raw.split('\n').filter((l) => l.trim() !== '').map((l) => JSON.parse(l));
   541	  };
   542	
   543	  it('RED (G4): a child-start record {childId, pid, pgid, startedAt} exists on disk WHILE the child is in flight (before completion)', async () => {
   544	    const io = makeLoopRepo({ runner: undefined });
   545	    let n = 0;
   546	    const inflightOk = [];
   547	    // The runner reports the child's group via opts.onStart (spawn time); the
   548	    // supervisor must persist the record synchronously in that handler — BEFORE
   549	    // the child completes — so a mid-run crash leaves the orphan reap-able.
   550	    io.superviseChild = (/** @type {any} */ _spec, /** @type {any} */ opts) => {
   551	      n += 1;
   552	      const info = { pid: 8000 + n, pgid: 9000 + n };
   553	      if (typeof opts.onStart === 'function') opts.onStart(info);
   554	      const rec = readChildren(io).find((r) => r.pgid === info.pgid);
   555	      inflightOk.push(
   556	        !!rec && typeof rec.childId === 'string' && rec.childId.length > 0 && rec.pid === info.pid && typeof rec.startedAt === 'string' && rec.startedAt.length > 0,
   557	      );
   558	      return Promise.resolve({ timedOut: false, exitCode: 0, verdict: 'APPROVED', findings: '', logPath: opts.logPath, ...info });
   559	    };
   560	    const code = await cmdLoop(['run'], io);
   561	    assert.equal(code, 0);
   562	    assert.ok(inflightOk.length >= 1, 'at least one child ran');
   563	    assert.ok(inflightOk.every(Boolean), 'each child-start record ({childId,pid,pgid,startedAt}) existed on disk while its child was in flight');
   564	  });
   565	
   566	  it('RED (G5/G4): on dead-lock reclaim, a still-alive recorded orphan group is KILLED (io.processKill) BEFORE the first new spawn', async () => {
   567	    const io = makeLoopRepo({ runner: undefined });
   568	    const events = []; // ordered: kills and spawns interleaved
   569	    io.processKill = (/** @type {number} */ pid, /** @type {any} */ sig) => { events.push({ kind: 'kill', pid, sig }); return true; };
   570	    const baseRunner = fakeRunner(io, [{ verdict: 'APPROVED' }, { verdict: 'APPROVED' }]);
   571	    io.superviseChild = (/** @type {any} */ spec, /** @type {any} */ opts) => { events.push({ kind: 'spawn' }); return baseRunner(spec, opts); };
   572	    io.__runner = baseRunner;
   573	    // The dead supervisor's orphan group (pgid 9001) is still alive; its lock is dead.
   574	    io.processAlive = (/** @type {number} */ p) => p === 9001;
   575	    io.fs.mkdirSync(DIR, { recursive: true });
   576	    io.fs.writeFileSync(`${DIR}/supervisor.lock`, JSON.stringify({ host: 'loop-host', pid: 999999, startTime: 7, runId: 'crashed' }));
   577	    io.fs.writeFileSync(`${DIR}/children.ndjson`, JSON.stringify({ childId: '001-plan', pid: 8001, pgid: 9001, startedAt: T0 }) + '\n');
   578	
   579	    const code = await cmdLoop(['run'], io);
   580	    assert.equal(code, 0, `the reclaiming run completes after reaping orphans; stderr: ${io.stderrText()}`);
   581	    const firstSpawn = events.findIndex((e) => e.kind === 'spawn');
   582	    const killIdx = events.findIndex((e) => e.kind === 'kill' && Math.abs(e.pid) === 9001);
   583	    assert.ok(killIdx >= 0, `the orphan group 9001 was killed on reclaim; events: ${JSON.stringify(events)}`);
   584	    assert.ok(firstSpawn === -1 || killIdx < firstSpawn, 'the orphan group was killed BEFORE the first new child spawned');
   585	  });
   586	});
   587	
   588	// ===========================================================================
   589	// G6 (B6) — `baton loop resume`: PARKED -> running; ESCALATED refused.
   590	describe('loop run — resume (G6)', () => {
   591	  const DIR = `${loopPaths('/repo').dir}`;
   592	  const seedState = (over) => ({
   593	    schema: 'baton/loop-state@1',
   594	    runId: 'loop-r',
   595	    goal: 'Ship the loop',
   596	    phaseCount: 2,
   597	    phaseIndex: 0,
   598	    iterations: {},
   599	    status: 'running',
   600	    parkReason: null,
   601	    escalation: null,
   602	    smokeApproval: null,
   603	    createdAt: T0,
   604	    journalSeq: 0,
   605	    // I3 reconciliation: stamped so a valid resume is not refused as unstamped.
   606	    flavor: 'loop',
   607	    specDigest: dedupeKey(loopSpec().phases),
   608	    ...over,
   609	  });
   610	
   611	  it('RED (G6): `baton loop resume` transitions a PARKED run back to running and continues (spawns the pending phase)', async () => {
   612	    const io = makeLoopRepo({ runner: undefined });
   613	    io.superviseChild = fakeRunner(io, [{ verdict: 'APPROVED' }, { verdict: 'APPROVED' }]);
   614	    io.__runner = io.superviseChild;
   615	    io.fs.mkdirSync(DIR, { recursive: true });
   616	    io.fs.writeFileSync(`${DIR}/state.json`, JSON.stringify(seedState({ status: 'parked', parkReason: 'operator paused' }), null, 2) + '\n');
   617	
   618	    const code = await cmdLoop(['resume'], io);
   619	    assert.equal(code, 0, `resume continues a parked run; stderr: ${io.stderrText()}`);
   620	    assert.ok(io.__runner.calls.length >= 1, 'resume spawned the pending phase');
   621	    assert.equal((await loadLoopState('/repo', io)).state.status, LOOP_STATUS.DONE, 'the resumed run completes');
   622	  });
   623	
   624	  it('RED (G6): resume on an ESCALATED run is REFUSED (escalation stays operator-only, no spawn)', async () => {
   625	    const io = makeLoopRepo({ runner: undefined });
   626	    io.superviseChild = fakeRunner(io, [{ verdict: 'APPROVED' }]);
   627	    io.__runner = io.superviseChild;
   628	    io.fs.mkdirSync(DIR, { recursive: true });
   629	    io.fs.writeFileSync(`${DIR}/state.json`, JSON.stringify(seedState({ status: 'escalated', escalation: { gate: 'gate-1', iteration: 5 } }), null, 2) + '\n');
   630	
   631	    const code = await cmdLoop(['resume'], io);
   632	    assert.notEqual(code, 0, 'an escalated run cannot be resumed');
   633	    assert.match(io.stderrText() + io.stdoutText(), /escalat|operator/i, 'the refusal explains escalation is operator-only');
   634	    assert.equal(io.__runner.calls.length, 0, 'resume never spawns on an escalated run');
   635	    assert.equal((await loadLoopState('/repo', io)).state.status, LOOP_STATUS.ESCALATED, 'the run stays escalated');
   636	  });
   637	
   638	  it('RED (G6): resume with NOTHING to resume REFUSES (exit 2) and never initializes a fresh run', async () => {
   639	    // A loop repo with loop.json but NO persisted state.json — e.g. `resume` run
   640	    // in the wrong directory. It must refuse, not init a fresh run from phase 0.
   641	    const io = makeLoopRepo({ runner: undefined });
   642	    io.superviseChild = fakeRunner(io, [{ verdict: 'APPROVED' }, { verdict: 'APPROVED' }]);
   643	    io.__runner = io.superviseChild;
   644	    assert.equal(io.fs.existsSync(`${DIR}/state.json`), false, 'precondition: no persisted run state exists');
   645	
   646	    const code = await cmdLoop(['resume'], io);
   647	    assert.equal(code, 2, 'nothing to resume is a usage error, not a silent fresh run');
   648	    const out = io.stderrText() + io.stdoutText();
   649	    assert.match(out, /nothing to resume/i, 'the refusal states there is nothing to resume');
   650	    assert.match(out, /baton loop run/, 'the refusal points at `baton loop run` to start a fresh run');
   651	    assert.equal(io.__runner.calls.length, 0, 'a nothing-to-resume refusal spawns no children');
   652	    // The teeth: a wrong-directory resume must not initialize and start over.
   653	    assert.equal(io.fs.existsSync(`${DIR}/state.json`), false, 'refusing to resume never creates a state.json');
   654	    assert.ok(!io.fs.existsSync(`${DIR}/supervisor.lock`), 'the nothing-to-resume refusal leaves no supervisor.lock behind (I4)');
   655	  });
   656	
   657	  it('RED (G6): `baton loop run` on a PARKED run refuses (exit 4) AND points at `resume with: baton loop resume` (symmetry with pipeline)', async () => {
   658	    const io = makeLoopRepo({ runner: undefined });
   659	    io.superviseChild = fakeRunner(io, [{ verdict: 'APPROVED' }, { verdict: 'APPROVED' }]);
   660	    io.__runner = io.superviseChild;
   661	    io.fs.mkdirSync(DIR, { recursive: true });
   662	    io.fs.writeFileSync(`${DIR}/state.json`, JSON.stringify(seedState({ status: 'parked', parkReason: 'operator paused' }), null, 2) + '\n');
   663	
   664	    const code = await cmdLoop(['run'], io);
   665	    assert.equal(code, 4, 'a plain `run` on a parked state refuses with the parked exit code');
   666	    const out = io.stderrText() + io.stdoutText();
   667	    assert.match(out, /parked/i, 'the refusal says the run is parked');
   668	    assert.match(out, /resume with: baton loop resume/, 'the refusal points at the resume command (pipeline already carries this pointer — pin the symmetry)');
   669	    assert.equal(io.__runner.calls.length, 0, 'a `run` on a parked state spawns nothing');
   670	    assert.equal((await loadLoopState('/repo', io)).state.status, LOOP_STATUS.PARKED, 'the run stays parked (run never resumes it)');
   671	  });
   672	});
   673	
   674	// ===========================================================================
   675	// H3 (A2/B5) — children.ndjson RETIRES completed children; reclaim kills only
   676	// still-in-flight groups (never an OS-recycled pid of a retired child).
   677	describe('loop run — child registry retirement (H3)', () => {
   678	  const DIR = `${loopPaths('/repo').dir}`;
   679	  const readChildren = (io) => {
   680	    const raw = io.files()[`${DIR}/children.ndjson`];
   681	    if (typeof raw !== 'string') return [];
   682	    return raw.split('\n').filter((l) => l.trim() !== '').map((l) => JSON.parse(l));
   683	  };
   684	
   685	  it('RED (H3a): after a clean run, EVERY started child has a matching retire record ({childId, endedAt})', async () => {
   686	    const io = makeLoopRepo({ runner: undefined });
   687	    // A runner that reports its group (so the supervisor writes the START record
   688	    // via its onStart handler); the RETIRE record is the supervisor's job on resolve.
   689	    let n = 0;
   690	    const notRetiredAtStart = [];
   691	    io.superviseChild = (/** @type {any} */ _spec, /** @type {any} */ opts) => {
   692	      n += 1;
   693	      const pgid = 9000 + n;
   694	      if (typeof opts.onStart === 'function') opts.onStart({ pid: 8000 + n, pgid });
   695	      // Ordering: the child is IN FLIGHT here — it must have a start record and
   696	      // NO retire yet (retirement happens only on resolution).
   697	      const start = readChildren(io).find((r) => r.pgid === pgid);
   698	      const retiredEarly = readChildren(io).some((r) => start && r.childId === start.childId && typeof r.endedAt === 'string');
   699	      notRetiredAtStart.push(!!start && !retiredEarly);
   700	      return Promise.resolve({ timedOut: false, exitCode: 0, verdict: 'APPROVED', findings: '', logPath: opts.logPath, pgid });
   701	    };
   702	    io.__runner = { calls: [] };
   703	    const code = await cmdLoop(['run'], io);
   704	    assert.ok(notRetiredAtStart.length >= 1 && notRetiredAtStart.every(Boolean), 'a just-started child has a start record and NO endedAt while in flight');
   705	    assert.equal(code, 0);
   706	    const recs = readChildren(io);
   707	    const started = recs.filter((r) => typeof r.pgid === 'number').map((r) => r.childId);
   708	    const retired = recs.filter((r) => typeof r.endedAt === 'string').map((r) => r.childId);
   709	    assert.ok(started.length >= 1, 'at least one child started');
   710	    for (const id of started) assert.ok(retired.includes(id), `child ${id} was retired (a {childId, endedAt} record exists)`);
   711	  });
   712	
   713	  it('RED (H3b): a dead-lock reclaim KILLS NOTHING for a RETIRED child, even when processAlive says its (recycled) pgid is alive', async () => {
   714	    const io = makeLoopRepo({ runner: undefined });
   715	    io.superviseChild = fakeRunner(io, [{ verdict: 'APPROVED' }, { verdict: 'APPROVED' }]);
   716	    io.__runner = io.superviseChild;
   717	    const killCalls = [];
   718	    io.processKill = (/** @type {number} */ pid, /** @type {any} */ sig) => { killCalls.push({ pid, sig }); return true; };
   719	    // The prior run's child is RETIRED, but its pgid was recycled by an unrelated live process.
   720	    io.processAlive = (/** @type {number} */ p) => p === 9001;
   721	    io.fs.mkdirSync(DIR, { recursive: true });
   722	    io.fs.writeFileSync(`${DIR}/supervisor.lock`, JSON.stringify({ host: 'loop-host', pid: 999999, startTime: 7, runId: 'crashed' }));
   723	    io.fs.writeFileSync(
   724	      `${DIR}/children.ndjson`,
   725	      JSON.stringify({ childId: '001-plan', pid: 8001, pgid: 9001, startedAt: T0 }) + '\n' + JSON.stringify({ childId: '001-plan', endedAt: T0 }) + '\n',
   726	    );
   727	
   728	    const code = await cmdLoop(['run'], io);
   729	    assert.equal(code, 0, `the reclaiming run completes; stderr: ${io.stderrText()}`);
   730	    assert.ok(!killCalls.some((k) => Math.abs(k.pid) === 9001), `a retired child is never killed (pid-reuse safety); kills: ${JSON.stringify(killCalls)}`);
   731	  });
   732	});
   733	
   734	// ===========================================================================
   735	// H5 (B4) vice-versa — a pipeline-flavored state is refused by `loop run`.
   736	describe('loop run — state flavor binding (H5)', () => {
   737	  const DIR = `${loopPaths('/repo').dir}`;

   813	  const DIR = `${loopPaths('/repo').dir}`;

   815	    const io = makeLoopRepo({ runner: undefined });

   818	    io.superviseChild = (/** @type {any} */ spec, /** @type {any} */ opts) => {
   819	      if (lockRunIdAtSpawn === null) {
   820	        const raw = io.files()[`${DIR}/supervisor.lock`];
   821	        lockRunIdAtSpawn = raw ? JSON.parse(raw).runId : 'no-lock';

   823	      return base(spec, opts);
   824	    };

   832	
   833	// ===========================================================================
   834	// I2 (B2) — retire records must not mask a SAME-childId in-flight child across
   835	// invocations; the registry is truncated on each successful acquisition.
   836	describe('loop run — retire records never mask a resumed in-flight child (I2)', () => {
   837	  const DIR = `${loopPaths('/repo').dir}`;
   838	
   839	  it('RED (I2a): run 1 retired childId X; run 2 has a FRESH in-flight X (no retire) + dead lock -> reclaim MUST kill X', async () => {
   840	    const io = makeLoopRepo({ runner: undefined });
   841	    io.superviseChild = fakeRunner(io, [{ verdict: 'APPROVED' }, { verdict: 'APPROVED' }]);
   842	    io.__runner = io.superviseChild;
   843	    const killCalls = [];
   844	    io.processKill = (/** @type {number} */ pid, /** @type {any} */ sig) => { killCalls.push({ pid, sig }); return true; };
   845	    // The resumed run's in-flight child (pgid 9002) is alive; run 1's retired X is not.
   846	    io.processAlive = (/** @type {number} */ p) => p === 9002;
   847	    io.fs.mkdirSync(DIR, { recursive: true });
   848	    io.fs.writeFileSync(`${DIR}/supervisor.lock`, JSON.stringify({ host: 'loop-host', pid: 999999, startTime: 7, runId: 'crashed' }));
   849	    io.fs.writeFileSync(
   850	      `${DIR}/children.ndjson`,
   851	      // Run 1: childId '001-plan' started (pgid 9001) then retired.
   852	      JSON.stringify({ childId: '001-plan', pid: 8001, pgid: 9001, startedAt: T0 }) + '\n' +
   853	        JSON.stringify({ childId: '001-plan', endedAt: T0 }) + '\n' +
   854	        // Run 2 (the crashed resume): the SAME childId '001-plan' started again (pgid 9002), never retired.
   855	        JSON.stringify({ childId: '001-plan', pid: 8002, pgid: 9002, startedAt: T0 }) + '\n',
   856	    );
   857	
   858	    const code = await cmdLoop(['run'], io);
   859	    assert.equal(code, 0, `the reclaiming run completes; stderr: ${io.stderrText()}`);
   860	    assert.ok(killCalls.some((k) => Math.abs(k.pid) === 9002), `the resumed in-flight X (9002) is killed despite an earlier retire of the same childId; kills: ${JSON.stringify(killCalls)}`);
   861	  });
   862	
   863	  it('RED (I2b): a NORMAL (non-reclaim) acquisition truncates prior-invocation records — a stale retire cannot linger to mask a future child', async () => {
   864	    const io = makeLoopRepo({ runner: undefined });
   865	    io.superviseChild = fakeRunner(io, [{ verdict: 'APPROVED' }, { verdict: 'APPROVED' }]);
   866	    io.__runner = io.superviseChild;
   867	    // A prior invocation's stale records (a distinctive pgid 4242000) are present;
   868	    // no lock, so this is a clean acquisition.
   869	    io.fs.mkdirSync(DIR, { recursive: true });
   870	    io.fs.writeFileSync(
   871	      `${DIR}/children.ndjson`,
   872	      JSON.stringify({ childId: '001-plan', pid: 4242000, pgid: 4242000, startedAt: T0 }) + '\n' + JSON.stringify({ childId: '001-plan', endedAt: T0 }) + '\n',
   873	    );
   874	    const code = await cmdLoop(['run'], io);
   875	    assert.equal(code, 0);
   876	    const raw = io.files()[`${DIR}/children.ndjson`] ?? '';
   877	    assert.doesNotMatch(raw, /4242000/, "a prior invocation's records are truncated at acquisition — they cannot mask a current child");
   878	  });
   879	});
   880	
   881	// ===========================================================================
   882	// I3 (A2/B4) — an EXISTING unstamped state (no flavor/specDigest) is refused,
   883	// with the archive instruction; I4 — the refusal leaves no lock behind.
   884	describe('loop run — unstamped legacy state is refused (I3 + I4)', () => {
   885	  const DIR = `${loopPaths('/repo').dir}`;
   886	  it('RED (I3/I4): an unstamped state.json (no flavor/specDigest) refuses exit 2 with the archive instruction, zero spawns, no leaked lock', async () => {
   887	    const io = makeLoopRepo({ runner: undefined });
   888	    io.superviseChild = fakeRunner(io, [{ verdict: 'APPROVED' }, { verdict: 'APPROVED' }]);
   889	    io.__runner = io.superviseChild;
   890	    io.fs.mkdirSync(DIR, { recursive: true });
   891	    io.fs.writeFileSync(

   893	      JSON.stringify({
   894	        schema: 'baton/loop-state@1', runId: 'legacy', goal: 'Ship the loop', phaseCount: 2, phaseIndex: 1,
   895	        iterations: {}, status: 'running', parkReason: null, escalation: null, smokeApproval: null, createdAt: T0, journalSeq: 0,
   896	        // NO flavor, NO specDigest — a pre-stamp/stripped legacy state.
   897	      }, null, 2) + '\n',
   898	    );
   899	    const code = await cmdLoop(['run'], io);
   900	    assert.equal(code, 2, 'an unstamped state is a mismatch — refused, not grandfathered');
   901	    assert.match(io.stderrText() + io.stdoutText(), /archive/i, 'the refusal tells the operator to archive the state');
   902	    assert.equal(io.__runner.calls.length, 0, 'no child spawned on an unstamped-state refusal');
   903	    assert.ok(!io.fs.existsSync(`${DIR}/supervisor.lock`), 'the refusal leaves no supervisor.lock behind (I4)');
   904	  });
   905	});
   906	
   907	// ===========================================================================
   908	// I5 (B5) — the run lock passes startTime to processAlive: a recycled pid
   909	// (alive by pid alone but failing the pid+startTime pair) is reclaimed, not
   910	// refused as live.
   911	describe('loop run — lock reclaim uses pid+startTime (I5)', () => {
   912	  const DIR = `${loopPaths('/repo').dir}`;
   913	  it('RED (I5): a lock whose pid is alive by a 1-arg check but fails the (pid, startTime) pair is treated as DEAD and reclaimed', async () => {
   914	    const io = makeLoopRepo({ runner: undefined });
   915	    io.superviseChild = fakeRunner(io, [{ verdict: 'APPROVED' }, { verdict: 'APPROVED' }]);
   916	    io.__runner = io.superviseChild;
   917	    // pid 7777 reads as alive for ANY defined startTime EXCEPT the recorded 999
   918	    // (which is the reused-pid case → dead). A 1-arg check, or a wrong-but-defined
   919	    // second arg, would read live and wedge the lock. Only passing the RECORDED
   920	    // startTime (999) reclaims — so the fake is inverted vs. a "match one value"
   921	    // trap, and the calls are recorded to prove the pair is passed through.
   922	    const aliveCalls = [];
   923	    io.processAlive = (/** @type {number} */ pid, /** @type {number | undefined} */ startTime) => {
   924	      aliveCalls.push({ pid, startTime });
   925	      if (pid !== 7777) return false;
   926	      return startTime !== 999; // dead ONLY for the recorded (pid, startTime) pair
   927	    };
   928	    io.fs.mkdirSync(DIR, { recursive: true });
   929	    io.fs.writeFileSync(`${DIR}/supervisor.lock`, JSON.stringify({ host: 'loop-host', pid: 7777, startTime: 999, runId: 'recycled' }));
   930	
   931	    const code = await cmdLoop(['run'], io);
   932	    assert.equal(code, 0, `a reused-pid lock is reclaimed (not refused as live); stderr: ${io.stderrText()}`);
   933	    assert.ok(io.__runner.calls.length >= 1, 'the run proceeds after reclaiming the reused-pid lock');
   934	    assert.ok(
   935	      aliveCalls.some((c) => c.pid === 7777 && c.startTime === 999),
   936	      `the reclaim passes the RECORDED (pid, startTime) pair to processAlive; calls: ${JSON.stringify(aliveCalls)}`,
   937	    );
   938	  });
   939	});

    95	 * Scripted fake child runner honoring the injection seam. Each call shifts the
    96	 * next result off the queue, writes its logContent to opts.logPath (so the
    97	 * command reads the transcript from the child's LOG file), and records the
    98	 * spawn spec.
    99	 */
   100	function fakeRunner(io, script) {
   101	  const queue = [...script];
   102	  const calls = /** @type {any[]} */ ([]);
   103	  const fn = (/** @type {any} */ spec, /** @type {any} */ opts = {}) => {
   104	    calls.push({ spec, opts, args: spec?.args ?? [], command: spec?.command, env: spec?.env });
   105	    const r = queue.shift() ?? { timedOut: false, exitCode: 0, verdict: 'APPROVED', findings: '' };
   106	    // No fallback: the command MUST pass a real logPath (asserted below).
   107	    const logPath = opts.logPath;
   108	    if (typeof r.logContent === 'string') {
   109	      const dir = logPath.slice(0, logPath.lastIndexOf('/'));
   110	      io.fs.mkdirSync(dir, { recursive: true });
   111	      io.fs.writeFileSync(logPath, r.logContent);
   112	    }
   113	    return Promise.resolve({ timedOut: r.timedOut ?? false, exitCode: r.exitCode ?? 0, verdict: r.verdict ?? 'APPROVED', findings: r.findings ?? '', logPath });
   114	  };
   115	  fn.calls = calls;
   116	  return fn;
   117	}
